local blacklistedUsers = {}
local detectedLogs = {}

function loadBlacklist()
    pcall(function()
        if type(isfolder) == "function" and isfolder("chanhyuk lua/blacklist") then
            local files = listfiles("chanhyuk lua/blacklist")
            for _, file in ipairs(files) do
                if file:sub(-4) == ".txt" then
                    local content = readfile(file)
                    local userIdStr = file:match("ID_(%d+)%.txt")
                    if userIdStr then
                        local uid = tonumber(userIdStr)
                        if uid then
                            blacklistedUsers[uid] = content
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
loadBlacklist()

local CONFIG_FOLDER = "chanhyuk lua/configs"
local CONFIG_SKIP = {
    DragConnections = true,
    CurrentTab = true,
    BlacklistInputText = true,
}

function ensureConfigFolder()
    pcall(function()
        if not isfolder then return end
        if not isfolder("chanhyuk lua") then makefolder("chanhyuk lua") end
        if not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) end
    end)
end

function serializeState()
    local out = {}
    for k, v in pairs(State) do
        if not CONFIG_SKIP[k] then
            local t = type(v)
            if t == "boolean" or t == "number" or t == "string" then
                out[k] = v
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end

function applyLoadedConfig(data)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
    for k, v in pairs(data) do
        if State[k] ~= nil and not CONFIG_SKIP[k] then
            local t = type(State[k])
            if t == "boolean" then
                State[k] = (v == true or v == "true" or v == 1)
            elseif t == "number" then
                State[k] = tonumber(v) or State[k]
            elseif t == "string" then
                State[k] = tostring(v)
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        if type(applyBloom) == "function" then applyBloom() end
        if type(applyFogSettings) == "function" then applyFogSettings() end
        if type(applySnowSettings) == "function" then applySnowSettings() end
        if type(applyFeatherSettings) == "function" then applyFeatherSettings() end
        if type(UISystem) == "table" and UISystem.UpdateVisuals then UISystem.UpdateVisuals() end
    end)
    return true
end

function saveConfig(name)
    name = tostring(name or "default"):gsub("[^%w_%-]", "")
    if name == "" then name = "default" end
    ensureConfigFolder()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local data = serializeState()
        local json = HttpService:JSONEncode(data)
        writefile(CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json", json)
    end)
    if ok then
        print("[chanhyuk] Config saved:", name)
        return true
    end
    warn("[chanhyuk] Config save failed:", err)
    return false
end

function loadConfig(name)
    name = tostring(name or "default"):gsub("[^%w_%-]", "")
    if name == "" then name = "default" end
    local path = CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        if not isfile or not isfile(path) then
            error("file not found: " .. path)
        end
        local raw = readfile(path)
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
    end)
    if ok and applyLoadedConfig(result) then
        print("[chanhyuk] Config loaded:", name)
        return true
    end
    warn("[chanhyuk] Config load failed:", result)
    return false
end

function listConfigs()
    local list = {}
    pcall(function()
        if not isfolder or not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then return end
        for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(CONFIG_FOLDER)) do
            local n = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if n then table.insert(list, n) end
        end
    end)
    return list
end

local killSoundTracked = {}
function playKillSound()
    if not State.KillSound then return end
    local id = tostring(State.KillSoundId or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
    if id == "" or id == "0" then return end
    pcall(function()
        local s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
        s.Volume = tonumber(State.KillSoundVol) or 1
        s.PlayOnRemove = false
        s.Parent = Workspace.CurrentCamera or Workspace
        s:Play()
        task.delay(8, function()
            pcall(function() s:Destroy() end)
        end)
    end)
end

function playHitSound()
    if not State.HitSound then return end
    local id = tostring(State.HitSoundId or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
    if id == "" or id == "0" then return end
    pcall(function()
        local s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
        s.Volume = tonumber(State.HitSoundVol) or 1
        s.PlayOnRemove = false
        s.Parent = Workspace.CurrentCamera or Workspace
        s:Play()
        task.delay(6, function()
            pcall(function() s:Destroy() end)
        end)
    end)
end

local hitNoticeGui, hitNoticeLabel = nil, nil
local hitNoticeToken = 0
function ensureHitNoticeGui()
    if hitNoticeGui and hitNoticeGui.Parent then return end
    pcall(function()
        local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        if not pg then return end
        local old = pg:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukHitNotice")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local g = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        g.Name = "ChanhyukHitNotice"
        g.ResetOnSpawn = false
        g.DisplayOrder = 200
        g.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        g.Parent = pg
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Name = "Notice"
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 56)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0.32, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = ""
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 90)
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        lbl.TextSize = 28
        lbl.Visible = false
        lbl.Parent = g
        hitNoticeGui = g
        hitNoticeLabel = lbl
    end)
end

function showHitNotice(custom)
    if not State.HitNotice then return end
    ensureHitNoticeGui()
    if not hitNoticeLabel then return end
    hitNoticeToken = hitNoticeToken + 1
    local token = hitNoticeToken
    local text = custom or State.HitNoticeText or "HIT"
    local hex = tostring(State.HitNoticeColor or "FF465A"):gsub("#", "")
    local col = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 90)
    pcall(function()
        if #hex >= 6 then
            col = Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(hex:sub(1,2),16) or 255, tonumber(hex:sub(3,4),16) or 70, tonumber(hex:sub(5,6),16) or 90)
        end
    end)
    hitNoticeLabel.Text = text
    hitNoticeLabel.TextSize = tonumber(State.HitNoticeSize) or 28
    hitNoticeLabel.TextColor3 = col
    hitNoticeLabel.Visible = true
    hitNoticeLabel.TextTransparency = 0
    hitNoticeLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
    task.spawn(function()
        local dur = tonumber(State.HitNoticeDuration) or 1.2
        task.wait(dur * 0.55)
        if token ~= hitNoticeToken then return end
        for i = 1, 10 do
            if token ~= hitNoticeToken then return end
            hitNoticeLabel.TextTransparency = i / 10
            hitNoticeLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35 + (i / 10) * 0.65
            task.wait(dur * 0.45 / 10)
        end
        if token == hitNoticeToken then
            hitNoticeLabel.Visible = false
        end
    end)
end

function onLocalHitFeedback()
    playHitSound()
    showHitNotice()
end
function hookKillSound(plr)
    if not plr or plr == LocalPlayer then return end
    if killSoundTracked[plr] then return end
    local function onChar(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not hum then return end
        local lastHp = hum.Health
        hum.Died:Connect(function()
            playKillSound()
            showHitNotice("KILL")
        end)
        hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(h)
            if h < lastHp and h > 0 then

                onLocalHitFeedback()
            end
            if h <= 0 then
                playKillSound()
                showHitNotice("KILL")
            end
            lastHp = h
        end)
    end
    if plr.Character then onChar(plr.Character) end
    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(onChar)
    killSoundTracked[plr] = true
end
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    hookKillSound(plr)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookKillSound)

function showTopWarning(text)
    pcall(function()
        if not topWarningFrame or not topWarningLabel then return end
        topWarningLabel.Text = tostring(text)
        topWarningFrame.Visible = true
        topWarningFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0, -60)
        topWarningFrame:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0, 18), Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, 0.35, true)
        if warningCoroutine then
            task.cancel(warningCoroutine)
        end
        warningCoroutine = task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(4.5)
            if topWarningFrame then
                topWarningFrame:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0, -70), Enum.EasingDirection.In, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, 0.3, true)
                task.wait(0.35)
                topWarningFrame.Visible = false
            end
        end)
    end)
end

function isUEUnderOffsetY(y)
    if not y then return false end
    local ay = math.abs(y)

    if ay >= 1004000 and ay <= 1004099 then return true end

    if ay >= 1000000 and ay <= 1009999 then return true end
    if ay >= 4000 and ay <= 4099 then return true end
    if y < 0 and ay <= 99 then return true end
    return false
end

function isUnderOffsetPull(hrp, targetHrp)
    if not hrp then return false end
    local y = hrp.Position.Y
    if isUEUnderOffsetY(y) then return true end
    if targetHrp then
        local dy = hrp.Position.Y - targetHrp.Position.Y

        if dy <= -46 or isUEUnderOffsetY(dy) or isUEUnderOffsetY(math.abs(dy)) then
            return true
        end
        if math.abs(dy) >= 1004000 and math.abs(dy) <= 1004099 then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local desyncVisualPos = nil
local desyncActive = false

function captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
    if not hrp then return end
    local y = hrp.Position.Y

    if math.abs(y) < 50000 then
        desyncVisualPos = hrp.Position
    elseif not desyncVisualPos then
        desyncVisualPos = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, 50, hrp.Position.Z)
    end
end

function applyDesyncCamera(hrp)

    if State.TP or State.FakePos then
        desyncActive = false
        return
    end
    if not desyncActive or not desyncVisualPos then return end
    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam or not hrp then return end
        if cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then return end
        if cam.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom and cam.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Follow then
            return
        end
        local focus = desyncVisualPos
        if not focus then return end
        if math.abs(focus.X) > 1e6 or math.abs(focus.Y) > 1e5 or math.abs(focus.Z) > 1e6 then
            focus = Vector3.new(
                math.clamp(focus.X, -5000, 5000),
                math.clamp(focus.Y, -50, 500),
                math.clamp(focus.Z, -5000, 5000)
            )
        end
        local dist = (hrp.Position - focus).Magnitude
        if dist < 150 then return end

        local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        if look.Magnitude < 0.01 then look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else look = look.Unit end
        local camPos = focus - look * 14 + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        if camPos.X == camPos.X and focus.X == focus.X then
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, focus + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
        end
    end)
end

local rageActive = false
local rageLockPos = nil
local rageTargetPlayer = nil
local rageRealCF = nil
local rageRealChar = nil
local ragePreParkCF = nil
local RAGE_RESTORE_NAME = "__chanhyuk_rage_restore"
local RAGE_CAMERA_NAME = "__chanhyuk_rage_camera"
local rageDesyncBound = false
local rageStepConn = nil

function rageGetTarget()
    local name = tostring(State.TargetName or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if name ~= "" then
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and (p.Name == name or p.DisplayName == name) then
                if p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    return p.Character, p
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local closest, closestPlr, minD = nil, nil, math.huge
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHrp then return nil, nil end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local th = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if th and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local d = (myHrp.Position - th.Position).Magnitude
                if d < minD then
                    minD = d
                    closest = p.Character
                    closestPlr = p
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest, closestPlr
end

function rageIsSanePos(pos)
    if not pos then return false end
    local y = pos.Y
    if y ~= y then return false end
    if math.abs(y) > 50000 then return false end
    if math.abs(pos.X) > 1e6 or math.abs(pos.Z) > 1e6 then return false end
    return true
end

function rageUnbindDesync()
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(RAGE_RESTORE_NAME) end)
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(RAGE_CAMERA_NAME) end)
    if rageStepConn then
        pcall(function() rageStepConn:Disconnect() end)
        rageStepConn = nil
    end
    rageDesyncBound = false
end

function rageIsSaneHome(pos)
    if not pos then return false end
    if pos.Y ~= pos.Y then return false end
    if math.abs(pos.Y) > 50000 then return false end
    if math.abs(pos.X) > 1e6 or math.abs(pos.Z) > 1e6 then return false end
    return true
end

function rageRestoreHome(pin)
    if not State.Rage or not rageActive then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end

    if rageRealChar and rageRealChar ~= char then
        local h0 = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if h0 and rageIsSaneHome(h0.Position) then
            rageRealCF = h0.CFrame
            rageRealChar = char
            ragePreParkCF = h0.CFrame
        else
            return
        end
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end

    local back = ragePreParkCF or rageRealCF
    if not back then return end
    pcall(function()
        local rot = hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(back.Position) * rot
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        if pin then
            local hu = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hu and hu:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
               and hu.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
                hu:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function rageCameraAnchor()
    if not State.Rage or not rageActive then return end
    local back = ragePreParkCF
    if back == nil and rageRealCF then back = rageRealCF end
    if not back then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
    local delta = back.Position - hrp.Position
    if not rageIsSaneHome(Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)) and math.abs(delta.Y) > 50000 then

    end
    if delta.Magnitude < 2 then return end
    if delta.Magnitude > 5e6 then return end
    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame + delta
    end)
end

function rageBindDesync()

    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(RAGE_RESTORE_NAME) end)
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(RAGE_CAMERA_NAME) end)
    pcall(function()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(RAGE_RESTORE_NAME, Enum.RenderPriority.First.Value - 1000, function()
            if State.Rage and rageActive then
                rageRestoreHome(false)
            end
        end)
    end)
    pcall(function()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(RAGE_CAMERA_NAME, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 5, function()
            if State.Rage and rageActive then
                rageCameraAnchor()
            end
        end)
    end)
    if rageStepConn then
        pcall(function() rageStepConn:Disconnect() end)
        rageStepConn = nil
    end
    rageStepConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if State.Rage and rageActive then
            rageRestoreHome(true)
        end
    end)

    if not _G.__chanhyuk_rage_hb then
        _G.__chanhyuk_rage_hb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not State or not State.Rage or not rageActive then return end
            pcall(triggerRage)
        end)
    end
    rageDesyncBound = true
end

function stopRage()
    State.Rage = false
    rageActive = false
    rageLockPos = nil
    rageTargetPlayer = nil
    pcall(rageUnbindDesync)
    pcall(function()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hrp then
            for _, n in ipairs({"RageBP", "RageBP_T", "BodyPosition", "BodyGyro", "BodyVelocity"}) do
                for _, o in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if o.Name == n or o:IsA("BodyPosition") or o:IsA("BodyGyro") or o:IsA("BodyVelocity") then
                        if tostring(o.Name):find("Rage") or o.Name == "RageBP" or o.Name == "RageBP_T" then
                            pcall(function() o:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            local restore = ragePreParkCF or (rageRealChar == char and rageRealCF) or nil
            if restore then
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.Anchored = false
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(restore.Position) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.Anchored = false
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
            end
        end
        if hum then
            pcall(function()
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                hum.AutoRotate = true
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                task.defer(function()
                    if hum and hum.Parent then
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        pcall(function()
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam and hum then
                cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                cam.CameraSubject = hum
            end
        end)
    end)
    ragePreParkCF = nil
    rageRealCF = nil
    rageRealChar = nil
end

function triggerRage()
    if not State.Rage then
        if rageActive then stopRage() end
        return
    end
    rageActive = true
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp then return end

    local targetChar, targetPlr = rageGetTarget()
    if not targetChar or not targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
    local targetHrp = targetChar.HumanoidRootPart
    rageTargetPlayer = targetPlr

    if rageIsSaneHome(hrp.Position) then
        if not rageRealCF or rageRealChar ~= char then
            rageRealCF = hrp.CFrame
            rageRealChar = char
            ragePreParkCF = hrp.CFrame
        end

        if ragePreParkCF and math.abs(ragePreParkCF.Position.Y) > 50000 then
            if rageIsSaneHome(hrp.Position) then
                ragePreParkCF = hrp.CFrame
                rageRealCF = hrp.CFrame
                rageRealChar = char
            end
        elseif not ragePreParkCF then
            ragePreParkCF = rageRealCF or hrp.CFrame
        end
    end
    if not ragePreParkCF and rageRealCF then
        ragePreParkCF = rageRealCF
    end

    rageBindDesync()

    if not rageLockPos then
        rageLockPos = Vector3.new(targetHrp.Position.X, 8778, targetHrp.Position.Z)
    else
        rageLockPos = Vector3.new(rageLockPos.X, 8778, rageLockPos.Z)
    end
    local lockCF = CFrame.new(rageLockPos)

    pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(targetHrp, "NetworkOwner", LocalPlayer) end)
            pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(targetHrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false) end)
            pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false) end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            if targetHrp.SetNetworkOwner then targetHrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
        end)
    end)

    for _ = 1, 24 do
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = lockCF
            targetHrp.CFrame = lockCF
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            targetHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            targetHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        local sbp = hrp:FindFirstChild("RageBP")
        if not sbp or not sbp:IsA("BodyPosition") then
            if sbp then sbp:Destroy() end
            sbp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            sbp.Name = "RageBP"
            sbp.Parent = hrp
        end
        sbp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
        sbp.P = 5e5
        sbp.D = 8000
        sbp.Position = lockCF.Position

        local rbp = targetHrp:FindFirstChild("RageBP")
        if not rbp or not rbp:IsA("BodyPosition") then
            if rbp then rbp:Destroy() end
            rbp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            rbp.Name = "RageBP"
            rbp.Parent = targetHrp
        end
        rbp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
        rbp.P = 5e5
        rbp.D = 8000
        rbp.Position = lockCF.Position

        if humanoid then
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
        end
        local th = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if th then
            th:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
        end
    end)
end

function startRage()
    State.Rage = true
    rageActive = true
    rageLockPos = nil
    ragePreParkCF = nil
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and rageIsSaneHome(hrp.Position) then
        rageRealCF = hrp.CFrame
        rageRealChar = char
        ragePreParkCF = hrp.CFrame
    end
    rageBindDesync()
end

function setRage(on)
    if on then
        startRage()
    else
        stopRage()
    end
end

local antiHitLastSafe = nil
local antiNoRageLock = nil
local underAttackSpawn = nil
local underAttackPhase = "hold"
local underAttackUntil = 0

local lastEnemyPos = nil
local fakePosReal = nil
local fakePosActive = false
local lastEnemyCheck = 0
local lastRankRefresh = 0

local ServiceManager = {}
ServiceManager.Cache = {}

function ServiceManager.Get(name)
    if ServiceManager.Cache[name] then return ServiceManager.Cache[name] end
    local success, service = pcall(game.GetService, game, name)
    if success and service then
        ServiceManager.Cache[name] = service
        return service
    end
    return nil
end

function ServiceManager.GetSafeParent()
    local lp = LocalPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
    if not lp then return nil end
    local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg then return pg end
    local ok, w = pcall(function() return lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10) end)
    if ok and w then return w end
    return nil
end

function logBlacklistedPlayer(p)
    if not p then return end
    if detectedLogs[p.UserId] then return end
    detectedLogs[p.UserId] = {Name = p.Name, Time = os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}
    print(string.format("[chanhyuk] 블랙리스트 유저 감지 (기록만 남김): %s (ID: %d)", p.Name, p.UserId))
    showTopWarning(string.format("⚠ BLACKLIST USER DETECTED: %s ⚠", p.Name))
end

function addBlacklistPlayer(playerOrName)
    local targetPlr = nil
    if typeof(playerOrName) == "Instance" and playerOrName:IsA("Player") then
        targetPlr = playerOrName
    elseif type(playerOrName) == "string" and playerOrName ~= "" then
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if string.lower(p.Name) == string.lower(playerOrName) or string.lower(p.DisplayName) == string.lower(playerOrName) or string.lower(p.Name):sub(1, #playerOrName) == string.lower(playerOrName) then
                targetPlr = p
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if targetPlr then
        local uid = targetPlr.UserId
        local uname = targetPlr.Name
        blacklistedUsers[uid] = uname
        pcall(function()
            writefile("chanhyuk lua/blacklist/ID_" .. tostring(uid) .. ".txt", uname)
        end)
        print("[chanhyuk] 블랙리스트 추가 완료:", uname, uid)
        if State.AutoBlock then
            logBlacklistedPlayer(targetPlr)
        end
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local NOSNIY_GROUP_ID = 3461453
local MOD_DETECT_NAMES = {
    nosniy = "Developer/Owner",
    senseiwarrior = "Developer/Owner",
    xlIsinner = "Anti-Cheat",
    xlisinner = "Anti-Cheat",
    liamgame09 = "Team",
    brian1kb = "Team",
    greatguyboom = "Builder",

    mozzzzy = "Wiki Staff",
    curtis2012op = "Wiki Staff",
    nopartythistime = "Wiki Staff",
    somerandomluckyguy = "Wiki Staff",
}
local MOD_RANK_MIN = 200
local modWarned = {}
local rankTags = {}

function isModSuspect(p)
    if not p then return false, nil end
    local n = string.lower(p.Name)
    local d = string.lower(p.DisplayName or "")
    if MOD_DETECT_NAMES[n] then return true, MOD_DETECT_NAMES[n] end
    for key, role in pairs(MOD_DETECT_NAMES) do
        if string.find(n, key, 1, true) or string.find(d, key, 1, true) then
            return true, role
        end
    end
    local ok, rank = pcall(function()
        return p:GetRankInGroup(NOSNIY_GROUP_ID)
    end)
    if ok and type(rank) == "number" and rank >= MOD_RANK_MIN then
        return true, "Group Rank " .. tostring(rank)
    end
    local ok2, role = pcall(function()
        return p:GetRoleInGroup(NOSNIY_GROUP_ID)
    end)
    if ok2 and type(role) == "string" then
        local lr = string.lower(role)
        if string.find(lr, "mod", 1, true) or string.find(lr, "admin", 1, true)
            or string.find(lr, "dev", 1, true) or string.find(lr, "owner", 1, true)
            or string.find(lr, "staff", 1, true) or string.find(lr, "scripter", 1, true)
            or string.find(lr, "builder", 1, true) then
            return true, role
        end
    end
    return false, nil
end

function checkModDetect(p)
    if not State.ModDetect or not p or p == LocalPlayer then return end
    if modWarned[p.UserId] then return end
    local hit, role = isModSuspect(p)
    if hit then
        modWarned[p.UserId] = true
        local msg = string.format("⚠ MOD/STAFF DETECTED: %s (%s) ⚠", p.Name, tostring(role or "?"))
        showTopWarning(msg)
        print("[chanhyuk]", msg)
    end
end

function clearRankTag(p)
    if rankTags[p] then
        pcall(function() rankTags[p]:Destroy() end)
        rankTags[p] = nil
    end
end

function updateRankTag(p)
    if not p then return end
end

function refreshAllRankTags()
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then
            updateRankTag(p)
        end
    end
end

function checkAndActionPlayer(p)
    if not p or p == LocalPlayer then return end
    if blacklistedUsers[p.UserId] then
        if State.AutoBlock then
            logBlacklistedPlayer(p)
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.3)
        checkModDetect(p)
        updateRankTag(p)
    end)
    p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if blacklistedUsers[p.UserId] and State.AutoBlock then
            task.wait(0.05)
            logBlacklistedPlayer(p)
        end
        task.wait(0.2)
        checkModDetect(p)
        updateRankTag(p)
    end)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(checkAndActionPlayer)

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.defer(function()
        local h = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
        if h then
            underAttackSpawn = h.Position
        end
    end)
end)
if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
    underAttackSpawn = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
end

local weatherBloom = nil
local bloomOrigLighting = nil

function ensureBloom()
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    local function getOrMake(parent)
        if not parent then return nil end
        local b = parent:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukBloom")
        if b and b:IsA("BloomEffect") then return b end
        b = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
        b.Name = "ChanhyukBloom"
        b.Enabled = false
        b.Intensity = 1
        b.Size = 24
        b.Threshold = 0.8
        b.Parent = parent
        return b
    end
    local bL = getOrMake(Lighting)
    local bC = getOrMake(cam)
    bloomEffect = bL or bC
    return bL, bC
end

function applyBloom()
    pcall(function()
        local bL, bC = ensureBloom()
        local inten = tonumber(State.BloomIntensity)
        if inten == nil then inten = 5 end
        local sz = tonumber(State.BloomSize)
        if sz == nil then sz = 80 end
        local th = tonumber(State.BloomThreshold)
        if th == nil then th = 0.4 end

        if State.Bloom then
            for _, b in ipairs({bL, bC}) do
                if b then
                    b.Enabled = true
                    b.Intensity = inten
                    b.Size = sz
                    b.Threshold = th
                end
            end

            pcall(function()
                local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                local g = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukBloomOverlay")
                if g then g:Destroy() end
            end)
        else
            for _, b in ipairs({bL, bC}) do
                if b then b.Enabled = false end
            end
            pcall(function()
                local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                local g = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukBloomOverlay")
                if g then g:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local ccEffect, sunRaysEffect, dofEffect = nil, nil, nil

function ensureCC()
    if not ccEffect or not ccEffect.Parent then
        ccEffect = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukCC")
        if not ccEffect then
            ccEffect = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            ccEffect.Name = "ChanhyukCC"
            ccEffect.Parent = Lighting
        end
        ccEffect.Enabled = false
    end
end

function applyColorCorrection()
    ensureCC()
    if State.CCEnabled then
        ccEffect.Enabled = true
        ccEffect.Saturation = tonumber(State.CCSaturation) or 0.2
        ccEffect.Contrast = tonumber(State.CCContrast) or 0.15
        ccEffect.Brightness = tonumber(State.CCBrightness) or 0.05
    else
        ccEffect.Enabled = false
    end
end

function applySunRays()
    if not sunRaysEffect or not sunRaysEffect.Parent then
        sunRaysEffect = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukSunRays")
        if not sunRaysEffect then
            sunRaysEffect = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect")
            sunRaysEffect.Name = "ChanhyukSunRays"
            sunRaysEffect.Parent = Lighting
        end
    end
    if State.SunRays then
        sunRaysEffect.Enabled = true
        sunRaysEffect.Intensity = tonumber(State.SunRaysIntensity) or 0.25
        sunRaysEffect.Spread = 0.8
    else
        sunRaysEffect.Enabled = false
    end
end

function applyDOF()
    return
end

local rainFolder = nil
local snowFolder = nil
local snowLayers = nil
local snowRenderConn = nil

function clearSnow()
    if snowRenderConn then
        pcall(function() snowRenderConn:Disconnect() end)
        snowRenderConn = nil
    end
    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            local old = cam:FindFirstChild("SnowEmitters")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
    if snowFolder then
        pcall(function() snowFolder:Destroy() end)
        snowFolder = nil
    end
    snowLayers = nil
end

function ensureSnow()
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return false end
    if snowFolder and snowFolder.Parent == cam and snowLayers and #snowLayers > 0 then
        return true
    end
    clearSnow()

    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "SnowEmitters"
    folder.Parent = cam

    local SPARKLE = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
    local SNOW_SPEED = 7 * 1.25
    local fade = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.08, 0.05),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.92, 0.05),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })
    local layerConfigs = {
        { name = "Near", area = 100, height = 55, rate = 160, size = 0.6 },
        { name = "Mid",  area = 900, height = 70, rate = 130, size = 1.4 },
        { name = "Far",  area = 2048, height = 90, rate = 120, size = 3.3 },
    }

    snowLayers = {}
    for _, cfg in ipairs(layerConfigs) do
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = "Snow" .. cfg.name
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.CanTouch = false
        part.CastShadow = false
        part.Transparency = 1
        part.Size = Vector3.new(cfg.area, 1, cfg.area)
        part.Parent = folder

        local snow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        snow.Name = "Snow"
        snow.Texture = SPARKLE
        snow.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(200, 235, 255))
        snow.LightEmission = 1
        snow.LightInfluence = 0
        pcall(function() snow.Brightness = 8 end)
        snow.Size = NumberSequence.new(cfg.size)
        snow.Transparency = fade
        snow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(cfg.height / SNOW_SPEED * 1.2, cfg.height / SNOW_SPEED * 1.4)
        snow.Speed = NumberRange.new(SNOW_SPEED - 2.5, SNOW_SPEED + 2.5)
        snow.Acceleration = Vector3.new(2, 0, 1)
        snow.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
        snow.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(20, 20)
        snow.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
        snow.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-50, 50)
        snow.ZOffset = 1
        snow.Rate = cfg.rate
        snow.Enabled = true
        snow.Parent = part

        table.insert(snowLayers, {
            part = part, snow = snow, height = cfg.height,
            rate = cfg.rate, size = cfg.size
        })
    end
    snowFolder = folder

    snowRenderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not State or not State.Snow then return end
        local cam2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam2 or not snowLayers then return end
        if snowFolder and snowFolder.Parent ~= cam2 then
            pcall(function() snowFolder.Parent = cam2 end)
        end
        local camPos = cam2.CFrame.Position
        local glow = tonumber(State.SnowGlow) or 1
        local amt = tonumber(State.SnowRate) or 1
        local szMul = tonumber(State.SnowSize) or 1
        for _, L in ipairs(snowLayers) do
            if L.part then
                L.part.Position = camPos + Vector3.new(0, L.height, 0)
            end
            if L.snow then
                L.snow.Enabled = true
                L.snow.Rate = (L.rate or 120) * math.max(0.1, amt)
                L.snow.LightEmission = 1
                pcall(function() L.snow.Brightness = 4 + math.max(0, glow) * 6 end)
                if L.size then
                    L.snow.Size = NumberSequence.new(L.size * math.max(0.2, szMul))
                end
                local spdMul = tonumber(State.SnowSpeed) or 1
                if spdMul < 0.05 then spdMul = 0.05 end
                local baseSpeed = 7 * 1.25 * spdMul
                L.snow.Speed = NumberRange.new(math.max(0.5, baseSpeed - 2.5), baseSpeed + 2.5)
            end
        end
    end)
    return true
end

function clearWeather(kind)
    if kind == "rain" or kind == "all" then
        if rainFolder then
            pcall(function() rainFolder:Destroy() end)
            rainFolder = nil
        end
    end
    if kind == "snow" or kind == "all" then
        clearSnow()
    end
end

function applySnowSettings()
    if not snowLayers then return end
    local amt = tonumber(State.SnowRate) or 1
    local szMul = tonumber(State.SnowSize) or 1
    local glow = tonumber(State.SnowGlow) or 1
    local spdMul = tonumber(State.SnowSpeed) or 1
    if amt < 0.05 then amt = 0.05 end
    if szMul < 0.1 then szMul = 0.1 end
    if glow < 0 then glow = 0 end
    if spdMul < 0.05 then spdMul = 0.05 end
    local baseSpeed = 7 * 1.25 * spdMul
    for _, L in ipairs(snowLayers) do
        if L.snow then
            L.snow.Enabled = true
            L.snow.Rate = (L.rate or 120) * amt
            L.snow.LightEmission = 1
            pcall(function() L.snow.Brightness = 4 + glow * 6 end)
            if L.size then
                L.snow.Size = NumberSequence.new(L.size * szMul)
            end
            L.snow.Speed = NumberRange.new(math.max(0.5, baseSpeed - 2.5 * spdMul), baseSpeed + 2.5 * spdMul)
            local h = L.height or 55
            local life = h / math.max(baseSpeed, 0.5)
            L.snow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(life * 1.2, life * 1.4)
            L.snow.Acceleration = Vector3.new(2 * spdMul, -8 * math.max(0, spdMul - 1), 1 * spdMul)
        end
    end
end

function applyWeather()
    if State.Snow then
        ensureSnow()
        applySnowSettings()
    else
        clearSnow()
    end
    if State.Bloom then
        applyBloom()
    end
end

local applyRain

applyRain = function()
    return
end

function updateRain(dt)
    return
end

local motePart, motes, weatherAtm = nil, nil, nil
local fogHorizonParts = {}
local fogWorldFolder = nil
local featherList, featherActive = {}, 0
local featherFolder = nil
local FEATHER_MAX = 40
local FEATHER_RADIUS = 120

function parseHexColor(hex, fallback)
    if type(hex) ~= "string" then return fallback end
    hex = hex:gsub("#", ""):gsub("%s", "")
    if #hex == 6 then
        local r = tonumber(hex:sub(1, 2), 16)
        local g = tonumber(hex:sub(3, 4), 16)
        local b = tonumber(hex:sub(5, 6), 16)
        if r and g and b then
            return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b)
        end
    end
    return fallback
end

local flowerFolder = nil
local flowerLayers = nil
local flowerRenderConn = nil

function clearFlower()
    if flowerRenderConn then
        pcall(function() flowerRenderConn:Disconnect() end)
        flowerRenderConn = nil
    end
    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            local old = cam:FindFirstChild("FlowerEmitters")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
    if flowerFolder then
        pcall(function() flowerFolder:Destroy() end)
        flowerFolder = nil
    end
    flowerLayers = nil
end

function ensureFlower()
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return false end
    if flowerFolder and flowerFolder.Parent == cam and flowerLayers and #flowerLayers > 0 then
        return true
    end
    clearFlower()

    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "FlowerEmitters"
    folder.Parent = cam

    local TEX_A = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
    local TEX_B = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
    local col = parseHexColor(State.FlowerColor, Color3.fromRGB(255, 183, 197))
    local colSoft = Color3.new(
        math.clamp(col.R * 0.85 + 0.15, 0, 1),
        math.clamp(col.G * 0.85 + 0.1, 0, 1),
        math.clamp(col.B * 0.9 + 0.05, 0, 1)
    )
    local fade = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.35),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.12, 0.05),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0.12),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })

    local function petalSize(base)
        return NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, base * 0.35),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, base * 1.05),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, base * 0.9),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, base * 0.25),
        })
    end
    local layerConfigs = {
        { name = "Near", area = 70, height = 42, rate = 55, size = 0.55, tex = TEX_A, z = 2 },
        { name = "Mid",  area = 420, height = 58, rate = 70, size = 0.95, tex = TEX_A, z = 1 },
        { name = "Far",  area = 1400, height = 78, rate = 50, size = 1.6, tex = TEX_B, z = 0 },
        { name = "Soft", area = 900, height = 65, rate = 35, size = 1.2, tex = TEX_B, z = 0.5 },
    }

    flowerLayers = {}
    for _, cfg in ipairs(layerConfigs) do
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = "Flower" .. cfg.name
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.CanTouch = false
        part.CastShadow = false
        part.Transparency = 1
        part.Size = Vector3.new(cfg.area, 1, cfg.area)
        part.Parent = folder

        local em = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        em.Name = "Petal"
        em.Texture = cfg.tex
        em.Color = ColorSequence.new(col, colSoft)
        em.LightEmission = 0.85
        em.LightInfluence = 0.05
        pcall(function() em.Brightness = 6 end)
        em.Size = petalSize(cfg.size)
        em.Transparency = fade
        em.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(4.5, 7.5)
        em.Speed = NumberRange.new(2.5, 5.5)
        em.Acceleration = Vector3.new(1.5, -2.2, 0.8)
        em.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
        em.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(28, 28)
        em.Rotation = NumberRange.new(-180, 180)

        em.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-140, 140)
        em.ZOffset = cfg.z or 1
        em.Drag = 0.35
        em.VelocityInheritance = 0
        em.Rate = cfg.rate
        em.Enabled = true
        em.Parent = part

        table.insert(flowerLayers, {
            part = part, em = em, height = cfg.height,
            rate = cfg.rate, size = cfg.size, area = cfg.area,
        })
    end

    flowerFolder = folder
    if flowerRenderConn then
        pcall(function() flowerRenderConn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    flowerRenderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not State or not State.Flower then return end
        local cam2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam2 then return end
        if flowerFolder and flowerFolder.Parent ~= cam2 then
            pcall(function() flowerFolder.Parent = cam2 end)
        end
        local camPos = cam2.CFrame.Position
        local amt = tonumber(State.FlowerRate) or 1
        local glow = tonumber(State.FlowerGlow) or 1
        local szMul = tonumber(State.FlowerSize) or 1
        local spdMul = tonumber(State.FlowerSpeed) or 1
        local spread = tonumber(State.FlowerSpread) or 1
        local flutter = tonumber(State.FlowerFlutter) or 1
        local col2 = parseHexColor(State.FlowerColor, Color3.fromRGB(255, 183, 197))
        local colSoft2 = Color3.new(
            math.clamp(col2.R * 0.85 + 0.15, 0, 1),
            math.clamp(col2.G * 0.85 + 0.1, 0, 1),
            math.clamp(col2.B * 0.9 + 0.05, 0, 1)
        )
        local t = tick()

        local windX = math.sin(t * 1.7) * 2.2 * flutter + math.sin(t * 0.6) * 1.1
        local windZ = math.cos(t * 1.3) * 1.8 * flutter + math.cos(t * 0.9) * 0.9
        for _, L in ipairs(flowerLayers or {}) do
            if L.part then
                local a = (L.area or 400) * math.max(0.3, spread)
                L.part.Size = Vector3.new(a, 1, a)
                L.part.Position = camPos + Vector3.new(0, L.height or 50, 0)
            end
            if L.em then
                L.em.Enabled = true
                L.em.Rate = (L.rate or 50) * math.max(0.05, amt)
                L.em.Color = ColorSequence.new(col2, colSoft2)
                L.em.LightEmission = math.clamp(0.5 + glow * 0.4, 0, 1)
                pcall(function() L.em.Brightness = 3 + math.max(0, glow) * 5 end)
                local base = (L.size or 1) * math.max(0.15, szMul)
                L.em.Size = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, base * 0.3),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, base * 1.1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, base * 0.85),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, base * 0.2),
                })
                local sp = 3.5 * math.max(0.2, spdMul)
                L.em.Speed = NumberRange.new(sp * 0.7, sp * 1.4)
                L.em.Acceleration = Vector3.new(windX, -1.8 * math.max(0.4, spdMul), windZ)
                local rot = 100 * math.max(0.2, flutter)
                L.em.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-rot * 1.4, rot * 1.4)
                L.em.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(22 + 12 * spread, 22 + 12 * spread)
            end
        end
    end)
    return true
end

function applyFlowerSettings()
    if not State.Flower then
        clearFlower()
        return
    end
    ensureFlower()
end

function clearFogFeather()
    pcall(function()
        if fogWorldFolder then fogWorldFolder:Destroy() end
        fogWorldFolder = nil
        if motePart then motePart:Destroy() end
        motePart, motes = nil, nil
        for _, o in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if o.Name == "ChanhyukFogWorld" then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
        end
        if featherFolder then featherFolder:Destroy() end
        featherFolder = nil
        featherList = {}
        featherActive = 0
        if weatherAtm and weatherAtm.Name == "ChanhyukFogAtm" then
            weatherAtm:Destroy()
            weatherAtm = nil
        end
        if fogHorizonParts then
            for _, p in pairs(fogHorizonParts) do
                pcall(function() p:Destroy() end)
            end
            fogHorizonParts = {}
        end
        pcall(function()
            Lighting.FogStart = 0
            Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
            local fb = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukFogBloom")
            if fb then fb:Destroy() end
            local fcc = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukFogCC")
            if fcc then fcc:Destroy() end
        end)
    end)
end

function ensureFog()

    if weatherAtm and weatherAtm.Parent then
        if motePart and motePart.Parent then return end
    end
    pcall(function()
        if fogWorldFolder then fogWorldFolder:Destroy() end
        if motePart then motePart:Destroy() end
    end)
    fogWorldFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    fogWorldFolder.Name = "ChanhyukFogWorld"
    fogWorldFolder.Parent = Workspace

    motePart = Instance.new("Part")
    motePart.Name = "FogMotes"
    motePart.Anchored = true
    motePart.CanCollide = false
    motePart.CanQuery = false
    motePart.CanTouch = false
    motePart.CastShadow = false
    motePart.Transparency = 1
    motePart.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
    motePart.Parent = fogWorldFolder

    motes = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    motes.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
    motes.LightEmission = 0.8
    motes.LightInfluence = 0
    motes.Size = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 6),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 12)
    })
    motes.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.85),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    })
    motes.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(4, 8)
    motes.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.2, 1)
    motes.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
    motes.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
    motes.Rate = 3
    motes.Enabled = false
    motes.Parent = motePart

    weatherAtm = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
    if not weatherAtm then
        weatherAtm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        weatherAtm.Name = "ChanhyukFogAtm"
        weatherAtm.Parent = Lighting
    end
end

function applyFogSettings()
    if not State.Fog then
        pcall(function()
            if motes then motes.Enabled = false end
            local atm = weatherAtm or Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
            if atm then
                atm.Density = 0
                atm.Haze = 0
                atm.Glare = 0
                atm.Offset = 0
            end
            Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
            Lighting.FogStart = 0
            local fb = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukFogBloom")
            if fb then fb:Destroy() end
            local fcc = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukFogCC")
            if fcc then fcc:Destroy() end
        end)
        return
    end
    ensureFog()
    local d = math.clamp(tonumber(State.FogDens) or 0, 0, 1)
    local fogC = parseHexColor(State.FogColor, Color3.fromRGB(200, 160, 255))
    local skyC = fogC:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 80), 0.45)
    local foffRaw = tonumber(State.FogOffset)
    if foffRaw == nil then foffRaw = 0.3 end

    local height = math.clamp(foffRaw, 0, 2)
    local range = tonumber(State.FogRange)
    if range == nil then range = 0.2 end
    range = math.clamp(range, 0.05, 2)
    local fs = tonumber(State.FogStart) or 80
    local fe = tonumber(State.FogEnd) or 1200
    if fe < fs then fe = fs + 50 end

    local distScale = 1 + range * 2
    fs = fs * distScale
    fe = fe * distScale

    local atmOff = math.clamp((math.clamp(foffRaw, 0, 1) * 2) - 1, -1, 1)

    local highBoost = math.clamp(foffRaw - 1, 0, 1)

    Lighting.FogColor = fogC
    Lighting.FogStart = fs
    Lighting.FogEnd = fe

    pcall(function()

        Lighting.ClockTime = 0
        Lighting.Brightness = 1.0 + d * 0.4
        Lighting.Ambient = fogC:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(25, 30, 70), 0.4)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = fogC:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(40, 50, 110), 0.3)

        if highBoost > 0 then
            Lighting.Ambient = Lighting.Ambient:Lerp(fogC, highBoost * 0.35)
        end
    end)

    local function applyAtm(atm)
        if not atm then return end
        atm.Color = fogC
        atm.Decay = skyC:Lerp(fogC, height * 0.3)
        atm.Density = 0.03 + d * 0.2
        atm.Offset = atmOff

        atm.Haze = 0.3 + d * 1.5 + height * 1.2
        atm.Glare = 0.05 + d * 0.25 + highBoost * 0.3
    end
    applyAtm(weatherAtm)
    local other = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
    if other and other ~= weatherAtm then applyAtm(other) end

    if weatherAtm then
        weatherAtm.Haze = 1.2 + d * 2.8 + height * 1.8
        weatherAtm.Density = 0.06 + d * 0.32

        weatherAtm.Glare = 0.55 + d * 0.9 + highBoost * 0.5
        weatherAtm.Color = fogC
    end
    local otherAtm = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
    if otherAtm and otherAtm ~= weatherAtm then
        otherAtm.Haze = 1.2 + d * 2.8 + height * 1.8
        otherAtm.Density = 0.06 + d * 0.32
        otherAtm.Glare = 0.55 + d * 0.9 + highBoost * 0.5
        otherAtm.Color = fogC
    end

    pcall(function()
        local b = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukFogBloom")
        if not b then
            b = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
            b.Name = "ChanhyukFogBloom"
            b.Parent = Lighting
        end
        b.Enabled = true
        b.Intensity = 0.7 + d * 1.4
        b.Size = 28 + d * 30
        b.Threshold = 0.35 + (1 - d) * 0.2
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local cc = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukFogCC")
        if not cc then
            cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            cc.Name = "ChanhyukFogCC"
            cc.Parent = Lighting
        end
        cc.Enabled = true
        cc.TintColor = fogC:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.35)
        cc.Brightness = 0.05 + d * 0.12
        cc.Contrast = 0.05
        cc.Saturation = 0.15 + d * 0.25
    end)

    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local base = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
    local far = 180 + range * 280

    local effectY = base.Y + 6 + height * 28
    if motePart then
        motePart.Position = Vector3.new(base.X, effectY, base.Z)
    end
    if not fogHorizonParts then fogHorizonParts = {} end
    local parentFolder = fogWorldFolder or Workspace
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local ang = (i - 1) * (math.pi * 2 / 8)
        local dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(ang), 0, math.sin(ang))
        local p = fogHorizonParts[i]
        if not p or not p.Parent then
            p = Instance.new("Part")
            p.Name = "FogHorizon_" .. tostring(i)
            p.Anchored = true
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.CanQuery = false
            p.CanTouch = false
            p.CastShadow = false
            p.Transparency = 1
            p.Size = Vector3.new(6, 6, 6)
            p.Parent = parentFolder
            local pe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            pe.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
            pe.LightEmission = 0.95
            pe.LightInfluence = 0
            pe.Size = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 8),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 4)
            })
            pe.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.75),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.5),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
            })
            pe.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(3, 5)
            pe.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.05, 0.35)
            pe.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(25, 25)
            pe.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
            pe.Drag = 0.5
            pe.Rate = 1.5
            pe.Enabled = true
            pe.Parent = p
            fogHorizonParts[i] = p
        end
        p.Position = base + dir * far + Vector3.new(0, effectY - base.Y, 0)
        local pe = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("ParticleEmitter")
        if pe then
            pe.Enabled = true
            pe.Color = ColorSequence.new(fogC)
            pe.Rate = 1.5 + d * 3
            pe.LightEmission = 0.95
            pe.LightInfluence = 0
            pe.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
            pe.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(25, 25)
        end

        local pl = p:FindFirstChild("FogGlowLight")
        if not pl then
            pl = Instance.new("PointLight")
            pl.Name = "FogGlowLight"
            pl.Shadows = false
            pl.Parent = p
        end
        pl.Enabled = true
        pl.Color = fogC
        pl.Brightness = 0.8 + d * 1.2
        pl.Range = 40 + d * 50
    end
    if motes then
        motes.Enabled = true
        motes.Color = ColorSequence.new(fogC)
        motes.Rate = 3 + d * 8
        motes.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
        motes.LightEmission = 1
        motes.LightInfluence = 0
    end
    if motePart then
        local ml = motePart:FindFirstChild("FogGlowLight")
        if not ml then
            ml = Instance.new("PointLight")
            ml.Name = "FogGlowLight"
            ml.Shadows = false
            ml.Parent = motePart
        end
        ml.Enabled = true
        ml.Color = fogC
        ml.Brightness = 1.0 + d * 1.5
        ml.Range = 60 + d * 80
    end
    pcall(applyBloom)
end

function ensureFeathers()
    if featherFolder and featherFolder.Parent then return end
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    featherFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    featherFolder.Name = "ChanhyukFeathers"
    featherFolder.Parent = cam or Workspace
    featherList = {}
    for i = 1, FEATHER_MAX do
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Name = "Feather"
        p.Anchored = true
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanQuery = false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.CastShadow = false
        p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        p.Size = Vector3.new(0.12, 0.04, 1.8)
        p.Transparency = 0.25
        p.Parent = nil
        featherList[i] = {
            part = p,
            pos = Vector3.zero,
            fall = 3,
            phase = math.random() * 6.28,
            sway = 0.7 + math.random() * 0.6,
            amp = 1.5 + math.random() * 1.5,
            spin = (math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5,
            attached = false,
        }
    end
end

function spawnFeather(fe)
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
    local ang = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    local dist = math.sqrt(math.random()) * FEATHER_RADIUS
    fe.pos = Vector3.new(
        camPos.X + math.cos(ang) * dist,
        camPos.Y + 35 + math.random() * 20,
        camPos.Z + math.sin(ang) * dist
    )
    fe.fall = 2.2 + math.random() * 2.5
    fe.phase = math.random() * 6.28
    fe.sway = 0.7 + math.random() * 0.6
    fe.amp = 1.4 + math.random() * 1.8
    fe.spin = (math.random() - 0.5) * 1.6
    local col = parseHexColor(State.FeatherColor, Color3.fromRGB(240, 248, 255))
    fe.part.Color = col
    local s = 0.7 + math.random() * 0.6
    fe.part.Size = Vector3.new(0.1 * s, 0.035 * s, 1.6 * s + math.random() * 0.8)
end

function applyFeatherSettings()
    if not State.Feather then
        for _, fe in ipairs(featherList) do
            if fe.attached then
                fe.part.Parent = nil
                fe.attached = false
            end
        end
        featherActive = 0
        return
    end
    ensureFeathers()
    local amt = math.clamp(tonumber(State.FeatherAmt) or 0.5, 0, 1)
    local n = math.floor(FEATHER_MAX * amt)
    featherActive = n
    for i = 1, FEATHER_MAX do
        local fe = featherList[i]
        if i <= n then
            if not fe.attached then
                fe.attached = true
                fe.part.Parent = featherFolder
                spawnFeather(fe)
            end
            fe.part.Color = parseHexColor(State.FeatherColor, Color3.fromRGB(240, 248, 255))
        elseif fe.attached then
            fe.attached = false
            fe.part.Parent = nil
        end
    end
end

local featherClock = 0
function updateFogFeather(dt)
    dt = dt or 0.016
    featherClock = featherClock + dt
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position

    if State.Fog and motePart then
        local foff = tonumber(State.FogOffset) or 0.3
        local height = math.clamp(foff, 0, 2)
        local range = math.clamp(tonumber(State.FogRange) or 0.2, 0.05, 2)
        local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local horiz = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if horiz.Magnitude < 0.01 then horiz = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else horiz = horiz.Unit end
        local far = 200 + range * 400
        local effectY = camPos.Y + 20 + height * 120
        motePart.Position = camPos + horiz * far + Vector3.new(0, effectY - camPos.Y, 0)
    end

    if State.Feather and featherActive > 0 then
        local R2 = FEATHER_RADIUS * FEATHER_RADIUS
        local floorY = camPos.Y - 30
        for i = 1, featherActive do
            local fe = featherList[i]
            if fe and fe.attached then
                local p = fe.pos
                local w = featherClock * fe.sway + fe.phase
                local nx = p.X + math.cos(w) * fe.amp * dt
                local nz = p.Z + math.sin(w * 0.8) * fe.amp * 0.6 * dt
                local ny = p.Y - fe.fall * (0.75 + 0.25 * math.sin(w * 2)) * dt
                local dx, dz = nx - camPos.X, nz - camPos.Z
                if ny < floorY or dx * dx + dz * dz > R2 then
                    spawnFeather(fe)
                else
                    fe.pos = Vector3.new(nx, ny, nz)
                end
                local pitch = math.sin(w * 0.7 + 1) * 0.5 - 0.35
                local roll = math.sin(w) * 0.9
                local yaw = featherClock * fe.spin + fe.phase
                fe.part.CFrame = CFrame.new(fe.pos) * CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(pitch, yaw, roll)
            end
        end
    end
end

local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local RGB = function(r,g,b) return Color3.fromRGB(r,g,b) end
local NSK = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new
local SPARKLE = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
local SOFT = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"

local auraFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
auraFolder.Name = "ChanhyukAura"
auraFolder.Parent = Workspace.CurrentCamera or Workspace

local rng = Random.new()
function rainbowSeq(off, sat)
	local kps = {}
	for i = 0, 6 do
		kps[#kps + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, Color3.fromHSV((off + i / 6) % 1, sat or 0.7, 1))
	end
	return ColorSequence.new(kps)
end

local state = {
	rainOn = true,
	snowOn = true,
	featherOn = true,
	fogOn = true,
	mist = true,
	stars = true,
	light = false,
	rainMult = 0.8,
}

function newAnchored(parent, shape, size, color, transp)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	if shape then p.Shape = shape end
	p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	p.Anchored = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	p.CanQuery = false
	p.CanTouch = false
	p.CastShadow = false
	p.Size = size
	p.Color = color
	p.Transparency = transp
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

function newEmitPart(parent, size)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Anchored = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	p.CanQuery = false
	p.CanTouch = false
	p.CastShadow = false
	p.Transparency = 1
	p.Size = size
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

function zig(a, b, n, jit, thick, color, dur)
	local pts = { a }
	for i = 1, n - 1 do
		local p = a:Lerp(b, i / n)
		pts[#pts + 1] = p + Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-jit, jit), rng:NextNumber(-jit, jit) * 0.3, rng:NextNumber(-jit, jit))
	end
	pts[#pts + 1] = b
	local model = Instance.new("Folder")
	model.Parent = auraFolder
	for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
		local p1, p2 = pts[i], pts[i + 1]
		local d = (p2 - p1).Magnitude
		if d > 0.01 then
			local part = newAnchored(model, nil, Vector3.new(thick, thick, d), color, 0)
			part.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(p1, p2) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -d / 2)
			TweenService:Create(part, TweenInfo.new(dur), {
				Transparency = 1,
				Size = Vector3.new(thick * 0.1, thick * 0.1, d),
			}):Play()
		end
	end
	task.delay(dur + 0.1, function() model:Destroy() end)
end

function meteor(from, to, size, dur, color)
	local p = newAnchored(auraFolder, Enum.PartType.Ball, Vector3.new(size, size, size), color or Color3.new(1,1,1), 0)
	p.Position = from
	local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, size * 0.5, 0)
	a0.Parent = p
	local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -size * 0.5, 0)
	a1.Parent = p
	local tr = Instance.new("Trail")
	tr.Attachment0 = a0
	tr.Attachment1 = a1
	tr.Lifetime = 0.5
	tr.LightEmission = 1
	tr.LightInfluence = 0
	tr.Color = ColorSequence.new(color or Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.fromRGB(150, 190, 255))
	tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
	tr.Parent = p
	TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Position = to }):Play()
	task.delay(dur + 0.6, function() p:Destroy() end)
end

local SPARKLE = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
local SOFT = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
local SNOW_SPEED = 7 * 1.25

local AURAS = {}
function defAura(name, c1, c2, build)
	table.insert(AURAS, { name = name, c1 = c1, c2 = c2, build = build })
end

local aura = nil
local auraIndex = 0
local auraHexBox, auraHexSwatch = nil, nil

function clearAura()
	if aura then
		if aura.cleanup then aura.cleanup() end
		aura.folder:Destroy()
		aura = nil
	end
end

function auraColors(def)
	local t = State._auraTint
	if t then return t, t:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), 0.55) end
	return def.c1, def.c2
end

function makeOrbiter(parent, size, trailLife, trailHalf, c1, c2)
	local p = newAnchored(parent, Enum.PartType.Ball, Vector3.new(size, size, size), c1, 0)
	local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, trailHalf, 0)
	a0.Parent = p
	local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -trailHalf, 0)
	a1.Parent = p
	local tr = Instance.new("Trail")
	tr.Attachment0 = a0
	tr.Attachment1 = a1
	tr.Lifetime = trailLife
	tr.LightEmission = 1
	tr.LightInfluence = 0
	tr.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1, c2)
	tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.05), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
	tr.Parent = p
	return p, tr
end

function buildAura(index)
	clearAura()
	auraIndex = index
	local def = AURAS[index]
	if not def then return end

	local char = LocalPlayer.Character
	local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not (root and hum) then return end

	local tint = State._auraTint
	local C1, C2 = auraColors(def)
	local DETAIL = 1
	local RATE = 1
	local function sc(n) return math.max(4, math.floor(n * DETAIL + 0.5)) end
	local function pick(c, default)
		if tint or c == nil then return default end
		return c
	end
	local function colOf(col)
		if col == 2 then return C2 end
		if typeof(col) == "Color3" then return pick(col, C1) end
		return C1
	end

	local f = Instance.new("Folder")
	f.Name = "Aura"
	f.Parent = auraFolder

	local list, ticks, anchors, extras, ems = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
	local lightBase, lightFn = 3, nil
	local A = { C1 = C1, C2 = C2 }

	local function addPart(size, shape, col, transp, fn)
		local p = newAnchored(f, shape, size, colOf(col), transp)
		local e = { p = p, f = fn }
		list[#list + 1] = e
		return p, e
	end
	A.part = addPart

	local function emitPart(size, fn)
		local p = newEmitPart(f, size)
		list[#list + 1] = { p = p, f = fn }
		return p
	end
	A.emitPart = emitPart

	local ANCHF = {
		feet = function(rp, fy) return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + 0.15, rp.Z) end,
		chest = function(rp, fy) return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + 3, rp.Z) end,
		head = function(rp, fy) return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + 6.6, rp.Z) end,
		sky = function(rp, fy) return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + 10, rp.Z) end,
		back = function(rp, fy, rcf) return rcf * CFrame.new(0, 1.2, 1.7) end,
	}
	local AREA = {
		feet = Vector3.new(5, 0.2, 5), chest = Vector3.new(3, 3, 3), head = Vector3.new(3, 0.2, 3),
		sky = Vector3.new(14, 0.2, 14), back = Vector3.new(4, 4, 0.5),
	}
	local function anchorPart(name, area)
		local key = name .. "|" .. tostring(area or "")
		local a = anchors[key]
		if not a then
			a = { part = newEmitPart(f, area or AREA[name]), name = name }
			anchors[key] = a
		end
		return a.part
	end

	local function emit(where, cfg)
		local part = type(where) == "string" and anchorPart(where, cfg.area) or where
		local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
		e.Texture = cfg.tex == "spark" and SPARKLE or SOFT
		e.Color = ColorSequence.new(pick(cfg.c1, C1), pick(cfg.c2, C2))
		if cfg.rainbow then e.Color = rainbowSeq(0, 0.7) end
		e.LightEmission = cfg.glow or 1
		e.LightInfluence = 0
		local sz = cfg.size or 1
		if type(sz) == "table" then
			e.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, sz[1]), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, sz[2]), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, sz[3] or 0) })
		else
			e.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, sz * 0.6), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, sz), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) })
		end
		local a = cfg.alpha or 0.2
		e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.15, a), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, a), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
		local life = cfg.life or { 1, 1.6 }
		e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(life[1], life[2])
		local sp = cfg.speed or { 2, 4 }
		e.Speed = NumberRange.new(sp[1], sp[2])
		e.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId[cfg.dir or "Top"]
		local spr = cfg.spread or 20
		e.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(spr, spr)
		e.Acceleration = cfg.accel or Vector3.zero
		e.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
		local rs = cfg.rot or 60
		e.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-rs, rs)
		e.Rate = (cfg.rate or 30) * RATE
		e.ZOffset = 1
		if cfg.orient == "flat" then
			e.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityPerpendicular
		elseif cfg.orient == "streak" then
			e.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel
		end
		if cfg.shape then
			pcall(function()
				e.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape[cfg.shape]
				e.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle[cfg.style or "Volume"]
				if cfg.inout then e.ShapeInOut = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut[cfg.inout] end
				if cfg.partial then e.ShapePartial = cfg.partial end
			end)
		end
		e.Parent = part
		table.insert(ems, e)
		return e
	end
	A.emit = emit

	local function ring(cfg)
		local segsRaw = cfg.n or 24
		local segs = segsRaw > 12 and sc(segsRaw) or segsRaw
		local r = cfg.r or 5
		local thick = cfg.thick or 0.12
		local segLen = cfg.len or (math.pi * 2 * r / segs * (cfg.frac or 0.72))
		local y0 = cfg.y or 0.14
		local speed = cfg.speed or 1
		local phase = cfg.phase or 0
		local tiltX, tiltZ, spin = cfg.tiltX or 0, cfg.tiltZ or 0, cfg.spin or 0
		local wave, waveN, waveS = cfg.wave or 0, cfg.waveN or 3, cfg.waveSpeed or 2
		local rise = cfg.rise
		for i = 1, segs do
			local a0 = phase + i * math.pi * 2 / segs
			local _, e = addPart(Vector3.new(thick, cfg.h or thick, segLen), nil, cfg.col or 1, cfg.transp or 0.1, function(t, rp, fy)
				local a = a0 + t * speed
				local rr, y = r, y0
				if rise then
					local u = (t * rise.speed + (cfg.riseOffset or 0)) % 1
					y = y0 + u * rise.h
					rr = r * (1 + (rise.grow or 0) * u)
				end
				if wave ~= 0 then y = y + math.sin(a * waveN + t * waveS) * wave end
				local lp = Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * rr, 0, math.sin(a) * rr)
				local tg = Vector3.new(-math.sin(a), 0, math.cos(a))
				local b = Vector3.new(rp.X, fy + y, rp.Z)
				if tiltX ~= 0 or tiltZ ~= 0 or spin ~= 0 then
					local plane = CFrame.Angles(tiltX, t * spin, tiltZ)
					local pos = b + plane:VectorToWorldSpace(lp)
					return CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + plane:VectorToWorldSpace(tg))
				end
				local pos = b + lp
				return CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + tg)
			end)
			if rise then
				e.tr = function(t)
					local u = (t * rise.speed + (cfg.riseOffset or 0)) % 1
					return 0.1 + 0.9 * u * u
				end
			end
			if cfg.hue then
				e.col = function(t) return Color3.fromHSV((i / segs + t * 0.12) % 1, 0.7, 1) end
			end
		end
	end
	A.ring = ring

	local function ringLocal(cfg)
		local segsRaw = cfg.n or 36
		local segs = segsRaw > 12 and sc(segsRaw) or segsRaw
		local r = cfg.r or 3
		local segLen = cfg.len or (math.pi * 2 * r / segs * (cfg.frac or 0.72))
		local center = cfg.center or Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 3.2)
		for i = 1, segs do
			local a0 = i * math.pi * 2 / segs
			addPart(Vector3.new(cfg.thick or 0.1, cfg.thick or 0.1, segLen), nil, cfg.col or 1, cfg.transp or 0.1, function(t, rp, fy, rcf)
				local a = a0 + t * (cfg.speed or 0.5)
				local lp = center + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * r, math.sin(a) * r, 0)
				local tg = Vector3.new(-math.sin(a), math.cos(a), 0)
				return rcf * CFrame.lookAt(lp, lp + tg)
			end)
		end
	end
	A.ringLocal = ringLocal

	local function spokes(cfg)
		local n = cfg.n or 6
		local r1, r2 = cfg.r1 or 1, cfg.r2 or 6
		local len, mid = r2 - r1, (r1 + r2) / 2
		for i = 1, n do
			local a0 = i * math.pi * 2 / n
			addPart(Vector3.new(cfg.thick or 0.1, cfg.thick or 0.1, len), nil, cfg.col or 1, cfg.transp or 0.1, function(t, rp, fy)
				local a = a0 + t * (cfg.speed or 0.3)
				local dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a))
				local pos = Vector3.new(rp.X, fy + (cfg.y or 0.15), rp.Z) + dir * mid
				return CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + dir)
			end)
		end
	end
	A.spokes = spokes

	local function spikes(cfg)
		local n = cfg.n or 10
		n = n > 12 and sc(n) or n
		local r, h, w = cfg.r or 5, cfg.h or 3, cfg.w or 0.35
		local lean, ps, spin, jag = cfg.lean or 0, cfg.pulse or 2, cfg.spin or 0, cfg.jag or 0.4
		local y0 = cfg.y or 0
		for i = 1, n do
			local a0 = i * math.pi * 2 / n
			local ph = i * 1.7
			local psi = ps * (1 + ((i * 7) % 5) / 5 * (cfg.pv or 0))
			local curH = h
			local p, e = addPart(Vector3.new(w, h, w), nil, cfg.col or 1, cfg.transp or 0.1, function(t, rp, fy)
				local a = a0 + t * spin
				curH = h * (1 - jag + jag * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * psi + ph)))
				local dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a))
				local pos = Vector3.new(rp.X, fy + y0 + curH / 2, rp.Z) + dir * (r + math.sin(lean) * curH / 2)
				return CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, -a, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -lean)
			end)
			e.sz = function() return Vector3.new(w, curH, w) end
			if cfg.pal and not tint then p.Color = cfg.pal[(i - 1) % #cfg.pal + 1] end
			if cfg.hue then
				e.col = function(t) return Color3.fromHSV((i / n + t * 0.15) % 1, 0.6, 1) end
			end
		end
	end
	A.spikes = spikes

	local function orb(cfg)
		local n = cfg.n or 4
		local size = cfg.size or 0.55
		for i = 1, n do
			local p, tr = makeOrbiter(f, size, cfg.tl or 0.9, cfg.th or size * 0.6, pick(cfg.c1, C1), pick(cfg.c2, C2))
			local ph = i * math.pi * 2 / n
			list[#list + 1] = { p = p, f = function(t, rp, fy)
				local a = t * (cfg.speed or 1.5) + ph
				local rr = (cfg.r or 4.5) + math.sin(t * (cfg.rf or 1) + i) * (cfg.rw or 0)
				local y = (cfg.y or 3) + math.sin(t * (cfg.yf or 1.8) + i * (cfg.yph or 1)) * (cfg.yamp or 1.3)
				local lp = Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * rr, 0, math.sin(a) * rr * (cfg.ellipse or 1))
				if cfg.tiltX or cfg.tiltZ then
					lp = CFrame.Angles(cfg.tiltX or 0, (cfg.spin or 0) * t, cfg.tiltZ or 0):VectorToWorldSpace(lp)
				end
				return CFrame.new(rp.X + lp.X, fy + y + lp.Y, rp.Z + lp.Z)
			end }
			if cfg.rainbow then
				ticks[#ticks + 1] = function(t) tr.Color = rainbowSeq(t * 0.2 + i * 0.1, 0.7) end
			end
		end
	end
	A.orb = orb

	local function shards(cfg)
		local n = cfg.n or 6
		for i = 1, n do
			local p, e = addPart(cfg.size or Vector3.new(0.3, 1.0, 0.3), nil, (i % 2 == 1) and 1 or (cfg.col2 or 2), 0.05, function(t, rp, fy)
				local a = t * (cfg.speed or 1.5) + i * math.pi * 2 / n
				local y = fy + (cfg.y or 3.6) + math.sin(t * 1.6 + i * 1.7) * (cfg.yamp or 1.4)
				local rr = (cfg.r or 3.6) + math.sin(t + i) * (cfg.rw or 0)
				local pos = Vector3.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * rr, y, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * rr)
				return CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(t * 2.1 + i, t * 1.4, i * 0.7)
			end)
			if cfg.pal and not tint then p.Color = cfg.pal[(i - 1) % #cfg.pal + 1] end
			if cfg.hue then
				e.col = function(t) return Color3.fromHSV((i / n + t * 0.15) % 1, 0.55, 1) end
			end
		end
	end
	A.shards = shards

	local function beam(cfg)
		local n = cfg.n or 6
		local len = cfg.len or 26
		for i = 1, n do
			local p = newEmitPart(f, Vector3.new(0.2, len, 0.2))
			local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
			a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, -len / 2, 0)
			a0.Parent = p
			local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
			a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, len / 2, 0)
			a1.Parent = p
			local bm = Instance.new("Beam")
			bm.Attachment0 = a0
			bm.Attachment1 = a1
			bm.FaceCamera = true
			bm.Segments = cfg.curve and 12 or 1
			bm.LightEmission = 1
			bm.LightInfluence = 0
			bm.Width0 = cfg.w0 or 0.9
			bm.Width1 = cfg.w1 or 0.05
			bm.Color = ColorSequence.new(pick(cfg.c1, C1), pick(cfg.c2, C2))
			bm.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cfg.a0 or 0.55), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, cfg.a1 or 0.85), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
			bm.Parent = p
			list[#list + 1] = { p = p, f = function(t, rp, fy)
				local a = i * math.pi * 2 / n + t * (cfg.spin or 0.3)
				local rr = (cfg.r or 3.2) + math.sin(t * 1.3 + i) * (cfg.rw or 0.5)
				local pos = Vector3.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * rr, fy + (cfg.y or 0) + len / 2 + 0.2, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * rr)
				return CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, -a, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, cfg.tilt or -0.16)
			end }
			if cfg.curve then
				ticks[#ticks + 1] = function(t)
					local c = math.sin(t * (cfg.cs or 1.2) + i * 1.3) * cfg.curve
					bm.CurveSize0 = c
					bm.CurveSize1 = -c
				end
			end
			if cfg.hue or cfg.hueRange then
				ticks[#ticks + 1] = function(t)
					local h
					if cfg.hueRange then
						local lo, hi = cfg.hueRange[1], cfg.hueRange[2]
						h = lo + (hi - lo) * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * 0.3 + i * 0.9))
					else
						h = (i / n + t * 0.12) % 1
					end
					bm.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromHSV(h, 0.65, 1), Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.12) % 1, 0.65, 1))
				end
			end
		end
	end
	A.beam = beam

	local function sphere(cfg)
		local size = cfg.size or 3
		local p, e = addPart(Vector3.new(size, size, size), Enum.PartType.Ball, cfg.col or 1, cfg.transp or 0.2, function(t, rp, fy, rcf)
			local off = cfg.off or Vector3.new(0, 4, 2.5)
			if cfg.fixed then return CFrame.new(rp.X + off.X, fy + off.Y, rp.Z + off.Z) end
			return CFrame.new(rcf:PointToWorldSpace(off))
		end)
		if cfg.mat then p.Material = Enum.Material[cfg.mat] end
		if cfg.color then p.Color = cfg.keep and cfg.color or pick(cfg.color, C1) end
		if cfg.pulse then
			e.sz = function(t)
				local s = size * (1 + cfg.pulse * math.sin(t * (cfg.ps or 2)))
				return Vector3.new(s, s, s)
			end
		end
		return p
	end
	A.sphere = sphere

	local function radial(cfg)
		local n = sc(cfg.n or 24)
		local r1, r2 = cfg.r1 or 2, cfg.r2 or 4
		for i = 1, n do
			local a0 = i * math.pi * 2 / n
			local jit = (i % 2 == 0) and 1 or (cfg.short or 0.6)
			local len = (r2 - r1) * jit
			addPart(Vector3.new(cfg.thick or 0.1, cfg.thick or 0.1, len), nil, cfg.col or 1, 0.1, function(t, rp, fy, rcf)
				local center = cfg.center(rp, fy, rcf, t)
				local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
				local a = a0 + t * (cfg.speed or 0.3)
				local dir = cam.RightVector * math.cos(a) + cam.UpVector * math.sin(a)
				local pos = center + dir * (r1 + len / 2)
				return CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + dir)
			end)
		end
	end
	A.radial = radial

	local function vortex(cfg)
		local n = sc(cfg.n or 20)
		local r1, r2 = cfg.r1 or 7, cfg.r2 or 0.5
		local y0, y1 = cfg.y0 or 0.5, cfg.y1 or 4
		local speed, spin, acc, twist = cfg.speed or 0.4, cfg.spin or 2, cfg.acc or 0, cfg.twist or 6
		for i = 1, n do
			local uCur = 0
			local _, e = addPart(cfg.size or Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3), cfg.shape, cfg.col or 1, 0.2, function(t, rp, fy)
				local u = (t * speed + i / n) % 1
				uCur = u
				local rr = r1 + (r2 - r1) * u
				local a = i * 2.399 + t * spin * (1 + u * acc) + u * twist
				local y = y0 + (y1 - y0) * u
				local pos = Vector3.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * rr, fy + y, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * rr)
				if cfg.flat then return CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(t * 2 + i, t * 1.3, i) end
				return CFrame.new(pos)
			end)
