			e.tr = function() return 0.12 + 0.88 * (1 - math.sin(uCur * math.pi)) ^ 1.5 end
		end
	end
	A.vortex = vortex

	local function bubbles(cfg)
		local n = sc(cfg.n or 12)
		for i = 1, n do
			local ph = rng:NextNumber()
			local r0 = rng:NextNumber(cfg.r1 or 1.5, cfg.r2 or 5)
			local a0 = rng:NextNumber(0, math.pi * 2)
			local s0 = (cfg.size or 0.5) * rng:NextNumber(0.6, 1.4)
			local uCur = 0
			local size = cfg.bar and Vector3.new(0.07, cfg.len or 1.6, 0.07) or Vector3.new(s0, s0, s0)
			local _, e = addPart(size, (not cfg.bar) and Enum.PartType.Ball or nil, cfg.col or 1, 0.3, function(t, rp, fy)
				local u = (t * (cfg.speed or 0.3) * (0.7 + ph * 0.6) + ph) % 1
				uCur = u
				local h = cfg.h or 9
				local y = cfg.down and (h * (1 - u)) or (h * u)
				local a = a0 + math.sin(t * 0.8 + ph * 6) * (cfg.wobble or 0.4)
				local rr = r0 + math.sin(t * 1.3 + ph * 9) * (cfg.rw or 0.3)
				return CFrame.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * rr, fy + y, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * rr)
			end)
			if cfg.fade == "end" then
				e.tr = function() return 0.15 + 0.85 * (1 - math.sin(uCur * math.pi)) ^ 2 end
			else
				e.tr = function() return 0.25 + 0.75 * uCur ^ 4 end
			end
		end
	end
	A.bubbles = bubbles

	local function panels(cfg)
		local n = cfg.n or 4
		for i = 1, n do
			local _, e = addPart(Vector3.new(cfg.w or 1.6, cfg.h or 1, 0.05), nil, cfg.col or 1, 0.35, function(t, rp, fy)
				local a = t * (cfg.speed or 1) + i * math.pi * 2 / n
				local rr = cfg.r or 4
				local y = fy + (cfg.y or 3.4) + math.sin(t * 1.1 + i * 2) * 0.8
				local pos = Vector3.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * rr, y, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * rr)
				return CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a)))
			end)
			e.tr = function(t)
				if math.random() < 0.04 then return 0.95 end
				return 0.3 + 0.12 * math.sin(t * 5 + i)
			end
		end
	end
	A.panels = panels

	local function lances(cfg)
		local n = cfg.n or 5
		local len = cfg.len or 6
		local w = cfg.w or 0.2
		for i = 1, n do
			local u = n == 1 and 0 or ((i - 1) / (n - 1) * 2 - 1)
			local ang = u * (cfg.arc or 1.25)
			local curLen = len
			local _, e = addPart(Vector3.new(w, w, len), nil, (i % 2 == 1) and 1 or 2, 0.1, function(t, rp, fy, rcf)
				curLen = len * (1 + math.sin(t * 1.5 + i) * 0.06)
				local dl = Vector3.new(math.sin(ang), 0.25 + 0.45 * (1 - math.abs(u)), 0.6 + 0.4 * math.cos(ang)).Unit
				local dir = rcf:VectorToWorldSpace(dl)
				local origin = rcf:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0.9))
				local center = origin + dir * (curLen / 2 + (cfg.gap or 0.8))
				return CFrame.lookAt(center, center + dir)
			end)
			e.sz = function() return Vector3.new(w, w, curLen) end
		end
	end
	A.lances = lances

	local function serpent(cfg)
		local n = sc(cfg.n or 16)
		local lag = cfg.lag or 0.1
		local function path(tau, fy, rp)
			local a = tau * (cfg.speed or 1.2)
			local rr = (cfg.r or 3.6) + math.sin(tau * 0.7) * 0.7
			local y = fy + (cfg.y or 4) + math.sin(tau * 0.9) * (cfg.yamp or 3.2)
			return Vector3.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * rr, y, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * rr)
		end
		local head
		for k = 1, n do
			local size = (cfg.size or 0.9) * (k == 1 and 1.7 or (1 - 0.55 * (k - 1) / n))
			local p = addPart(Vector3.new(size, size, size), Enum.PartType.Ball, (k % 3 == 0) and 2 or 1, 0.05, function(t, rp, fy)
				return CFrame.new(path(t - k * lag, fy, rp))
			end)
			if k == 1 then head = p end
		end
		return head
	end
	A.serpent = serpent

	local function wings(cfg)
		local tp = WING_TS[cfg.style or "normal"]
		local fpn = #tp
		local lens = cfg.lens or { 7, 9, 10.5, 10, 8.5, 6.5 }
		local phis = cfg.phis or { 78, 60, 44, 30, 17, 5 }
		local c1w, c2w = cfg.c1 or Color3.new(1,1,1), cfg.c2 or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 150)
		if tint then c1w, c2w = C2, C1 end
		local feathers, partsAll = {}, {}
		for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			for k = 1, #lens do
				local col = c1w:Lerp(c2w, (k - 1) / math.max(#lens - 1, 1))
				local parts = newFeatherParts(f, col, cfg.transp or 0.2, tp)
				local offs = {}
				fitFeather(parts, offs, lens[k] / 3.6, tp)
				table.insert(feathers, { parts = parts, offs = offs, side = side, k = k, phi = math.rad(phis[k]), len = lens[k] })
				for j = 1, fpn do partsAll[#partsAll + 1] = parts[j] end
			end
		end
		local cfsW = table.create(#partsAll)
		ticks[#ticks + 1] = function(t, dt, rp, fy, rcf)
			local flap = math.sin(t * (cfg.flapSpeed or 1.7)) * (cfg.flap or 0.28) + 0.05
			local m = 0
			for _, wf in ipairs(feathers) do
				local phi = wf.phi + flap * (1 + (wf.k - 1) * 0.12)
				local dl = Vector3.new(wf.side * math.cos(phi), math.sin(phi), cfg.sweep or 0.35).Unit
				local dir = rcf:VectorToWorldSpace(dl)
				local origin = rcf:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(wf.side * 0.65, cfg.h or 1.1, 0.75 + wf.k * 0.05))
				local center = origin + dir * (wf.len / 2)
				local cf = CFrame.lookAt(center, center - dir, rcf.LookVector)
				for j = 1, fpn do
					m += 1
					cfsW[m] = cf * wf.offs[j]
				end
			end
			workspace:BulkMoveTo(partsAll, cfsW)
		end
	end
	A.wings = wings

	local function disc(cfg)
		local r = cfg.r or 8
		local _, e = addPart(Vector3.new(0.1, r * 2, r * 2), Enum.PartType.Cylinder, cfg.col or 1, 0.75, function(t, rp, fy)
			return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + 0.08, rp.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
		end)
		e.tr = function(t) return 0.72 + math.sin(t * (cfg.ps or 3)) * 0.08 end
		local np = cfg.pulses or 2
		for k = 1, np do
			local _, pe = addPart(Vector3.new(0.05, 2, 2), Enum.PartType.Cylinder, cfg.pcol or 1, 0.5, function(t, rp, fy)
				return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + 0.1, rp.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
			end)
			local function age(t)
				local a = (t * (cfg.pspeed or 0.4) + (k - 1) / np) % 1
				return cfg.inward and (1 - a) or a
			end
			pe.sz = function(t)
				local d = 2 + r * 2.6 * age(t)
				return Vector3.new(0.05, d, d)
			end
			pe.tr = function(t)
				local a = (t * (cfg.pspeed or 0.4) + (k - 1) / np) % 1
				return 0.3 + 0.7 * a
			end
		end
	end
	A.disc = disc

	local function pillar(cfg)
		local w, H = cfg.w or 3, cfg.h or 46
		local function cf(t, rp, fy)
			return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + H / 2, rp.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
		end
		local _, e1 = addPart(Vector3.new(H, w, w), Enum.PartType.Cylinder, 1, 0.86, cf)
		local _, e2 = addPart(Vector3.new(H, w * 0.35, w * 0.35), Enum.PartType.Cylinder, 2, 0.72, cf)
		e1.tr = function(t) return 0.86 + math.sin(t * 2.4) * 0.05 end
		e2.tr = function(t) return 0.72 + math.sin(t * 2.4) * 0.06 end
	end
	A.pillar = pillar

	local function stream(cfg)
		local sa0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
		sa0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0.3)
		sa0.Parent = root
		local sa1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
		sa1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -2.6, 0.3)
		sa1.Parent = root
		local tr = Instance.new("Trail")
		tr.Attachment0 = sa0
		tr.Attachment1 = sa1
		tr.Lifetime = cfg.life or 0.7
		tr.LightEmission = 1
		tr.LightInfluence = 0
		tr.FaceCamera = true
		tr.Color = ColorSequence.new(pick(cfg.c1, C1), pick(cfg.c2, C2))
		tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.35), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
		tr.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.2) })
		tr.Parent = root
		extras = { sa0, sa1, tr }
	end
	A.stream = stream

	local function bolts(cfg)
		local nextT = clock + 0.5
		ticks[#ticks + 1] = function(t, dt, rp, fy)
			if t < nextT then return end
			nextT = t + (cfg.min or 0.5) + math.random() * (cfg.var or 1)
			local ctr = Vector3.new(rp.X, fy, rp.Z)
			if cfg.kind == "sky" then
				local top = ctr + Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-8, 8), 55, rng:NextNumber(-8, 8))
				local bot = ctr + Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-7, 7), 0, rng:NextNumber(-7, 7))
				zig(top, bot, 8, 3.5, cfg.thick or 0.22, C2, 0.35)
			else
				local chest = ctr + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
				local function pt(rad)
					return chest + Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-1, 1), rng:NextNumber(-1, 1), rng:NextNumber(-1, 1)).Unit * rad
				end
				zig(pt(rng:NextNumber(0.8, 1.6)), pt(rng:NextNumber(3, cfg.rad or 5)), 5, 0.6, cfg.thick or 0.09, C2, 0.18)
			end
		end
	end
	A.bolts = bolts

	A.tick = function(fn) ticks[#ticks + 1] = fn end
	A.setLight = function(base, fn) lightBase, lightFn = base, fn end
	A.rainbow = rainbowSeq
	A.meteor = meteor

	local ok, err = pcall(def.build, A)
	if not ok then warn("[Aura] " .. def.name .. " 빌드 오류: " .. tostring(err)) end

	local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
	light.Color = C1
	light.Brightness = lightBase
	light.Range = 22
	light.Parent = anchorPart("chest")

	local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
	hl.Adornee = char
	hl.FillColor = C2
	hl.FillTransparency = 0.85
	hl.OutlineColor = C1
	hl.OutlineTransparency = 0.05
	hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
	hl.Parent = f

	local parts, cfs = {}, {}
	local function update(t, dt)
		if not root.Parent then return end
		local rp = root.Position
		local rcf = root.CFrame
		local off = hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 and 3 or (hum.HipHeight + root.Size.Y / 2)
		local fy = rp.Y - off

		for _, a in pairs(anchors) do
			a.part.CFrame = ANCHF[a.name](rp, fy, rcf)
		end

		local n = #list
		for i = 1, n do
			local e = list[i]
			parts[i] = e.p
			cfs[i] = e.f(t, rp, fy, rcf)
			if e.sz then e.p.Size = e.sz(t) end
			if e.tr then e.p.Transparency = e.tr(t) end
			if e.col then e.p.Color = e.col(t) end
		end
		workspace:BulkMoveTo(parts, cfs)

		for i = 1, #ticks do
			ticks[i](t, dt, rp, fy, rcf)
		end

		light.Brightness = lightFn and lightFn(t, lightBase) or (lightBase + math.sin(t * 4) * 0.6)
	end

	aura = {
		folder = f,
		update = update,
		cleanup = function()
			for _, o in ipairs(extras) do o:Destroy() end
		end,
	}

	update(clock, 0)
	for _, e in ipairs(ems) do
		pcall(function() e:Emit(math.clamp(math.floor(e.Rate * 0.5), 0, 30)) end)
	end
end

defAura("Inferno", Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 60), Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 20), function(A)
	A.emit("feet", { c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 70), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 10), size = { 1.6, 2.8, 0.2 }, alpha = 0.3, life = { 0.7, 1.1 }, speed = { 8, 12 }, spread = 16, rate = 60, accel = Vector3.new(0, 7, 0), rot = 80 })
	A.emit("feet", { tex = "spark", c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 120), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 20), size = 0.6, alpha = 0.05, life = { 1.3, 2.2 }, speed = { 6, 13 }, spread = 45, rate = 40, accel = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0) })
	A.emit("feet", { area = Vector3.new(15, 0.2, 15), shape = "Disc", style = "Surface", partial = 0.6, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 40), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 30, 0), size = { 1, 2, 0.2 }, alpha = 0.35, life = { 0.8, 1.3 }, speed = { 2, 4 }, spread = 10, rate = 55, accel = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0) })
	A.spikes({ n = 9, r = 4.8, h = 4.2, w = 0.5, jag = 0.7, pulse = 5, pv = 1, lean = 0.12 })
	A.orb({ n = 3, r = 4.6, speed = 2.8, size = 1.0, tl = 1.1, y = 3, yamp = 1.6 })
	A.disc({ r = 8, pulses = 2, pspeed = 0.55 })
	A.setLight(4, function(t, b) return b + math.random() * 1.6 end)
end)

defAura("Thunder", Color3.fromRGB(120, 205, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), function(A)
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", c1 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 230, 255), c2 = Color3.new(1,1,1), size = 0.8, alpha = 0, life = { 0.25, 0.55 }, speed = { 10, 22 }, spread = 180, rate = 70 })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 3, 5, 4 }, alpha = 0.78, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 255), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 60, 200), life = { 0.5, 0.9 }, speed = { 1, 2 }, spread = 180, rate = 12, rot = 20 })
	A.ring({ n = 26, r = 3.4, y = 3.2, speed = 6, wave = 0.55, waveN = 9, waveSpeed = 14, thick = 0.09, frac = 0.55, col = 2, tiltX = 0.25, spin = 1.2 })
	A.ring({ n = 20, r = 6.5, y = 0.15, speed = -2.5, thick = 0.1, frac = 0.35 })
	A.bolts({ kind = "arc", min = 0.12, var = 0.3, rad = 5 })
	A.bolts({ kind = "sky", min = 0.9, var = 1.8 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 3, pspeed = 1.1 })
	A.stream({})
	A.setLight(3, function(t, b) return b + (math.random() < 0.08 and 10 or 0) end)
end)

defAura("Galaxy", Color3.fromRGB(185, 105, 255), Color3.fromRGB(90, 210, 255), function(A)
	A.vortex({ n = 34, r1 = 0.5, r2 = 6.5, y0 = 9, y1 = 9, speed = 0.12, spin = 0.9, acc = 0.5, twist = 5, size = Vector3.new(0.28, 0.28, 0.28), shape = Enum.PartType.Ball })
	A.vortex({ n = 16, r1 = 6.5, r2 = 0.5, y0 = 9.2, y1 = 9.2, speed = 0.12, spin = -1.1, twist = -5, col = 2, size = Vector3.new(0.22, 0.22, 0.22), shape = Enum.PartType.Ball })
	A.orb({ n = 3, r = 5.5, speed = 0.9, size = 1.0, tl = 1.6, tiltX = 0.5, tiltZ = 0.3, spin = 0.2, y = 4.2, yamp = 0, ellipse = 0.8 })
	A.ring({ n = 40, r = 6, y = 4, tiltX = 1.1, tiltZ = 0.4, spin = 0.3, speed = 0.5, thick = 0.05, frac = 0.5, col = 2 })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 4.5, 7, 4 }, alpha = 0.8, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 70, 230), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 30, 150), life = { 2, 3 }, speed = { 1, 2.2 }, spread = 180, rate = 9, rot = 15 })
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", area = Vector3.new(12, 12, 12), shape = "Sphere", style = "Volume", size = 0.5, alpha = 0.1, life = { 2, 4 }, speed = { 0.5, 1.5 }, spread = 180, rate = 30 })
end)

defAura("Void", Color3.fromRGB(160, 70, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), function(A)
	A.sphere({ size = 2.6, off = Vector3.new(0, 9.6, 0), fixed = true, color = Color3.fromRGB(4, 0, 10), keep = true, mat = "SmoothPlastic", transp = 0 })
	A.ring({ n = 44, r = 3.4, y = 9.6, tiltX = 1.15, speed = 3.5, thick = 0.14, frac = 0.8 })
	A.ring({ n = 30, r = 2.2, y = 9.6, tiltX = 1.15, speed = -4.5, thick = 0.09, frac = 0.7, col = 2 })
	A.emit("sky", { area = Vector3.new(15, 15, 15), shape = "Sphere", style = "Surface", inout = "Inward", tex = "spark", size = 0.5, alpha = 0.05, life = { 1.2, 1.6 }, speed = { 5, 8 }, spread = 0, rate = 60 })
	A.vortex({ n = 22, r1 = 9, r2 = 0.8, y0 = 0.3, y1 = 0.3, speed = 0.22, spin = 2.6, acc = 1.2, twist = 4, size = Vector3.new(0.22, 0.22, 0.22), shape = Enum.PartType.Ball })
	A.emit("chest", { c1 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 0, 50), c2 = Color3.new(0,0,0), glow = 0, size = { 4, 6, 3 }, alpha = 0.35, life = { 1, 1.6 }, speed = { 1, 2.5 }, spread = 60, rate = 40 })
	A.disc({ r = 9, pulses = 3, inward = true, pspeed = 0.5, col = 2 })
	A.setLight(2.5)
end)

defAura("Prism", Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90), Color3.fromRGB(90, 130, 255), function(A)
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", rainbow = true, size = 1.0, alpha = 0.05, life = { 0.9, 1.5 }, speed = { 4, 8 }, spread = 60, rate = 90 })
	A.emit("chest", { rainbow = true, size = { 3, 5, 3 }, alpha = 0.85, life = { 1, 1.6 }, speed = { 1, 3 }, spread = 15, rate = 20 })
	A.spikes({ n = 12, r = 4.2, h = 3.2, w = 0.35, jag = 0.5, pulse = 2, lean = 0.25, spin = 0.6, hue = true })
	A.ring({ n = 48, r = 7, speed = 0.8, thick = 0.1, hue = true })
	A.ring({ n = 30, r = 5, y = 0.16, speed = -1.1, thick = 0.08, frac = 0.5, hue = true })
	A.shards({ n = 8, r = 3.8, speed = 1.5, hue = true })
	A.beam({ n = 8, len = 28, r = 3.2, hue = true })
	A.orb({ n = 3, r = 4.8, speed = 2.2, size = 0.7, tl = 1.4, y = 3, yamp = 2.2, ellipse = 0.6, rainbow = true })
	A.disc({ r = 8, pulses = 2, pcol = 2 })
end)

defAura("Glacier", Color3.fromRGB(170, 235, 255), Color3.new(1,1,1), function(A)
	A.spikes({ n = 10, r = 4.6, h = 4.8, w = 0.7, jag = 0.3, pulse = 0.9, lean = -0.18, col = 2, transp = 0.25 })
	A.spikes({ n = 6, r = 2.6, h = 2.6, w = 0.5, jag = 0.3, pulse = 1.3, lean = -0.1 })
	A.emit("sky", { tex = "spark", size = 0.7, alpha = 0, dir = "Bottom", life = { 3.2, 4.2 }, speed = { 2.5, 3.5 }, spread = 15, rate = 60, accel = Vector3.new(0.6, 0, 0.4) })
	A.emit("feet", { area = Vector3.new(12, 0.2, 12), shape = "Disc", style = "Surface", size = { 3, 5, 4 }, alpha = 0.85, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 240, 255), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 200, 255), life = { 2.5, 3.5 }, speed = { 0.5, 1 }, spread = 90, rate = 8, glow = 0.6 })
	A.ring({ n = 36, r = 7.5, speed = 0.15, thick = 0.06, frac = 0.9 })
	A.shards({ n = 6, r = 3.2, speed = 1.2, size = Vector3.new(0.22, 0.9, 0.22), rw = 0.4 })
	A.beam({ n = 5, len = 20, r = 4, tilt = 0.1, w0 = 1.6, w1 = 0.8, spin = 0.1, a0 = 0.75 })
	A.disc({ r = 8, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.25 })
end)

defAura("Sakura", Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 205), Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 240), function(A)
	A.vortex({ n = 26, r1 = 1.6, r2 = 3.4, y0 = 0.2, y1 = 9, speed = 0.22, spin = 2.2, twist = 3, size = Vector3.new(0.55, 0.05, 0.38), flat = true })
	A.vortex({ n = 12, r1 = 2.4, r2 = 4.5, y0 = 0.2, y1 = 8, speed = 0.16, spin = -1.6, twist = -2, col = 2, size = Vector3.new(0.45, 0.05, 0.3), flat = true })
	A.emit("sky", { size = { 0.6, 0.8, 0.5 }, alpha = 0.25, dir = "Bottom", life = { 4, 6 }, speed = { 1.5, 2.5 }, spread = 25, rate = 25, accel = Vector3.new(1.2, 0, 0.6), rot = 120, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 210), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 240) })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 4, 6, 3 }, alpha = 0.85, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 200), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 225), life = { 1.2, 1.8 }, speed = { 0.5, 1.5 }, spread = 180, rate = 10, glow = 0.7 })
	A.ring({ n = 5, r = 3.6, len = 2.6, thick = 0.9, h = 0.05, speed = 0.4, transp = 0.5 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 2, pspeed = 0.3 })
	A.stream({ life = 1.0 })
end)

defAura("Seraph", Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 140), Color3.new(1,1,1), function(A)
	A.wings({ style = "normal", lens = { 7, 9.5, 11, 11, 9.5, 7.5 }, phis = { 80, 63, 48, 34, 21, 8 }, sweep = 0.3, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 252, 240), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 140), flap = 0.2, flapSpeed = 1.3 })
	A.ring({ n = 30, r = 2.3, y = 7.2, speed = 1.4, thick = 0.15, frac = 0.85 })
	A.ring({ n = 40, r = 3.0, y = 7.2, speed = -0.8, thick = 0.07, frac = 0.6, col = 2 })
	A.beam({ n = 9, len = 30, r = 4.2, tilt = 0.22, w0 = 1.5, w1 = 0.3, spin = 0.12, a0 = 0.7 })
	A.emit("sky", { tex = "spark", c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 180), c2 = Color3.new(1,1,1), size = 0.9, alpha = 0.05, dir = "Bottom", life = { 3, 4.5 }, speed = { 2, 3 }, spread = 30, rate = 25 })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 5, 7, 4 }, alpha = 0.85, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 190), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 110), life = { 1.2, 1.8 }, speed = { 0.5, 1.5 }, spread = 180, rate = 8 })
	A.pillar({ w = 2.4 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.3 })
end)

defAura("Aurora", Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 190), Color3.fromRGB(190, 120, 255), function(A)
	A.beam({ n = 7, len = 36, r = 5, tilt = 0, w0 = 3.4, w1 = 2.6, a0 = 0.65, a1 = 0.85, spin = 0.08, curve = 7, cs = 0.9, hueRange = { 0.36, 0.8 } })
	A.beam({ n = 5, len = 28, r = 3.2, tilt = 0, w0 = 2.2, w1 = 1.6, a0 = 0.75, spin = -0.12, curve = 5, cs = 1.3, hueRange = { 0.42, 0.85 } })
	A.emit("sky", { area = Vector3.new(18, 0.2, 18), tex = "spark", size = 0.7, alpha = 0.05, life = { 3, 5 }, speed = { 0.3, 1 }, spread = 180, rate = 30 })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 4, 7, 4 }, alpha = 0.85, life = { 1.5, 2.2 }, speed = { 0.8, 2 }, spread = 180, rate = 10, glow = 0.8 })
	A.orb({ n = 2, r = 5, speed = 1.2, size = 0.6, tl = 1.8, y = 5, yamp = 2.5, rw = 1, rainbow = true })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 1 })
end)

defAura("Eclipse", Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 90), Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 40), function(A)
	local OFF = Vector3.new(0, 2.2, 2.8)
	local function center(rp, fy, rcf) return rcf:PointToWorldSpace(OFF) end
	A.sphere({ size = 3.4, off = OFF, color = Color3.fromRGB(3, 2, 5), keep = true, mat = "SmoothPlastic", transp = 0 })
	A.radial({ n = 44, r1 = 1.9, r2 = 4.6, thick = 0.09, speed = 0.25, short = 0.5, center = center })
	A.radial({ n = 30, r1 = 1.75, r2 = 2.3, thick = 0.16, col = 2, speed = -0.4, short = 1, center = center })
	A.emit("back", { area = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), size = { 5, 8, 5 }, alpha = 0.75, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 80), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 30), life = { 1, 1.6 }, speed = { 0.2, 0.6 }, spread = 180, rate = 12 })
	A.emit("feet", { c1 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 15, 25), c2 = Color3.new(0,0,0), glow = 0, size = { 4, 6, 3 }, alpha = 0.4, life = { 1.2, 2 }, speed = { 1, 2.5 }, spread = 40, rate = 20 })
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", size = 0.6, alpha = 0.05, life = { 0.8, 1.4 }, speed = { 3, 7 }, spread = 180, rate = 30 })
	local shadow = A.part(Vector3.new(0.08, 12, 12), Enum.PartType.Cylinder, 1, 0, function(t, rp, fy)
		return CFrame.new(rp.X, fy + 0.06, rp.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
	end)
	shadow.Color = Color3.new(0,0,0)
	shadow.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	A.ring({ n = 48, r = 6, speed = 0.3, thick = 0.08, frac = 0.95 })
	A.setLight(3.5)
end)

defAura("Cyber", Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 235), Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 200), function(A)
	A.ring({ n = 6, r = 5.2, len = 6.1, thick = 0.12, speed = 0.5 })
	A.ring({ n = 6, r = 6.9, len = 8, thick = 0.09, speed = -0.35, col = 2 })
	A.ring({ n = 40, r = 2.6, y = 0.2, thick = 0.08, frac = 0.85, col = 2, rise = { h = 6.5, speed = 0.5 } })
	A.ring({ n = 40, r = 2.6, y = 0.2, thick = 0.08, frac = 0.85, rise = { h = 6.5, speed = 0.5 }, riseOffset = 0.5 })
	A.panels({ n = 4, r = 3.9, y = 3.4, speed = 1.1, w = 1.7, h = 1.05 })
	A.shards({ n = 8, r = 5.2, speed = -1.4, size = Vector3.new(0.35, 0.35, 0.35), yamp = 2.2 })
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", size = 0.5, alpha = 0, life = { 0.3, 0.7 }, speed = { 5, 12 }, spread = 180, rate = 50 })
	A.stream({})
	A.setLight(3.5, function(t, b) return b + (math.random() < 0.1 and 6 or 0) end)
end)

defAura("Crimson", Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 90), Color3.fromRGB(150, 0, 40), function(A)
	A.sphere({ size = 7.5, off = Vector3.new(0, 8, 6), transp = 0.2 })
	A.wings({ style = "thin", lens = { 11, 12.5, 12, 10 }, phis = { 72, 50, 30, 12 }, sweep = 0.4, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 130), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 0, 25), flap = 0.22 })
	A.spikes({ n = 12, r = 4.4, h = 3.6, w = 0.16, jag = 0.6, pulse = 1.6, pv = 1, lean = 0.35 })
	A.bubbles({ n = 12, r1 = 1.5, r2 = 4.5, h = 9, speed = 0.35, size = 0.3, down = true, fade = "end" })
	A.emit("feet", { c1 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 10, 40), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 0, 10), glow = 0.3, size = { 3, 5, 4 }, alpha = 0.7, life = { 1.5, 2.5 }, speed = { 1, 2.5 }, spread = 50, rate = 18 })
	A.vortex({ n = 14, r1 = 2, r2 = 4, y0 = 0.3, y1 = 8, speed = 0.15, spin = -1.8, twist = -2, size = Vector3.new(0.4, 0.05, 0.28), flat = true, col = 2 })
	A.disc({ r = 8, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.25, pcol = 2 })
	A.setLight(3, function(t, b) return b + math.max(0, math.sin(t * 3)) ^ 6 * 3 end)
end)

defAura("Solar", Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 90), Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 20), function(A)
	local OFF = Vector3.new(0, 6.4, 0)
	A.sphere({ size = 2.4, off = OFF, color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 70), pulse = 0.08, ps = 3, transp = 0.05 })
	A.radial({ n = 32, r1 = 1.5, r2 = 3.6, thick = 0.12, speed = 0.5, short = 0.5, center = function(rp, fy, rcf) return rcf:PointToWorldSpace(OFF) end })
	A.emit("sky", { area = Vector3.new(2, 2, 2), size = { 2, 3, 0.5 }, alpha = 0.4, life = { 0.6, 1 }, speed = { 4, 7 }, spread = 180, rate = 40 })
	A.orb({ n = 3, r = 4.8, speed = 1.6, size = 1.1, tl = 1.3, y = 3.5, yamp = 2 })
	A.ring({ n = 12, r = 7, len = 1.6, thick = 0.5, h = 0.05, speed = 0.5, transp = 0.2 })
	A.disc({ r = 8, pulses = 2 })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 4, 6, 3 }, alpha = 0.85, life = { 1, 1.6 }, speed = { 0.5, 1.5 }, spread = 180, rate = 10 })
	A.stream({})
	A.setLight(4.5)
end)

defAura("Nebula", Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 220), Color3.fromRGB(100, 140, 255), function(A)
	local pal = { { Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 220), Color3.fromRGB(200, 60, 200) }, { Color3.fromRGB(120, 110, 255), Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 200) }, { Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 120), Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100) } }
	for k = 1, 3 do
		local part = A.emitPart(Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), function(t, rp, fy)
			local a = t * (0.7 + k * 0.15) + k * 2.1
			local r = 4.5 + k * 0.7
			return CFrame.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * r, fy + 2.5 + k * 1.6 + math.sin(t + k) * 1.2, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * r)
		end)
		A.emit(part, { size = { 3, 6, 8 }, alpha = 0.85, c1 = pal[k][1], c2 = pal[k][2], life = { 2.4, 3.4 }, speed = { 0.3, 1 }, spread = 180, rate = 9, rot = 20 })
		A.emit(part, { tex = "spark", size = 0.5, alpha = 0.05, life = { 1.5, 2.5 }, speed = { 0.5, 2 }, spread = 180, rate = 15 })
	end
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", area = Vector3.new(16, 16, 16), shape = "Sphere", style = "Volume", size = 0.5, alpha = 0.05, life = { 2, 4 }, speed = { 0.2, 0.8 }, spread = 180, rate = 25 })
	A.orb({ n = 2, r = 6, speed = -0.9, size = 0.6, tl = 2, y = 4, yamp = 2 })
	A.setLight(3)
end)

	A.bubbles({ n = 18, r1 = 1.2, r2 = 5.5, h = 9, speed = 0.28, size = 0.55, wobble = 0.6, rw = 0.5 })

	A.ring({ n = 3, r = 6.2, len = 5.2, thick = 0.35, h = 0.06, speed = 0.5, transp = 0.25 })
	A.ring({ n = 3, r = 4.0, len = 3.2, thick = 0.3, h = 0.06, y = 0.17, speed = -0.8, col = 2, transp = 0.25 })
	A.orb({ n = 4, r = 4.2, speed = -1.4, size = 0.6, tl = 0.8, y = 3.5, yamp = 2.4, rw = 1.4, rf = 2, yf = 2.6 })
	A.emit("head", { area = Vector3.new(6, 0.2, 6), size = 0.5, alpha = 0.1, dir = "Bottom", life = { 1, 1.4 }, speed = { 3, 6 }, spread = 25, rate = 14, accel = Vector3.new(0, -14, 0) })

	A.setLight(3, function(t, b) return b + math.sin(t * 6) * 0.8 end)
end)

defAura("Lunar", Color3.fromRGB(215, 230, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 210), function(A)
	A.sphere({ size = 5.2, off = Vector3.new(3, 17, 4), fixed = true, transp = 0.1, pulse = 0.02, ps = 1.2 })
	local glow = A.emitPart(Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), function(t, rp, fy) return CFrame.new(rp.X + 3, fy + 17, rp.Z + 4) end)
	A.emit(glow, { size = { 9, 12, 9 }, alpha = 0.88, life = { 1.5, 2.2 }, speed = { 0, 0.1 }, spread = 0, rate = 3, rot = 5 })
	A.beam({ n = 1, len = 17, r = 0, rw = 0, tilt = 0, w0 = 5, w1 = 3.5, a0 = 0.88, a1 = 0.95, spin = 0 })
	A.emit("sky", { area = Vector3.new(16, 0.2, 16), tex = "spark", size = 0.6, alpha = 0.05, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 240, 255), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 210), dir = "Bottom", life = { 3, 4.5 }, speed = { 2, 3.2 }, spread = 20, rate = 45 })
	A.orb({ n = 4, r = 5.6, speed = 0.5, size = 0.8, tl = 1.2, y = 4, yamp = 1.5 })
	A.ring({ n = 28, r = 7, speed = 0.2, len = 0.25, thick = 0.25, h = 0.05 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.2 })
	A.setLight(2.5, function(t, b) return b + math.sin(t * 1.2) * 0.4 end)
end)

defAura("Dragon", Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 150), Color3.fromRGB(20, 120, 80), function(A)
	local head = A.serpent({ n = 18, speed = 1.3, r = 3.8, size = 0.85, yamp = 3.4, lag = 0.09 })
	A.emit(head, { tex = "spark", c1 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 255, 180), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 255, 120), size = 0.7, alpha = 0, life = { 0.6, 1 }, speed = { 2, 5 }, spread = 180, rate = 35 })
	A.emit(head, { size = { 1.5, 2.5, 0.2 }, alpha = 0.4, life = { 0.4, 0.7 }, speed = { 1, 3 }, spread = 25, rate = 25 })
	A.serpent({ n = 14, speed = -1.0, r = 2.6, size = 0.6, yamp = 2.6, lag = 0.1, y = 3.5 })
	A.spikes({ n = 14, r = 5.6, h = 1.4, w = 0.3, jag = 0.7, pulse = 3, pv = 1, lean = 0.6, col = 2 })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 3.5, 6, 3 }, alpha = 0.85, c1 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 255, 140), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 90, 60), life = { 1, 1.6 }, speed = { 0.5, 1.5 }, spread = 180, rate = 8 })
	A.ring({ n = 40, r = 7, speed = 0.5, thick = 0.1, frac = 0.5 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 2 })
end)

defAura("Blade", Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 110), Color3.new(1,1,1), function(A)
	for i = 1, 8 do
		A.part(Vector3.new(0.1, 3.4, 0.55), nil, (i % 2 == 1) and 2 or 1, 0.05, function(t, rp, fy)
			local a = t * 0.9 + i * math.pi / 4
			local pos = Vector3.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * 4.4, fy + 4.4 + math.sin(t * 1.3 + i) * 0.5, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * 4.4)
			return CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, -a, 0)
		end)
	end

	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 100), c2 = Color3.new(1,1,1), size = 0.6, alpha = 0, life = { 0.2, 0.5 }, speed = { 6, 14 }, spread = 180, rate = 25 })
	A.emit("feet", { c1 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 30, 60), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 0, 15), glow = 0.4, size = { 3, 5, 3 }, alpha = 0.75, life = { 1.2, 2 }, speed = { 0.5, 1.5 }, spread = 60, rate = 10 })
	A.disc({ r = 6.5, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.3 })
end)

defAura("Crystal", Color3.fromRGB(150, 240, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 240), function(A)
	local pastels = { Color3.fromRGB(150, 240, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 240), Color3.fromRGB(190, 255, 170), Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 150), Color3.fromRGB(190, 170, 255) }
	A.spikes({ n = 10, r = 4.6, h = 5, w = 0.7, jag = 0.35, pulse = 0.8, lean = -0.15, pal = pastels, transp = 0.2 })
	A.spikes({ n = 7, r = 2.6, h = 3, w = 0.5, jag = 0.35, pulse = 1.1, lean = 0.12, pal = pastels, transp = 0.2 })
	A.shards({ n = 7, r = 3.6, speed = 1.0, size = Vector3.new(0.55, 0.9, 0.55), yamp = 2, pal = pastels })
	A.beam({ n = 10, len = 20, r = 4.6, tilt = 0.15, w0 = 0.9, w1 = 0.1, spin = 0.3, hue = true })
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", rainbow = true, size = 0.6, alpha = 0.05, life = { 0.8, 1.4 }, speed = { 3, 8 }, spread = 180, rate = 50 })
	A.ring({ n = 6, r = 5.2, len = 6, thick = 0.09, speed = 0.3 })
	A.ring({ n = 6, r = 5.2, len = 6, thick = 0.09, speed = 0.3, phase = math.pi / 6, col = 2 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.3 })
end)

defAura("Chrono", Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 120), Color3.fromRGB(180, 130, 60), function(A)
	local CEN = Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 3.2)
	A.ringLocal({ n = 48, r = 3.9, center = CEN, speed = 0.05, thick = 0.09, frac = 0.9 })
	A.ringLocal({ n = 36, r = 3.3, center = CEN, speed = -0.3, thick = 0.06, frac = 0.5, col = 2 })
	A.ringLocal({ n = 16, r = 1.6, center = CEN + Vector3.new(0, 0, -0.15), speed = 0.8, len = 0.35, thick = 0.3, col = 2 })
	for i = 1, 12 do
		local a = i * math.pi / 6
		local len = (i % 3 == 0) and 0.8 or 0.4
		A.part(Vector3.new(0.1, len, 0.08), nil, 1, 0.05, function(t, rp, fy, rcf)
			local dir = Vector3.new(math.sin(a), math.cos(a), 0)
			return rcf * CFrame.new(CEN + dir * (3.6 - len / 2)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -a)
		end)
	end
	A.part(Vector3.new(0.16, 2.3, 0.08), nil, 2, 0.05, function(t, rp, fy, rcf)
		local a = t * 0.12
		return rcf * CFrame.new(CEN + Vector3.new(math.sin(a), math.cos(a), 0) * 1.15) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -a)
	end)
	A.part(Vector3.new(0.1, 3.3, 0.08), nil, 1, 0.05, function(t, rp, fy, rcf)
		local a = t * 1.4
		return rcf * CFrame.new(CEN + Vector3.new(math.sin(a), math.cos(a), 0) * 1.65) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -a)
	end)
	A.emit("head", { area = Vector3.new(3, 0.2, 3), tex = "spark", size = 0.5, alpha = 0.05, dir = "Bottom", life = { 1.5, 2.2 }, speed = { 2, 3 }, spread = 10, rate = 25 })
	A.ring({ n = 12, r = 5.6, len = 0.35, thick = 0.3, h = 0.05, speed = 0.5 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 2, inward = true, pspeed = 0.2, col = 2 })
	A.setLight(3, function(t, b) return b + math.sin(t * 6.28) * 0.3 end)
end)

defAura("Celestial", Color3.fromRGB(200, 220, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 180), function(A)
	local N = 11
	local sp = {}
	for i = 1, N do
		sp[i] = { a = i * 2.4, r = 5 + (i % 3) * 1.1, y = 1.5 + ((i * 5) % 8) }
	end
	local function starPos(i, t, rp, fy)
		local s = sp[i]
		local a = s.a + t * 0.12
		return Vector3.new(rp.X + math.cos(a) * s.r, fy + s.y, rp.Z + math.sin(a) * s.r)
	end
	for i = 1, N do
		local _, e = A.part(Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), Enum.PartType.Ball, 2, 0.05, function(t, rp, fy)
			return CFrame.new(starPos(i, t, rp, fy))
		end)
		e.sz = function(t)
			local s = 0.5 * (1 + 0.35 * math.sin(t * 2 + i))
			return Vector3.new(s, s, s)
		end
	end
	local links = {}
	for i = 1, N - 1 do links[#links + 1] = { i, i + 1 } end
	links[#links + 1] = { 2, 5 }
	links[#links + 1] = { 4, 8 }
	links[#links + 1] = { 3, 9 }
	for _, lk in ipairs(links) do
		local dist = 1
		local _, e = A.part(Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 1), nil, 1, 0.45, function(t, rp, fy)
			local p1, p2 = starPos(lk[1], t, rp, fy), starPos(lk[2], t, rp, fy)
			dist = math.max((p2 - p1).Magnitude, 0.1)
			return CFrame.lookAt((p1 + p2) / 2, p2)
		end)
		e.sz = function() return Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, dist) end
	end
	local nextM = clock + 1.5
	A.tick(function(t, dt, rp, fy)
		if t < nextM then return end
		nextM = t + rng:NextNumber(1.6, 3.4)
		local c = Vector3.new(rp.X, fy, rp.Z)
		local from = c + Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-20, 20), 32, rng:NextNumber(-20, 20))
		A.meteor(from, from + Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-12, 12), -22, rng:NextNumber(-12, 12)), 0.4, 0.8, Color3.new(1,1,1))
	end)
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", area = Vector3.new(16, 16, 16), shape = "Sphere", style = "Volume", size = 0.5, alpha = 0.05, life = { 2, 4 }, speed = { 0.2, 0.8 }, spread = 180, rate = 25 })
	A.ring({ n = 12, r = 7.5, len = 0.3, thick = 0.3, h = 0.05, speed = 0.15 })
	A.ring({ n = 48, r = 7.5, speed = 0.15, thick = 0.05, frac = 0.9, col = 2 })
	A.disc({ r = 7, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.2 })
	A.setLight(2.5)
end)

	A.spikes({ n = 10, r = 4.8, h = 4.6, w = 0.6, jag = 0.55, pulse = 1.6, pv = 1, lean = 0.15, col = 2, transp = 0 })
	A.emit("feet", { area = Vector3.new(15, 0.2, 15), shape = "Disc", style = "Surface", c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 30), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 20, 10), glow = 0.3, size = { 2, 4, 3 }, alpha = 0.7, life = { 1.4, 2 }, speed = { 1, 2.5 }, spread = 60, rate = 20 })
	A.emit("chest", { tex = "spark", c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 90), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 20), size = 0.6, alpha = 0.05, life = { 0.6, 1 }, speed = { 4, 9 }, spread = 40, rate = 40, accel = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0) })
	A.ring({ n = 16, r = 6, speed = 0.2, thick = 0.14, frac = 0.5, col = 2 })
	A.disc({ r = 8, pulses = 2, pspeed = 0.4, col = 2 })
	A.setLight(3.5, function(t, b) return b + math.random() * 1.2 end)
end)

defAura("Halo", Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 225), Color3.fromRGB(200, 220, 255), function(A)
	A.ring({ n = 40, r = 3.4, y = 7.4, speed = 0.8, thick = 0.12, frac = 0.9 })
	A.ring({ n = 40, r = 3.4, y = 7.4, speed = 0.8, thick = 0.12, frac = 0.9, tiltX = 1.5708 })
	A.pillar({ w = 2, h = 40 })
	A.emit("sky", { tex = "spark", size = 0.6, alpha = 0.05, dir = "Bottom", life = { 3, 4 }, speed = { 1.5, 2.5 }, spread = 20, rate = 22, c1 = Color3.new(1,1,1), c2 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 230, 255) })
	A.emit("chest", { size = { 4, 6, 3 }, alpha = 0.88, life = { 1.2, 1.8 }, speed = { 0.4, 1 }, spread = 180, rate = 8 })
	A.disc({ r = 6, pulses = 1, pspeed = 0.25 })
	A.setLight(3.5)
end)

function shockwave(pos, color, endSize, delay)
	task.delay(delay, function()
		if not auraFolder.Parent then return end
		local r = newAnchored(nil, Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Vector3.new(0.25, 2, 2), color, 0.15)
		r.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2)
		r.Parent = auraFolder
		TweenService:Create(r, TweenInfo.new(1.0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Size = Vector3.new(0.25, endSize, endSize),
			Transparency = 1,
		}):Play()
		task.delay(1.1, function() r:Destroy() end)
	end)
end

function introFx(def)
	local char = LocalPlayer.Character
	local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return end
	local C1, C2 = auraColors(def)
	local pos = root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.9, 0)
	shockwave(pos, C1, 64, 0)
	shockwave(pos, C2, 40, 0.12)

	local ball = newAnchored(auraFolder, Enum.PartType.Ball, Vector3.new(2, 2, 2), C2, 0.2)
	ball.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position)
	TweenService:Create(ball, TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Size = Vector3.new(34, 34, 34),
		Transparency = 1,
	}):Play()
	task.delay(0.6, function() ball:Destroy() end)

	TweenService:Create(bloomEffect or Instance.new("BloomEffect"), TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Intensity = 0.4 + (tonumber(State.SnowGlow) or 0.6) * 2.0 + 1.6 }):Play()
	task.delay(0.16, function()
		TweenService:Create(bloomEffect or Instance.new("BloomEffect"), TweenInfo.new(0.7), { Intensity = 0.4 + (tonumber(State.SnowGlow) or 0.6) * 2.0 }):Play()
	end)

	local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	if cam then
		local baseFov = cam.FieldOfView
		TweenService:Create(cam, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{ FieldOfView = baseFov + 9 }):Play()
		task.delay(0.2, function()
			TweenService:Create(cam, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{ FieldOfView = baseFov }):Play()
		end)
	end
end

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
	char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
	char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
	if State.Aura and auraIndex and auraIndex > 0 then
		task.defer(function() buildAura(auraIndex) end)
	end
end)

function clearChanAura()
    clearAura()
end

function buildChanAura()
    State.Aura = false
    if true then
        clearAura()
        return
    end
    if not State.Aura then
        clearAura()
        return
    end
    local idx = tonumber(State.AuraType) or 1
    if idx < 1 then idx = 1 end
    if idx > #AURAS then idx = #AURAS end
    State.AuraType = idx

    if State.AuraColor1 and State.AuraColor1 ~= "" then
        State._auraTint = parseHexColor(State.AuraColor1, nil)
    else
        State._auraTint = nil
    end
    buildAura(idx)
end

function updateChanAura(dt)
    if not State.Aura then
        if auraIndex and auraIndex > 0 then
            clearAura()
            auraIndex = 0
        end
        return
    end
    if not aura or auraIndex == 0 then
        buildChanAura()
        return
    end

    if aura and aura.tick then
        pcall(function() aura.tick(dt or 0.016) end)
    end
end

local AURA_DEFS = AURAS
for i, a in ipairs(AURAS) do
    a.c1 = a.c1
    a.c2 = a.c2
end

function isKnifeWeaponTable(v)
    if type(v) ~= "table" then return false end
    local ok, name = pcall(function()
        return rawget(v, "Name") or rawget(v, "name") or rawget(v, "DisplayName") or rawget(v, "Id") or rawget(v, "id")
    end)
    if ok and name then
        local n = string.lower(tostring(name))
        if n:find("knife") or n:find("blade") or n:find("dagger") or n:find("katana") or n:find("sword") then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function restoreWeaponCooldowns()
    for _, v in ipairs(weaponTables) do
        pcall(function()
            local orig = originalCooldowns[v]
            if orig then
                for k, val in pairs(orig) do
                    if val ~= nil then v[k] = val end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function applyNoCD(stateVal)
    lastApplyNoCD = tick()

    local keys = {
        "AttackCooldown", "HeavyAttackCooldown", "Cooldown", "FireRate",
        "ShootCooldown", "ReloadTime", "ReloadCooldown", "EquipCooldown",
        "BurstCooldown", "NextShot", "NextAttack", "LastAttack",
        "AttackDelay", "SwingCooldown", "MeleeCooldown", "PrimaryCooldown",
        "SecondaryCooldown", "AbilityCooldown", "SkillCooldown",
        "DashCooldown", "DashCD", "RecoveryTime", "ResetTime",
        "StabCooldown", "LungeCooldown", "SwingDelay", "HitCooldown",
        "ShootSpread", "ShootRecoil", "ShootAccuracy", "Recoil", "Spread", "MaxSpread",
    }
    for _, v in ipairs(weaponTables) do
        pcall(function()
            if stateVal then
                for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
                    local cur = rawget(v, k)
                    if cur ~= nil and type(cur) == "number" then
                        if k:find("Spread") or k:find("Recoil") or k:find("Accuracy") then
                            v[k] = 0
                        else
                            v[k] = 0
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                local orig = originalCooldowns[v]
                if orig then
                    for k, val in pairs(orig) do
                        if val ~= nil then v[k] = val end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function applyNoCDKnife()
    lastApplyNoCD = tick()

    if #weaponTables == 0 and not scanned then
        pcall(scanWeapons)
    end
    local keys = {
        "AttackCooldown", "HeavyAttackCooldown", "Cooldown", "FireRate",
        "ShootCooldown", "ReloadTime", "ReloadCooldown", "EquipCooldown",
        "BurstCooldown", "NextShot", "NextAttack", "LastAttack",
        "AttackDelay", "SwingCooldown", "MeleeCooldown", "PrimaryCooldown",
        "SecondaryCooldown", "AbilityCooldown", "SkillCooldown",
        "RecoveryTime", "ResetTime", "StabCooldown", "LungeCooldown",
        "SwingDelay", "AttackSpeed", "SwingRate", "HitCooldown"
    }
    for _, v in ipairs(weaponTables) do
        pcall(function()
            for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
                local cur = rawget(v, k)
                if cur ~= nil and type(cur) == "number" then
                    v[k] = 0
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
        local tool = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if not tool then return end
        for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("NumberValue") or d:IsA("IntValue") then
                local n = string.lower(d.Name)
                if n:find("cool") or n:find("delay") or n:find("recover") or n:find("swing") or n:find("attack") or n:find("stab") or n:find("lunge") then
                    d.Value = 0
                end
            end
        end
        for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
            pcall(function()
                if tool:GetAttribute(k) ~= nil then
                    tool:SetAttribute(k, 0)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if game.GameId ~= 6035872082 then return end
        local Storage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local mod = Storage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        local lib = mod and mod:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary")
        if not lib then return end
        local ok, ItemLibrary = pcall(require, lib)
        if not ok or type(ItemLibrary) ~= "table" or type(ItemLibrary.Items) ~= "table" then return end
        for name, data in pairs(ItemLibrary.Items) do
            if typeof(data) == "table" then
                local ln = string.lower(tostring(name))

                if ln:find("knife") or ln:find("blade") or ln:find("dagger") or ln:find("sword") or ln:find("katana") or ln:find("melee") or ln:find("fist") or true then
                    if data.AttackCooldown ~= nil then data.AttackCooldown = 0 end
                    if data.SwingCooldown ~= nil then data.SwingCooldown = 0 end
                    if data.MeleeCooldown ~= nil then data.MeleeCooldown = 0 end
                    if data.Cooldown ~= nil then data.Cooldown = 0 end
                    if data.RecoveryTime ~= nil then data.RecoveryTime = 0 end
                    if data.ResetTime ~= nil then data.ResetTime = 0 end
                    if data.StabCooldown ~= nil then data.StabCooldown = 0 end
                    if data.LungeCooldown ~= nil then data.LungeCooldown = 0 end
                    if data.AttackDelay ~= nil then data.AttackDelay = 0 end
                    if data.SwingDelay ~= nil then data.SwingDelay = 0 end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local gunExceptions = {
    ["Sniper"] = false,
    ["Crossbow"] = false,
    ["Bow"] = false,
    ["RPG"] = false,
}
local lastItemLibPatch = 0
function applyItemLibraryGuns()

    if tick() - lastItemLibPatch < 0.05 then return end
    lastItemLibPatch = tick()
    pcall(function()
        if game.GameId ~= 6035872082 then return end
        local Storage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local okReq, Items = pcall(function()
            return require(Storage.Modules.ItemLibrary).Items
        end)
        if not okReq or type(Items) ~= "table" then return end

        for name, data in pairs(Items) do
            if typeof(data) == "table" and not gunExceptions[name] then
                if data.ShootSpread then data.ShootSpread = 0 end
                if data.ShootAccuracy then data.ShootAccuracy = 0 end
                if data.ShootRecoil then data.ShootRecoil = 0 end
                if data.ShootCooldown then data.ShootCooldown = 0.001 end
                if data.ShootBurstCooldown then data.ShootBurstCooldown = 0.001 end
            end
        end

        if State.FastFist then
            for name, data in pairs(Items) do
                if typeof(data) == "table" then
                    for _, key in ipairs({
                        "AttackCooldown", "SwingCooldown", "MeleeCooldown", "Cooldown",
                        "RecoveryTime", "ResetTime", "HeavyAttackCooldown", "HeavyCooldown",
                        "DashCooldown", "DashCD", "AbilityCooldown", "SkillCooldown",
                        "SecondaryCooldown", "SpecialCooldown", "ThrowCooldown",
                        "StabCooldown", "SlashCooldown", "ComboCooldown", "ParryCooldown",
                    }) do
                        if type(data[key]) == "number" then
                            data[key] = 0.001
                        end
                    end

                    if data.ShootSpread then data.ShootSpread = 0 end
                    if data.ShootAccuracy then data.ShootAccuracy = 0 end
                    if data.ShootRecoil then data.ShootRecoil = 0 end
                    if data.Recoil then data.Recoil = 0 end
                    if data.Spread then data.Spread = 0 end
                    if data.MaxSpread then data.MaxSpread = 0 end
                    if data.CameraRecoil then data.CameraRecoil = 0 end
                    if data.Kickback then data.Kickback = 0 end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local fighterRemotes = {}
function getFighterRemote(name)
    if fighterRemotes[name] and fighterRemotes[name].Parent then
        return fighterRemotes[name]
    end
    local ok, rem = pcall(function()
        local r = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local path = r:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        path = path and path:FindFirstChild("Replication")
        path = path and path:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
        return path and path:FindFirstChild(name)
    end)
    if ok and rem then
        fighterRemotes[name] = rem
        return rem
    end
    return nil
end

local lastRemoteAttack = 0
function remoteFastAttack(tool)
    if tick() - lastRemoteAttack < 0.02 then return end
    lastRemoteAttack = tick()
    pcall(function()
        local useItem = getFighterRemote("UseItem")
        local useFb = getFighterRemote("UseItemFeedback")
        local quick = getFighterRemote("RegisterQuickAttack")
        local equipFb = getFighterRemote("EquipItemFeedback")
        local toolName = tool and tool.Name or nil

        if quick and quick:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            pcall(function() quick:FireServer() end)
            pcall(function() quick:FireServer(toolName) end)
            pcall(function() quick:FireServer(true) end)
        end

        if useItem and useItem:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            pcall(function() useItem:FireServer() end)
            pcall(function() useItem:FireServer(toolName) end)
            pcall(function() useItem:FireServer(tool) end)
            pcall(function() useItem:FireServer(1) end)
        end

        if useFb and useFb:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function() useFb:InvokeServer() end)
                pcall(function() useFb:InvokeServer(toolName) end)
            end)
        end
        if equipFb and equipFb:IsA("RemoteFunction") and toolName then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function() equipFb:InvokeServer(toolName) end)
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function scanWeapons()
    if scanned then return end
    scanned = true
    weaponTables = {}
    originalCooldowns = {}
    local function tryAdd(v)
        if type(v) ~= "table" then return end
        if originalCooldowns[v] then return end
        local ok, has = pcall(function()

            local a = rawget(v, "AttackCooldown")
            local h = rawget(v, "HeavyAttackCooldown")
            local s = rawget(v, "SwingCooldown")
            local m = rawget(v, "MeleeCooldown")
            local c = rawget(v, "Cooldown")
            local f = rawget(v, "FireRate")
            local d = rawget(v, "DashCooldown") or rawget(v, "DashCD")

            local ammo = rawget(v, "Ammo") or rawget(v, "MaxAmmo")
            local range = rawget(v, "MaxDistance") or rawget(v, "Range")
            if a ~= nil or h ~= nil or s ~= nil or m ~= nil or d ~= nil then
                return true
            end
            if c ~= nil and type(c) == "number" and not ammo and not range then
                return true
            end
            if f ~= nil and type(f) == "number" and not ammo and not range then
                return true
            end
            return false
        end)
        if ok and has then
            pcall(function()
                originalCooldowns[v] = {
                    AttackCooldown = rawget(v, "AttackCooldown"),
                    HeavyAttackCooldown = rawget(v, "HeavyAttackCooldown"),
                    Cooldown = rawget(v, "Cooldown"),
                    FireRate = rawget(v, "FireRate"),
                    SwingCooldown = rawget(v, "SwingCooldown"),
                    MeleeCooldown = rawget(v, "MeleeCooldown"),
                    DashCooldown = rawget(v, "DashCooldown"),
                    DashCD = rawget(v, "DashCD"),
                }
            end)
            table.insert(weaponTables, v)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        if not getgc then
            print("[chanhyuk] getgc 없음")
            return
        end
        local ok, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
        if not ok or not gc then ok, gc = pcall(getgc) end
        if not ok or not gc then
            print("[chanhyuk] getgc 실패")
            return
        end
        for i = 1, #gc do
            tryAdd(gc[i])
        end

        if #weaponTables < 2 then
            for _, v in pairs(gc) do
                tryAdd(v)
            end
        end
    end)

    print("[chanhyuk] 무기 스캔 완료 (대상 수:", #weaponTables, ")")
end

function isAliveCharacter(char)
    if not char then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    if hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then return false end
    if hum.Sit and hum.SeatPart == nil and hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return false end
    return true
end

function isEnemy(p)
    if not p or p == LocalPlayer then
        return false
    end

    local myTeam = LocalPlayer.Team
    local theirTeam = p.Team

    if myTeam ~= nil and theirTeam ~= nil then
        if myTeam == theirTeam then
            return false
        end
        local ok1, n1 = pcall(function() return tostring(myTeam.Name) end)
        local ok2, n2 = pcall(function() return tostring(theirTeam.Name) end)
        if ok1 and ok2 and n1 ~= "" and n1 == n2 then
            return false
        end

        local ok3, tc1 = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer.TeamColor end)
        local ok4, tc2 = pcall(function() return p.TeamColor end)
        if ok3 and ok4 and tc1 and tc2 and tc1 == tc2 then
            return false
        end
        return true
    end

    if (myTeam ~= nil) ~= (theirTeam ~= nil) then
        return true
    end

    return true
end

function isEnemyCharacter(char)
    if not char then return false end
    local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
    if not plr then return false end
    return isEnemy(plr)
end

function isTeammate(p)
    if not p or p == LocalPlayer then return true end
    return not isEnemy(p)
end

function nameMatchesTarget(p, query)
    if not query or query == "" then return false end
    local q = string.lower(query):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if q == "" then return false end
    local n = string.lower(tostring(p.Name or ""))
    local d = string.lower(tostring(p.DisplayName or ""))

    if n:find(q, 1, true) or d:find(q, 1, true) then return true end

    if n:sub(1, #q) == q or d:sub(1, #q) == q then return true end
    return false
end

function getClosestPlayer()
    local target, shortest = nil, math.huge
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil end

    local query = tostring(State.TargetName or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")

    if query ~= "" then
        local named, namedDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and nameMatchesTarget(p, query) and isAliveCharacter(p.Character) then
                if isEnemy(p) then
                    local thrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if thrp then
                        local dist = (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < namedDist then
                            namedDist = dist
                            named = p.Character
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if named then return named end

    end

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if isEnemy(p) and isAliveCharacter(p.Character) then
            local thrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if thrp then
                local dist = (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < shortest then
                    shortest = dist
                    target = p.Character
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return target
end

local perfectBlockBackup = {}
local lastPerfectBlockPatch = 0

function isRiotShieldToolName(name)
    local n = tostring(name or "")
    if n == "Riot Shield" or n == "Riot shield" then return true end
    local ln = string.lower(n):gsub("%s+", " ")
    return ln == "riot shield" or ln == "riotshield"
end

function boostRange(v)
    if type(v) ~= "number" or v ~= v then return 99999 end
    if v <= 0 then return 99999 end
    local x = v * 50
    if x < 99999 then return 99999 end
    return x
end

function patchRiotShieldTool(tool)
    if not tool then return end
    local okTool = pcall(function()
        return tool:IsA("Tool") and isRiotShieldToolName(tool.Name)
    end)
    if not okTool then return end
    if not tool:IsA("Tool") then return end
    if not isRiotShieldToolName(tool.Name) then return end

    pcall(function()
        local attrs = tool:GetAttributes()
        if type(attrs) ~= "table" then return end
        for k, v in pairs(attrs) do
            if type(v) == "number" then
                local lk = string.lower(tostring(k))
                if (v >= 450 and v <= 550)
                    or lk:find("durab")
                    or (lk:find("shield") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp")))
                    or (lk:find("block") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp")))
                    or (lk:find("absorb") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp"))) then
                    tool:SetAttribute(k, math.huge)
                elseif lk:find("range") or lk:find("radius") or lk:find("angle") or lk:find("reach") then
                    tool:SetAttribute(k, boostRange(v))
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        for _, k in ipairs({
            "ShieldHealth", "MaxShieldHealth", "ShieldHP", "Durability", "MaxDurability",
            "BlockHealth", "AbsorbHealth", "ShieldDurability", "Health", "CurrentShieldHealth"
        }) do
            tool:SetAttribute(k, math.huge)
        end
        for _, k in ipairs({
            "BlockAngle", "AbsorbAngle", "DefenseAngle", "ShieldAngle",
            "BlockRange", "BlockRadius", "AbsorbRange", "ShieldRange", "AttackReach"
        }) do
            tool:SetAttribute(k, 99999)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("NumberValue") or d:IsA("IntValue") then
                local val = d.Value
                local lk = string.lower(d.Name)
                if type(val) == "number" then
                    if (val >= 450 and val <= 550)
                        or lk:find("durab")
                        or (lk:find("shield") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp")))
                        or (lk:find("block") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp"))) then
                        d.Value = math.huge
                    elseif lk:find("range") or lk:find("radius") or lk:find("angle") or lk:find("reach") then
                        d.Value = boostRange(val)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function scanAndPatchAllRiotShields()
    local function scanContainer(parent)
        if not parent then return end
        pcall(function()
            for _, ch in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("Tool") and isRiotShieldToolName(ch.Name) then
                    patchRiotShieldTool(ch)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    scanContainer(LocalPlayer.Character)
    scanContainer(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"))
end

function restorePerfectBlock()
    perfectBlockGcDone = false
    perfectBlockGcTables = {}
    pcall(function()
        if game.GameId ~= 6035872082 then return end
        local ok, Items = pcall(function()
            return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.ItemLibrary).Items
        end)
        if not ok or type(Items) ~= "table" then return end
        for name, bak in pairs(perfectBlockBackup) do
            local data = Items[name]
            if typeof(data) == "table" and type(bak) == "table" then
                for k, v in pairs(bak) do
                    if type(v) ~= "table" then
                        data[k] = v
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    perfectBlockBackup = {}
end

local perfectBlockGcDone = false
local perfectBlockGcTables = {}

function patchShieldDataTable(data)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
    local hit = false
    for k, v in pairs(data) do
        if type(v) == "number" then
            local lk = string.lower(tostring(k))
            if (v >= 450 and v <= 550)
                or lk:find("durab")
                or (lk:find("shield") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp")))
                or (lk:find("block") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp")))
                or (lk:find("absorb") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp"))) then
                rawset(data, k, math.huge)
                hit = true
            elseif lk:find("range") or lk:find("radius") or lk:find("angle") or lk:find("reach") then
                rawset(data, k, boostRange(v))
                hit = true
            end
        end
    end
    for _, k in ipairs({
        "ShieldHealth", "MaxShieldHealth", "ShieldHP", "Durability", "MaxDurability",
        "BlockHealth", "AbsorbHealth", "ShieldDurability", "Health", "CurrentShieldHealth"
    }) do
        rawset(data, k, math.huge)
        hit = true
    end
    for _, k in ipairs({
        "BlockAngle", "AbsorbAngle", "DefenseAngle", "ShieldAngle",
        "BlockRange", "BlockRadius", "AbsorbRange", "ShieldRange", "AttackReach"
    }) do
        rawset(data, k, 99999)
        hit = true
    end
    return hit
end

function scanPerfectBlockGetgc(force)
    if not force and perfectBlockGcDone and #perfectBlockGcTables > 0 then

        for _, data in ipairs(perfectBlockGcTables) do
            pcall(patchShieldDataTable, data)
        end
        return
    end
    if perfectBlockGcDone and not force then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return end
            local ok, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
            if not ok or not gc then ok, gc = pcall(getgc) end
            if not ok or not gc then return end
            local found = {}
            local n = 0
            local function consider(v)
                if type(v) ~= "table" then return end
                local nm = rawget(v, "Name")
                if isRiotShieldToolName(nm) then
                    table.insert(found, v)
                    pcall(patchShieldDataTable, v)
                    return
                end

                local isInst = false
                pcall(function()
                    if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("Tool") and isRiotShieldToolName(v.Name) then
                        isInst = true
                        patchRiotShieldTool(v)
                    end
                end)
                if isInst then return end

                local sh = rawget(v, "ShieldHealth") or rawget(v, "MaxShieldHealth") or rawget(v, "Durability")
                if type(sh) == "number" and sh >= 450 and sh <= 550 then
                    local hasShieldHint = rawget(v, "BlockAngle") or rawget(v, "BlockRange") or rawget(v, "AbsorbAngle")
                    if hasShieldHint or isRiotShieldToolName(nm) then
                        table.insert(found, v)
                        pcall(patchShieldDataTable, v)
                    end
                end
            end
            if type(gc) == "table" then
                local len = #gc
                if len > 0 then
                    for i = 1, math.min(len, 15000) do
                        consider(gc[i])
                        n = n + 1
                        if n % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
                    end
                end
                if #found < 1 then
                    n = 0
                    for _, v in pairs(gc) do
                        consider(v)
                        n = n + 1
                        if n > 10000 then break end
                        if n % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
                    end
                end
            end
            perfectBlockGcTables = found
            perfectBlockGcDone = true
            if #found > 0 then
                print("[chanhyuk] Perfect Block getgc found:", #found)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

function applyPerfectBlock(force)
    if not State or not State.PerfectBlock then return end
    if not force and (tick() - lastPerfectBlockPatch) < 0.5 then return end
    lastPerfectBlockPatch = tick()

    pcall(scanAndPatchAllRiotShields)

    pcall(function() scanPerfectBlockGetgc(force) end)

    pcall(function()
        if game.GameId ~= 6035872082 then return end
        local ok, Items = pcall(function()
            return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.ItemLibrary).Items
        end)
        if not ok or type(Items) ~= "table" then return end
        for name, data in pairs(Items) do
            if typeof(data) == "table" and isRiotShieldToolName(name) then
                if not perfectBlockBackup[name] then
                    perfectBlockBackup[name] = {}
                end
                local bak = perfectBlockBackup[name]
                for k, v in pairs(data) do
                    if type(v) == "number" then
                        local lk = string.lower(tostring(k))
                        if (v >= 450 and v <= 550)
                            or lk:find("durab")
                            or (lk:find("shield") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp")))
                            or (lk:find("block") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp")))
                            or (lk:find("absorb") and (lk:find("health") or lk:find("hp"))) then
                            if bak[k] == nil then bak[k] = v end
                            data[k] = math.huge
                        elseif lk:find("range") or lk:find("radius") or lk:find("angle") or lk:find("reach") then
                            if bak[k] == nil then bak[k] = v end
                            data[k] = boostRange(v)
                        end
                    end
                end
                for _, k in ipairs({
                    "ShieldHealth", "MaxShieldHealth", "ShieldHP", "Durability", "MaxDurability",
                    "BlockHealth", "AbsorbHealth", "ShieldDurability", "Health"
                }) do
                    if bak[k] == nil and data[k] ~= nil then bak[k] = data[k] end
                    data[k] = math.huge
                end
                for _, k in ipairs({
                    "BlockAngle", "AbsorbAngle", "DefenseAngle", "ShieldAngle",
                    "BlockRange", "BlockRadius", "AbsorbRange", "ShieldRange", "AttackReach"
                }) do
                    if bak[k] == nil and data[k] ~= nil then bak[k] = data[k] end
                    data[k] = 99999
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local silentHookInstalled = false
local silentOldFire = nil
local lionEnums = nil
local lionUseItemRemote = nil

function lionGetEnums()
    if lionEnums then return lionEnums end
    pcall(function()
        local m = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        local e = m and m:FindFirstChild("Enums")
        if e then lionEnums = require(e) end
    end)
    return lionEnums
end

function lionGetUseItemRemote()
    if lionUseItemRemote and lionUseItemRemote.Parent then return lionUseItemRemote end
    pcall(function()
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local items = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Items")
        local use = items and (items:FindFirstChild("UseItem") or items:FindFirstChild("Use"))
        if use then lionUseItemRemote = use return end

        if remotes then
            for _, d in ipairs(remotes:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") and (d.Name == "UseItem" or d.Name:lower():find("useitem")) then
                    lionUseItemRemote = d
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    return lionUseItemRemote
end

function silentGetTargetPart()
    local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
    if not tChar then return nil, nil end
    local head = tChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local hrp = tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local part = head or hrp
    if not part then return nil, nil end
    local fov = tonumber(State.SilentAimFOV) or 0
    if fov > 0 then
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if onScreen then
                local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                local md = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - Vector2.new(mouse.X, mouse.Y)).Magnitude
                if md > fov then
                    return nil, nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return tChar, part
end

    return
end

local lionDesyncConn = nil
local lionDesyncClientCF = nil
local lionDesyncServerCF = nil
local ugGroundCache = nil
local ugGroundCacheT = 0

function stopLionDesync()
    if lionDesyncConn then
        pcall(function() lionDesyncConn:Disconnect() end)
        lionDesyncConn = nil
    end
    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ChanLionDesync")
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ChanUGDesync")
    end)
    lionDesyncClientCF = nil
    lionDesyncServerCF = nil
    pcall(function()
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            local bp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanUG_BP")
            if bp then bp:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
end

function startLionDesync()
    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ChanLionDesync")
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ChanUGDesync")
    end)
    pcall(function()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("ChanUGDesync", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value, function()
            if not State.Underground then return end
            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp or not lionDesyncClientCF then return end

            hrp.CFrame = lionDesyncClientCF
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end)

    if lionDesyncConn then
        pcall(function() lionDesyncConn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    lionDesyncConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not State.Underground then return end
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp or not lionDesyncClientCF then return end
        hrp.CFrame = lionDesyncClientCF
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end

function getGroundYUnderMe(hrp)
    local origin = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = char and {char} or {}
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), params)
    if hit then
        return hit.Position.Y
    end

    if ugGroundCache and (tick() - ugGroundCacheT) < 2 then
        return ugGroundCache
    end
    return hrp.Position.Y - 3
end

function applyLionUnderground(hrp)
    if not State.Underground or not hrp then
        return
    end
    State.UndergroundDesync = true

    local depth = tonumber(State.UndergroundDistance) or 6
    if depth < 1 then depth = 1 end
    if depth > 80 then depth = 80 end

    local groundY = getGroundYUnderMe(hrp)
    ugGroundCache = groundY
    ugGroundCacheT = tick()
    local standY = groundY + 3
    local rot = hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position

    if not lionDesyncClientCF then
        local px, pz = hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Z
        if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 5000 and hrp.Position.Y > groundY - 2 then
            px, pz = hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Z
        end
        lionDesyncClientCF = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(px, standY, pz)) * rot
    else

        local p = lionDesyncClientCF.Position
        if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 5000 and hrp.Position.Y > groundY - 2 then
            lionDesyncClientCF = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, standY, hrp.Position.Z)) * rot
        else
            lionDesyncClientCF = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(p.X, standY, p.Z)) * rot
        end
    end

    local base = lionDesyncClientCF.Position
    local serverPos = Vector3.new(base.X, groundY - depth, base.Z)
    local ugCF = CFrame.new(serverPos) * rot
    lionDesyncServerCF = ugCF

    pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
        end
    end)

    for _ = 1, 28 do
        hrp.CFrame = ugCF
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end

    local bp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanUG_BP")
    if not bp or not bp:IsA("BodyPosition") then
        if bp then pcall(function() bp:Destroy() end) end
        bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
        bp.Name = "ChanUG_BP"
        bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
        bp.P = 1e6
        bp.D = 5000
        bp.Parent = hrp
    end
    bp.Position = serverPos
    bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
    bp.P = 1e6
    hrp.Anchored = false

    startLionDesync()
end
