    return
end

local indicatorGui = nil
local indicatorLabels = {}
local maulOriginals = {}

function ensureIndicatorGui()
    if indicatorGui and indicatorGui.Parent then return indicatorGui end
    local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 3)
    if not pg then return nil end
    local old = pg:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukIndicators")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "ChanhyukIndicators"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 99990
    gui.Parent = pg
    indicatorGui = gui
    indicatorLabels = {}
    return gui
end

function setIndicatorLines(lines)
    local gui = ensureIndicatorGui()
    if not gui then return end
    if not State.Indicators then
        for _, lb in pairs(indicatorLabels) do
            lb.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end
    local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local ox = tonumber(State.IndicatorOffsetX) or 0
    local oy = tonumber(State.IndicatorOffsetY) or 25
    local baseX = mouse.X + ox
    local baseY = mouse.Y + oy
    for i = 1, math.max(#lines, #indicatorLabels) do
        local lb = indicatorLabels[i]
        if not lb then
            lb = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lb.Name = "Ind" .. i
            lb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 18)
            lb.Font = Enum.Font.Code
            lb.TextSize = 14
            lb.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lb.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
            lb.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            lb.Parent = gui
            indicatorLabels[i] = lb
        end
        if lines[i] then
            lb.Visible = true
            lb.Text = lines[i]
            lb.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 240, 255)
            lb.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(baseX, baseY + (i - 1) * 18)
        else
            lb.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

function updateIndicators()
    return
end

function restoreMaulSlam()
    for info, vals in pairs(maulOriginals) do
        pcall(function()
            if type(info) == "table" then
                for k, v in pairs(vals) do
                    info[k] = v
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    table.clear(maulOriginals)
end

function applyMaulSlam()
    if not State.MaulSlam then
        restoreMaulSlam()
        return
    end
    local mult = tonumber(State.MaulSlamMult) or 3
    if mult < 1 then mult = 1 end

    pcall(function()
        local fc = require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.FighterController)
        local fighter = fc.LocalFighter or (fc.GetFighter and fc:GetFighter(LocalPlayer))
        local item = fighter and fighter.EquippedItem
        local info = item and item.Info
        if type(info) == "table" then
            if not maulOriginals[info] then
                maulOriginals[info] = {}
            end
            for _, key in ipairs({"SlamDamage", "SlamRadius", "SlamForce", "GroundSlamDamage", "GroundSlamRadius"}) do
                if type(info[key]) == "number" then
                    if maulOriginals[info][key] == nil then
                        maulOriginals[info][key] = info[key]
                    end
                    info[key] = maulOriginals[info][key] * mult
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local Items = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.ItemLibrary).Items
        for name, data in pairs(Items) do
            if type(data) == "table" and type(name) == "string" then
                local ln = string.lower(name)
                if ln:find("maul", 1, true) or ln:find("slam", 1, true) or data.SlamDamage or data.SlamRadius then
                    if not maulOriginals[data] then maulOriginals[data] = {} end
                    for _, key in ipairs({"SlamDamage", "SlamRadius", "SlamForce", "GroundSlamDamage", "GroundSlamRadius"}) do
                        if type(data[key]) == "number" then
                            if maulOriginals[data][key] == nil then
                                maulOriginals[data][key] = data[key]
                            end
                            data[key] = maulOriginals[data][key] * mult
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local magicBulletHooked = false
local magicBulletParts = {}
local magicStopAccum = 0

function magicGetTargetHrp()
    local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
    if not tChar then return nil end
    local hrp = tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local range = tonumber(State.MagicBulletRange)
    if range and range > 0 then
        local my = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if my and (my.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > range then
            return nil
        end
    end
    return hrp
end

function isMagicProjectile(inst)
    if not inst then return false end
    local n = string.lower(tostring(inst.Name or ""))
    if n:find("ball", 1, true) or n:find("arrow", 1, true) or n:find("bolt", 1, true)
        or n:find("projectile", 1, true) or n:find("sling", 1, true) or n:find("pellet", 1, true)
        or n:find("bullet", 1, true) or n:find("round", 1, true) or n:find("shot", 1, true)
        or n:find("thrown", 1, true) or n:find("missile", 1, true) or n:find("proj", 1, true)
        or n:find("dagger", 1, true) or n:find("knife", 1, true) or n:find("rocket", 1, true) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function isUnderCharOrTool(inst)
    local p = inst
    while p and p ~= Workspace do
        if p:IsA("Tool") or p:IsA("Backpack") or p:IsA("Player") then return true end
        if p:IsA("Model") and p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
        p = p.Parent
    end
    return false
end

function magicTrackPart(part)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
    if isUnderCharOrTool(part) then return end
    if not isMagicProjectile(part) and not isMagicProjectile(part.Parent) then return end
    local sz = math.max(part.Size.X, part.Size.Y, part.Size.Z)
    if sz > 80 then return end
    for _, e in ipairs(magicBulletParts) do
        if e.part == part then return end
    end
    pcall(function()
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanTouch = true
        part.CanQuery = true
        part.Massless = true

        if part.CollisionGroup then
            pcall(function() part.CollisionGroup = "Default" end)
        end
    end)
    table.insert(magicBulletParts, { part = part, t0 = tick() })
end

function applyMagicBullet()
    if not State.MagicBullet then return end
    if not magicBulletHooked then
        magicBulletHooked = true
        pcall(function()
            Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if not State.MagicBullet then return end
                task.defer(function()
                    if not State.MagicBullet or not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
                    if isUnderCharOrTool(obj) then return end
                    pcall(function()
                        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                            magicTrackPart(obj)
                        elseif obj:IsA("Model") or obj:IsA("Folder") then
                            if isMagicProjectile(obj) then
                                for _, d in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if d:IsA("BasePart") then magicTrackPart(d) end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        if game.GameId ~= 6035872082 then return end
        if not _G.__magicLibTick or (tick() - _G.__magicLibTick) > 1 then
            _G.__magicLibTick = tick()
            local ok, Items = pcall(function()
                return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.ItemLibrary).Items
            end)
            if ok and type(Items) == "table" then
                for _, data in pairs(Items) do
                    if typeof(data) == "table" and (data.IsProjectile or data.ProjectileSpeed) then
                        pcall(function()
                            data.ProjectileSpeed = 12000
                            data.ProjectileLifetime = 9999
                            data.ProjectileGravity = 0
                            data.RaycastPierceCount = 9999
                            data.RaycastBounceCount = 0
                            if data.ShootSpread then data.ShootSpread = 0 end
                            if data.WallCheck then data.WallCheck = false end
                            if data.CanPierce ~= nil then data.CanPierce = true end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local target = magicGetTargetHrp()
    if not target then return end
    local tPos = target.Position
    local tVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local speed = tVel.Magnitude
    local now = tick()

    if not magicStopAccum then magicStopAccum = 0 end
    if speed < 2.5 then
        magicStopAccum = magicStopAccum + 0.016
    else
        magicStopAccum = 0
    end
    local targetStopped = magicStopAccum >= 0.12

    local tVel2 = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local pred = tPos + tVel2 * 0.06
    local headHover = pred + Vector3.new(0, 5.2, 0)
    local headHit = pred + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)

    local i = 1
    while i <= #magicBulletParts do
        local e = magicBulletParts[i]
        local p = e.part
        if not p or not p.Parent or not p:IsA("BasePart") or (now - e.t0) > 14 then
            table.remove(magicBulletParts, i)
        else
            if e.phase == nil then e.phase = "chase" end
            pcall(function()
                p.CanCollide = false
                p.CanTouch = true
                p.CanQuery = true
                p.Massless = true
                p.Anchored = false

                local aim
                if e.phase == "drop" or targetStopped then
                    e.phase = "drop"
                    aim = headHit
                else

                    aim = headHover
                    local distXZ = (Vector3.new(p.Position.X, 0, p.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(headHover.X, 0, headHover.Z)).Magnitude
                    local distY = math.abs(p.Position.Y - headHover.Y)
                    if distXZ < 3 and distY < 4 then
                        e.phase = "hover"
                    else
                        e.phase = "chase"
                    end
                end

                local dir = aim - p.Position
                local dist = dir.Magnitude
                if e.phase == "drop" then

                    if dist > 1.2 then
                        local step = math.min(dist, 80)
                        local nextPos = p.Position + dir.Unit * step
                        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(nextPos, aim)
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir.Unit * 9000
                    else

                        for _ = 1, 6 do
                            p.CFrame = CFrame.new(headHit)
                            p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -120, 0)
                            p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                        p.Size = Vector3.new(
                            math.max(p.Size.X, 4),
                            math.max(p.Size.Y, 4),
                            math.max(p.Size.Z, 4)
                        )
                    end
                elseif e.phase == "hover" then

                    local follow = headHover + tVel * 0.04
                    p.CFrame = CFrame.new(follow)
                    p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel
                    p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                else

                    if dist > 6 then
                        local step = math.min(dist, 500)
                        local nextPos = p.Position + dir.Unit * step
                        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(nextPos, aim)
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir.Unit * 14000
                    else
                        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(aim)
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel
                    end
                end

                if sethiddenproperty then
                    pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(p, "NetworkIsSleeping", false) end)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if p.SetNetworkOwner then p:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
                end)
            end)
            i = i + 1
        end
    end
end

function restoreMagicBullet()
    for i = #magicBulletParts, 1, -1 do
        table.remove(magicBulletParts, i)
    end
end

local magicMeleeLast = 0
local magicMeleeLibTick = 0
function applyMagicMelee()
    if not State.MagicMelee then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if not hrp then return end

    pcall(function()
        if game.GameId ~= 6035872082 then return end
        if tick() - magicMeleeLibTick < 0.5 then return end
        magicMeleeLibTick = tick()
        local ok, Items = pcall(function()
            return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.ItemLibrary).Items
        end)
        if not ok or type(Items) ~= "table" then return end
        for name, data in pairs(Items) do
            if typeof(data) == "table" then
                local ln = string.lower(tostring(name))
                local isMelee = data.IsMelee or data.Melee or data.AttackRange or data.MeleeRange
                    or ln:find("sword") or ln:find("knife") or ln:find("dagger") or ln:find("bat")
                    or ln:find("axe") or ln:find("katana") or ln:find("blade") or ln:find("fist")
                    or ln:find("melee") or ln:find("club") or ln:find("hammer") or ln:find("spear")
                if isMelee or data.SwingCooldown or data.MeleeCooldown or data.AttackCooldown then
                    for _, k in ipairs({
                        "AttackRange", "MeleeRange", "Range", "Reach", "HitRange",
                        "MaxRange", "WeaponRange", "SwingRange", "DamageRange"
                    }) do
                        pcall(function() data[k] = 1e9 end)
                    end
                    if data.IsProjectile then

                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local target = magicGetTargetHrp()

    local range = tonumber(State.MagicMeleeRange)
    if range == nil or range <= 0 then range = 1e9 end
    if not target then return end
    local dist = (hrp.Position - target.Position).Magnitude
    if dist > range then return end

    local aim = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.0, 0)
    pcall(function()
        local flat = Vector3.new(aim.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, aim.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + flat.Unit)
        end
    end)

    if tool then
        local tname = string.lower(tool.Name or "")
        local gunLike = tname:find("gun") or tname:find("rifle") or tname:find("pistol")
            or tname:find("shotgun") or tname:find("sniper") or tname:find("bow")
            or tname:find("sling") or tname:find("rpg") or tname:find("crossbow")
        if gunLike then return end

        pcall(function()
            local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
                handle.CanTouch = true
                handle.CanQuery = true
                handle.CanCollide = false
                handle.Massless = true
                if not handle:GetAttribute("MagicMeleeBase") then
                    handle:SetAttribute("MagicMeleeBase", true)
                    handle:SetAttribute("MMX", handle.Size.X)
                    handle:SetAttribute("MMY", handle.Size.Y)
                    handle:SetAttribute("MMZ", handle.Size.Z)
                end
                handle.Size = Vector3.new(12, 12, 12)

                handle.CFrame = CFrame.new(aim)
            end
            for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    d.CanTouch = true
                    d.CanQuery = true
                    d.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
            local now = tick()
            if now - magicMeleeLast > 0.03 then
                magicMeleeLast = now
                for _ = 1, 8 do
                    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function calculateTPCFrame(targetHrp, mode)
    local targetCF = targetHrp.CFrame
    local targetPos = targetCF.Position
    local tVel = targetHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity

    local pred = targetPos + tVel * 0.12
    local lookFlat = Vector3.new(targetCF.LookVector.X, 0, targetCF.LookVector.Z)
    if lookFlat.Magnitude < 0.01 then
        lookFlat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    else
        lookFlat = lookFlat.Unit
    end
    if mode == "Above" then
        return CFrame.new(pred + Vector3.new(0, 2.0, 0), pred)
    elseif mode == "Below" then
        local pos = pred - Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
        return CFrame.new(pos, pos + lookFlat)
    elseif mode == "Front" then
        return CFrame.new(pred + (lookFlat * 1.0) + Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0), pred)
    elseif mode == "Back" then
        return CFrame.new(pred - (lookFlat * 1.0) + Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0), pred)
    end

    return CFrame.new(pred, pred + lookFlat)
end

function applyAntiNGM()
    if not State.AntiNGM then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    pcall(function()
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hrp then
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            pcall(function()
                if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
            end)
        end
        if humanoid then
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            humanoid.Sit = false
            if humanoid.WalkSpeed < 16 then humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16 end
            if humanoid.JumpPower < 50 then humanoid.JumpPower = 50 end
            if humanoid.JumpHeight and humanoid.JumpHeight < 7.2 then humanoid.JumpHeight = 7.2 end
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, true)
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end

        for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                pcall(function()
                    tool.Enabled = true
                    if tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
                        tool.Handle.CanTouch = true
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if bp then
            for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                    pcall(function() tool.Enabled = true end)
                end
            end
        end

        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p.Character then
                for _, v in ipairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("ForceField") then
                        pcall(function() v:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function updateShortVoid(hrp)
    if not hrp then return end
    pcall(function()
        local dist = tonumber(State.ShortVoidDist) or 50
        if dist < 0.1 then dist = 0.1 end
        local base = hrp.Position

        local ox = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * dist
        local oy = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * dist
        local oz = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * dist
        local dest = Vector3.new(base.X + ox, base.Y + oy, base.Z + oz)
        local cf = CFrame.new(dest)

        for _ = 1, 6 do
            hrp.CFrame = cf
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

            ox = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * dist
            oy = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * dist
            oz = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * dist
            cf = CFrame.new(base.X + ox, base.Y + oy, base.Z + oz)
        end
    end)
end

function getUESyncTarget()
    local name = tostring(State.TargetName or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if name ~= "" then
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and (p.Name == name or p.DisplayName == name) then
                if p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        return p.Character, p
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local closest, closestPlr, minD = nil, nil, math.huge
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHrp then return nil, nil end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local th = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if th and hum and hum.Health > 0 then

                local enemy = true
                if type(isEnemy) == "function" then
                    enemy = isEnemy(p)
                end
                if enemy then
                    local d = (myHrp.Position - th.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < minD then
                        minD = d
                        closest = p.Character
                        closestPlr = p
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest, closestPlr
end

function updateUESyncKill(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not State.UESyncKill then return end
    if not hrp or not char then return end
    pcall(function()
        local targetChar = select(1, getUESyncTarget())
        if not targetChar then return end
        local tHrp = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local tHum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not tHrp or not tHum or tHum.Health <= 0 then return end

        local tPos = tHrp.Position
        local under = false
        if type(isUnderOffsetY) == "function" then
            under = isUnderOffsetY(tPos.Y)
        elseif type(isUEUnderOffsetY) == "function" then
            under = isUEUnderOffsetY(tPos.Y)
        else
            local ay = math.abs(tPos.Y)
            under = (ay >= 1004000 and ay <= 1004099) or (ay >= 1000000 and ay <= 1005000)
        end

        local attachCF = tHrp.CFrame
        if under then
            attachCF = CFrame.new(tPos + Vector3.new(0, 0.15, 0))
        else
            attachCF = tHrp.CFrame
        end

        pcall(function()
            if sethiddenproperty then
                pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false) end)
            end
            if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then
                pcall(function() hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end)
            end
        end)

        for _ = 1, 12 do
            hrp.CFrame = attachCF
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if tool then
            for _ = 1, 8 do
                pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
            end
        end
        if State.FastFist and type(applyNoCD) == "function" then
            pcall(function() applyNoCD(true) end)
        end
        if type(remoteFastAttack) == "function" then
            pcall(function() remoteFastAttack(tool) end)
        end
    end)
end

function scanKillBricks()
    killScanned = true
    killParts = {}
    pcall(function()
        for _, inst in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
                local n = string.lower(inst.Name)
                local ok = false
                if string.find(n, "kill", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "void", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "death", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "lava", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "die", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "toxic", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "barrier", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "outofbounds", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "oob", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "fall", 1, true) then
                    ok = true
                end
                if not ok and inst.Position.Y < -30 and inst.Size.X >= 10 and inst.Size.Z >= 10 then
                    ok = true
                end
                if ok then
                    table.insert(killParts, inst)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    print("[chanhyuk] kill bricks:", #killParts)
end

local baitCenter = nil
local baitWander = Vector3.zero
local baitPartsCached = false

function baitSafeY(part)

    local top = part.Position.Y + math.abs(part.Size.Y) * 0.5
    return top + 0.35
end

local solixLastEnemyDist = nil
local solixLastEnemyPos = nil
local solixLastEnemyVel = nil
local solixEvadeUntil = 0
local solixPhase = 0
local solixOrigin = nil

function updateSolixCounter(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not State.SolixCounter or not hrp then
        solixLastEnemyDist = nil
        solixLastEnemyPos = nil
        solixLastEnemyVel = nil
        solixOrigin = nil
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
        if not tChar then return end
        local tHrp = tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local tHum = tChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not tHrp or not tHum or tHum.Health <= 0 then return end

        local myPos = hrp.Position
        local tPos = tHrp.Position
        local dist = (myPos - tPos).Magnitude
        local tVel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local speed = tVel.Magnitude
        local range = tonumber(State.SolixCounterRange) or 80
        if range < 30 then range = 30 end
        if dist > range then return end

        local now = tick()
        solixPhase = (solixPhase + 1) % 6

        if not solixOrigin or (Vector3.new(myPos.X, 0, myPos.Z) - Vector3.new(solixOrigin.X, 0, solixOrigin.Z)).Magnitude > 160 then
            solixOrigin = Vector3.new(myPos.X, myPos.Y, myPos.Z)
        end

        local toMe = myPos - tPos
        local flatAway = Vector3.new(toMe.X, 0, toMe.Z)
        if flatAway.Magnitude < 0.08 then
            flatAway = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
        else
            flatAway = flatAway.Unit
        end
        local look = tHrp.CFrame.LookVector
        local lookFlat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if lookFlat.Magnitude < 0.05 then lookFlat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else lookFlat = lookFlat.Unit end
        local side = Vector3.new(-lookFlat.Z, 0, lookFlat.X)
        local sideSign = (math.floor(now * 31 + solixPhase) % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1

        local dest
        if solixPhase == 0 then

            local anti = Vector3.zero
            if speed > 2 then
                local fv = Vector3.new(tVel.X, 0, tVel.Z)
                if fv.Magnitude > 0.1 then anti = -fv.Unit * (30 + dist * 0.2) end
            end
            dest = myPos + anti + side * sideSign * (22 + math.random() * 16) + flatAway * (18 + math.random() * 12)
            dest = dest + Vector3.new(0, 8 + math.sin(now * 47) * 10, 0)
        elseif solixPhase == 1 then

            local push = (dist < 25) and (flatAway * (28 + math.random() * 16)) or (flatAway * -(12 + math.random() * 8))
            dest = myPos + push + side * sideSign * (20 + math.random() * 14)
            dest = dest + Vector3.new(0, 14 + math.abs(math.sin(now * 33)) * 14, 0)
        elseif solixPhase == 2 then

            local y = myPos.Y + (30 + math.sin(now * 53) * 18 + math.cos(now * 23) * 12)
            dest = Vector3.new(myPos.X + side.X * sideSign * (12 + math.random() * 10), y, myPos.Z + side.Z * sideSign * (12 + math.random() * 10))
        elseif solixPhase == 3 then

            local ang = now * 37 + solixPhase * 3.1
            local rad = 26 + math.sin(now * 15) * 12
            dest = Vector3.new(tPos.X + math.cos(ang) * rad, tPos.Y + 12 + math.sin(now * 43) * 12, tPos.Z + math.sin(ang) * rad)
        elseif solixPhase == 4 then

            dest = myPos - flatAway * (26 + math.random() * 16) + side * sideSign * (20 + math.random() * 14)
            dest = dest + Vector3.new(0, 12 + math.random() * 12, 0)
        else

            local ang = now * 41
            dest = tPos + Vector3.new(math.cos(ang) * 30, 18 + math.abs(math.sin(now * 29)) * 16, math.sin(ang) * 30)
        end

        if dest.Y > 1003900 and dest.Y < 1004200 then
            dest = Vector3.new(dest.X, dest.Y + 250, dest.Z)
        end
        if dest.Y < -150 then
            dest = Vector3.new(dest.X, math.max(myPos.Y, 8) + 12, dest.Z)
        end
        if solixOrigin then
            local flatD = Vector3.new(dest.X - solixOrigin.X, 0, dest.Z - solixOrigin.Z)
            if flatD.Magnitude > 200 then
                dest = Vector3.new(solixOrigin.X + flatD.Unit.X * 150, dest.Y, solixOrigin.Z + flatD.Unit.Z * 150)
            end
        end

        solixLastEnemyDist = dist
        solixLastEnemyPos = tPos
        solixLastEnemyVel = tVel

        local cf = CFrame.new(dest)
        for _ = 1, 26 do
            hrp.CFrame = cf
            if speed > 2 then
                local fv = Vector3.new(tVel.X, 0, tVel.Z)
                if fv.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = -fv.Unit * 85
                else
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            else
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.Anchored = false
    end)
end

local kiciaPhase = "move"
local kiciaPhaseUntil = 0
local kiciaHomeXZ = nil
local kiciaWanderIdx = 0

function updateKiciaCounter(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not State.KiciaCounter or not hrp then
        kiciaPhase = "move"
        kiciaPhaseUntil = 0
        kiciaHomeXZ = nil
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local now = tick()
        local myPos = hrp.Position
        if not kiciaHomeXZ then
            kiciaHomeXZ = Vector3.new(myPos.X, 0, myPos.Z)
        end

        local yMin = tonumber(State.KiciaYMin) or 613751818746169
        local yMax = tonumber(State.KiciaYMax) or 999999999999999
        if yMax < yMin then
            yMin, yMax = yMax, yMin
        end
        local moveTime = tonumber(State.KiciaMoveTime) or 1.0
        if moveTime < 0.05 then moveTime = 0.05 end
        local stopTime = tonumber(State.KiciaStopTime) or 0.35
        if stopTime < 0 then stopTime = 0 end

        if now >= (kiciaPhaseUntil or 0) then
            if kiciaPhase == "move" and stopTime > 0 then
                kiciaPhase = "stop"
                kiciaPhaseUntil = now + stopTime
            else
                kiciaPhase = "move"
                kiciaPhaseUntil = now + moveTime
            end
        end

        if kiciaPhase == "stop" then

            local holdY = myPos.Y
            if holdY < yMin or holdY > yMax then
                holdY = yMin + (yMax - yMin) * 0.5
            end
            local cf = CFrame.new(myPos.X, holdY, myPos.Z)
            for _ = 1, 22 do
                hrp.CFrame = cf
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            hrp.Anchored = false
            return
        end

        kiciaWanderIdx = (kiciaWanderIdx or 0) + 1
        local t = now * 55 + kiciaWanderIdx * 0.7
        local ySpan = yMax - yMin

        local yWave = (math.sin(t * 1.7) * 0.5 + 0.5)
        local yWave2 = (math.cos(t * 2.3) * 0.5 + 0.5)
        local y = yMin + ySpan * (0.15 + 0.7 * yWave)

        if kiciaWanderIdx % 7 == 0 then
            y = yMin + ySpan * (kiciaWanderIdx % 2)
        elseif kiciaWanderIdx % 5 == 0 then
            y = yMin + ySpan * yWave2
        end
        if y < yMin then y = yMin end
        if y > yMax then y = yMax end

        local rad = 40 + (kiciaWanderIdx % 11) * 18
        local ang = t * 3.1
        local ox = math.cos(ang) * rad + math.sin(t * 8) * 25
        local oz = math.sin(ang) * rad + math.cos(t * 7) * 25
        local pos = Vector3.new(kiciaHomeXZ.X + ox, y, kiciaHomeXZ.Z + oz)

        local cf = CFrame.new(pos)
        for _ = 1, 30 do
            hrp.CFrame = cf
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        hrp.Anchored = false
    end)
end

function applyAimbot()
    if not State.Aimbot then return end
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
    if not tChar then return end
    local partName = tostring(State.AimbotPart or "Head")
    local part = tChar:FindFirstChild(partName) or tChar:FindFirstChild("Head") or tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not part then return end
    local tHrp = tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local aimPos = part.Position
    if tHrp then
        local vel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        aimPos = part.Position + vel * 0.035
    end
    local fov = tonumber(State.AimbotFOV) or 250
    local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPos)
    local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local md = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - Vector2.new(mouse.X, mouse.Y)).Magnitude
    if fov > 0 then
        if (not onScreen) and sp.Z < 0 then return end
        if onScreen and md > fov then return end
    end

    local smooth = tonumber(State.AimbotSmooth) or 0.12
    if smooth < 0.02 then smooth = 0.02 end
    if smooth > 0.5 then smooth = 0.5 end

    local distFactor = math.clamp(md / math.max(fov, 1), 0, 1)
    local alpha = smooth * (0.35 + distFactor * 0.65)
    local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
    local goal = CFrame.new(camPos, aimPos)
    cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(goal, alpha)
end

local kiciaStickIdx = 0
function updateKiciaStickCounter(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not State.KiciaStickCounter or not hrp then return end
    pcall(function()
        local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
        if not targetChar then return end
        local tHrp = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not tHrp then return end
        local dist = (hrp.Position - tHrp.Position).Magnitude
        local range = tonumber(State.KiciaStickRange) or 12

        if dist > range then return end

        kiciaStickIdx = (kiciaStickIdx or 0) + 1
        local now = tick()
        local tpos = tHrp.Position
        local tlook = tHrp.CFrame.LookVector

        local phase = kiciaStickIdx % 6
        local offset
        if phase == 0 then
            offset = Vector3.new(0, 18 + (kiciaStickIdx % 5), 0)
        elseif phase == 1 then
            offset = -tlook * (22 + (kiciaStickIdx % 7)) + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
        elseif phase == 2 then
            offset = tHrp.CFrame.RightVector * (20 + (kiciaStickIdx % 9)) + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)
        elseif phase == 3 then
            offset = -tHrp.CFrame.RightVector * (20 + (kiciaStickIdx % 8)) + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
        elseif phase == 4 then
            offset = tlook * 15 + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)
        else
            local ang = now * 40
            offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(ang) * 28, 14, math.sin(ang) * 28)
        end
        local pos = tpos + offset
        local cf = CFrame.new(pos, tpos)
        hrp.Anchored = false
        for _ = 1, 18 do
            hrp.CFrame = cf
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end

pcall(function()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ChanAimbot")
end)

pcall(function()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ChanDongkoDesync")
end)
RunService:BindToRenderStep("ChanDongkoDesync", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
    if not State.Dongko or not dongkoClientCF then return end
    local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end

    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam and cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then

        end
    end)
end)

RunService:BindToRenderStep("ChanAimbot", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value, function()
    if State.Aimbot then
        pcall(applyAimbot)
    end
end)

function applyPerfectSpam(hrp)
    if not State.PerfectSpam or not hrp then return end

    perfectSpamPhase = (perfectSpamPhase + 1) % 12
    local now = tick()
    local ang = now * 55 + perfectSpamPhase * 1.4
    local rad = 600 + (perfectSpamPhase % 6) * 400
    local base = hrp.Position
    local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
    if tChar then
        local th = tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if th then base = th.Position end
    end
    local dest = Vector3.new(
        base.X + math.cos(ang) * rad * (25 + perfectSpamPhase * 3),
        base.Y + 200 + math.abs(math.sin(now * 31)) * 800 + perfectSpamPhase * 120,
        base.Z + math.sin(ang) * rad * (25 + perfectSpamPhase * 3)
    )

    if dest.Y > 1003900 and dest.Y < 1004200 then
        dest = Vector3.new(dest.X, dest.Y + 500, dest.Z)
    end
    local cf = CFrame.new(dest)
    for _ = 1, 32 do
        hrp.CFrame = cf
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    hrp.Anchored = false
end

local gngPhase = 0
local gngAnchor = nil
function updateGNG(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not State.GNG or not hrp then
        gngAnchor = nil
        _G.__gngUecLastTp = nil
        _G.__gngUecHoldCF = nil
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local mode = tostring(State.GNGMode or "UE")
        local now = tick()
        gngPhase = (gngPhase + 1) % 8
        local myPos = hrp.Position
        local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
        local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local tPos = tHrp and tHrp.Position or myPos
        local tVel = tHrp and tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero

        if mode == "UE" then
            if not _G.__gngUecLastTp then _G.__gngUecLastTp = 0 end
            local delay = tonumber(State.UECTPDelay) or 0.05
            if delay < 0.01 then delay = 0.01 end
            local yDist = tonumber(State.UECYDistance) or 500
            if yDist < 1 then yDist = 1 end
            if yDist > 1000 then yDist = 1000 end
            local baseX, baseZ = myPos.X, myPos.Z
            if tHrp then
                baseX = tHrp.Position.X
                baseZ = tHrp.Position.Z
            end
            local prevY = 50
            if _G.__gngUecHoldCF then
                prevY = _G.__gngUecHoldCF.Y
            else
                if tHrp then
                    local ty = tHrp.Position.Y
                    if ty > -500 and ty < 5000 then prevY = ty end
                elseif math.abs(myPos.Y) < 5000 then
                    prevY = myPos.Y
                end
            end
            if (now - _G.__gngUecLastTp) >= delay then
                _G.__gngUecLastTp = now
                local fromY = hrp.Position.Y
                if fromY < -500 or fromY > 1e12 then
                    fromY = prevY
                end
                local oy = fromY + yDist
                if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then
                    oy = fromY + yDist + 50
                end
                local cf = CFrame.new(baseX, oy, baseZ)
                _G.__gngUecHoldCF = cf
                for _ = 1, 16 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end

            hrp.Anchored = false
            return
        end

        if not gngAnchor or (Vector3.new(myPos.X,0,myPos.Z) - Vector3.new(gngAnchor.X,0,gngAnchor.Z)).Magnitude > 150 then
            if math.abs(myPos.Y) < 5000 then
                gngAnchor = Vector3.new(myPos.X, myPos.Y, myPos.Z)
            else
                gngAnchor = Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
            end
        end

        local dest
        if mode == "Solix" then

            local toMe = myPos - tPos
            local flat = Vector3.new(toMe.X, 0, toMe.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude < 0.1 then flat = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) else flat = flat.Unit end
            local side = Vector3.new(-flat.Z, 0, flat.X)
            local sign = (math.floor(now * 31 + gngPhase) % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1
            local anti = Vector3.zero
            if tVel.Magnitude > 1.0 then
                local fv = Vector3.new(tVel.X, 0, tVel.Z)
                if fv.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    anti = -fv.Unit * (32 + gngPhase * 6)
                end
            end
            local phase = gngPhase % 5
            if phase == 0 then
                dest = myPos + anti + side * sign * (24 + math.random() * 16) + flat * (14 + math.random() * 10)
                dest = dest + Vector3.new(0, 10 + math.sin(now * 47) * 12, 0)
            elseif phase == 1 then
                dest = tPos + side * sign * (36 + math.random() * 18) - flat * 24
                dest = dest + Vector3.new(0, 24 + math.random() * 20, 0)
            elseif phase == 2 then
                local y = myPos.Y + (28 + math.sin(now * 53) * 16)
                dest = Vector3.new(myPos.X + side.X * sign * 14, y, myPos.Z + side.Z * sign * 14)
            elseif phase == 3 then
                local ang = now * 41
                dest = Vector3.new(tPos.X + math.cos(ang) * 32, tPos.Y + 16 + math.abs(math.sin(now * 29)) * 18, tPos.Z + math.sin(ang) * 32)
            else
                dest = myPos - flat * (28 + math.random() * 16) + side * sign * (22 + math.random() * 14)
                dest = dest + Vector3.new(0, 14 + math.random() * 12, 0)
            end
        elseif mode == "Kicia" then

            local tLook = tHrp and tHrp.CFrame.LookVector or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)
            local tLookFlat = Vector3.new(tLook.X, 0, tLook.Z)
            if tLookFlat.Magnitude < 0.05 then tLookFlat = Vector3.new(0,0,-1) else tLookFlat = tLookFlat.Unit end
            local side = Vector3.new(-tLookFlat.Z, 0, tLookFlat.X)
            local sign = (math.floor(now * 11) % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1
            local dist = (myPos - tPos).Magnitude
            if dist < 8 then

                dest = tPos + side * sign * (16 + math.random() * 10)
                    + tLookFlat * (-6)
                    + Vector3.new(0, 10 + math.random() * 8, 0)
            elseif gngPhase % 4 == 0 then

                local ang = now * 15 + gngPhase
                local rad = 18 + math.sin(now * 7) * 8
                dest = Vector3.new(
                    tPos.X + math.cos(ang) * rad,
                    tPos.Y + 10 + math.abs(math.sin(now * 25)) * 12,
                    tPos.Z + math.sin(ang) * rad
                )
            elseif gngPhase % 4 == 1 then

                dest = tPos + side * sign * (12 + math.random() * 8) + tLookFlat * 5
                dest = Vector3.new(dest.X, tPos.Y + 5 + math.sin(now * 19) * 3, dest.Z)
            else

                local ang = now * 12 + gngPhase * 0.9
                local rad = 20 + math.sin(now * 6) * 8
                dest = Vector3.new(
                    tPos.X + math.cos(ang) * rad,
                    tPos.Y + 12 + math.abs(math.sin(now * 21)) * 14,
                    tPos.Z + math.sin(ang) * rad
                )
            end
            if (dest - tPos).Magnitude < 10 then
                dest = tPos + Vector3.new(math.cos(now * 9) * 22, 14, math.sin(now * 9) * 22)
            end
        else

            dest = Vector3.new(myPos.X, myPos.Y + 50, myPos.Z)
        end

        if dest.Y > 1003900 and dest.Y < 1004200 then
            dest = Vector3.new(dest.X, dest.Y + 250, dest.Z)
        end
        if dest.Y < -100 then
            dest = Vector3.new(dest.X, 40, dest.Z)
        end

        local cf = CFrame.new(dest)
        local loops = (mode == "Kicia" or mode == "Solix") and 28 or 14
        for _ = 1, loops do
            hrp.CFrame = cf
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        hrp.Anchored = false
    end)
end

function updateBaiting(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not State.Baiting then
        baitCF = nil
        baitCenter = nil
        baitWander = Vector3.zero
        if baitPartsCached then
            pcall(function()
                if hrp then
                    hrp.CanCollide = true
                    hrp.CanTouch = true
                    hrp.Anchored = false
                end
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
                    pcall(function()
                        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
                        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
                        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    end)
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(char and char:GetDescendants() or {}) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        part.CanCollide = true
                        part.CanTouch = true
                    end
                end
            end)
            baitPartsCached = false
        end
        return
    end
    baitPartsCached = true
    if #killParts == 0 then
        killScanned = false
        pcall(scanKillBricks)
    end
    if #killParts == 0 then return end

    local best = nil
    if baitCenter and baitCenter.Parent then
        best = baitCenter
    else
        local bestDist = math.huge
        for _, part in ipairs(killParts) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                local d = (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bestDist then
                    bestDist = d
                    best = part
                end
            end
        end
        baitCenter = best
    end
    if not best then return end

    local t = tick()
    local halfX = math.max(best.Size.X * 0.5, 2)
    local halfZ = math.max(best.Size.Z * 0.5, 2)
    local rMax = math.clamp(math.min(halfX, halfZ) * 0.9, 1.0, 2.5)
    local r = 1.0 + math.abs(math.sin(t * 17.3)) * (rMax - 1.0)
    local ang = t * 21
        + math.sin(t * 9.1) * 2.8
        + math.cos(t * 14.7) * 2.1
        + math.sin(t * 31.0) * 1.4
    local bx = best.Position.X + math.cos(ang) * r
    local bz = best.Position.Z + math.sin(ang) * r
    local topY = best.Position.Y + math.abs(best.Size.Y) * 0.5

    local by = topY + 0.55 + math.sin(t * 29) * 0.25
    baitCF = CFrame.new(bx, by, bz)

    pcall(function()
        for _ = 1, 40 do
            hrp.CFrame = baitCF
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        hrp.Anchored = false

        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.CanQuery = false
                if part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    part.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
        end
        hrp.CanCollide = false
        hrp.CanTouch = false
        if humanoid then
            humanoid.PlatformStand = true
            humanoid.Sit = false
            humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
            humanoid.JumpPower = 0
            pcall(function()
                humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
                humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
                humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
                humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, false)
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
            end)

            if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
                humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
            end
            if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
            end
        end

        if hrp.Position.Y < topY - 1 then
            hrp.CFrame = baitCF
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end

function updateHitboxEdit(char)
    if not State or not State.HitboxEdit or not char then return end
    pcall(function()
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local s = tonumber(State.HitboxEditSize) or 2
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                part.Size = Vector3.new(s, s, s)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local antiAimOriginal = {}
function applyAntiAim(char)
    return
end

local lastAntiKillHealth = nil
function applyAntiKillbrick(char, humanoid)
    if not State.AntiKillbrick then
        lastAntiKillHealth = nil
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
        if humanoid then
            humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            if lastAntiKillHealth == nil then
                lastAntiKillHealth = humanoid.Health
            end
            if humanoid.Health < lastAntiKillHealth then
                humanoid.Health = lastAntiKillHealth
            else
                lastAntiKillHealth = humanoid.Health
            end
            if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if #killParts == 0 then
            killScanned = false
            scanKillBricks()
        end
        for _, part in ipairs(killParts) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.CanQuery = false
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp and hrp.Position.Y < -100 then
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 150, 0)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end

function cleanupFly(hrp, humanoid)
    if flyBV then pcall(function() flyBV:Destroy() end) flyBV = nil end
    if flyBG then pcall(function() flyBG:Destroy() end) flyBG = nil end
    if hrp then
        pcall(function()
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end)
    end
    flyPlatformSet = false
end

function doFly(hrp, humanoid)
    if not State.Fly then
        if flyPlatformSet or flyBV or flyBG then
            cleanupFly(hrp, humanoid)
        end
        return
    end
    if State.TP or State.Underground or State.Baiting or State.HitBehind then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        hrp.Anchored = false
        humanoid.PlatformStand = true
        humanoid.Sit = false
        humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.max(humanoid.WalkSpeed, 16)
        humanoid.JumpPower = math.max(humanoid.JumpPower, 50)
        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
        for _, obj in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("BodyPosition") or obj:IsA("BodyGyro") or obj:IsA("BodyVelocity") then
                if obj.Name ~= "ChanhyukFlyBV" and obj.Name ~= "ChanhyukFlyBG" then
                    obj:Destroy()
                end
            end
            if obj:IsA("AlignPosition") or obj:IsA("AlignOrientation") or obj:IsA("LinearVelocity") or obj:IsA("VectorForce") then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
    flyPlatformSet = true

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local speed = tonumber(State.FlySpeed) or 50
    local moveDir = Vector3.zero

    if humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 then
        local joystickDir = humanoid.MoveDirection
        local camFlatForward = (cam.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1))
        if camFlatForward.Magnitude > 0.001 then camFlatForward = camFlatForward.Unit end
        local camFlatRight = (cam.CFrame.RightVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1))
        if camFlatRight.Magnitude > 0.001 then camFlatRight = camFlatRight.Unit end
        local forwardAmount = joystickDir:Dot(camFlatForward)
        local rightAmount = joystickDir:Dot(camFlatRight)
        moveDir = cam.CFrame.LookVector * forwardAmount + cam.CFrame.RightVector * rightAmount
    else
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then moveDir = moveDir + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then moveDir = moveDir - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then moveDir = moveDir - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then moveDir = moveDir + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then moveDir = moveDir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then moveDir = moveDir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
    end

    if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
        local vel = moveDir.Unit * speed
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel
        hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + vel * 0.03
    else
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    local pos = hrp.Position
    local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
    local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then
        flat = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
    end
    if flat.Magnitude > 0.05 then
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, pos + flat.Unit)
    else
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, hrp.CFrame.Rotation:ToEulerAnglesYXZ())
    end
end

    task.wait(2)
    scanned = false
    scanWeapons()
    if #weaponTables < 2 then
        task.wait(2)
        scanned = false
        scanWeapons()
    end
end)
task.spawn(function() pcall(scanKillBricks) end)

for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    checkAndActionPlayer(p)
end

local wallAngleParams = RaycastParams.new()
wallAngleParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local wallAngleLastCF = nil
local wallAngleCandidate = nil
local wallAngleSwitchAt = 0
local wallAngleIdx = 0

local function wallAngleGetEnemy()
    local name = tostring(State.TargetName or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if name ~= "" then
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and (p.Name == name or p.DisplayName == name) then
                local h = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hu = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if h and hu and hu.Health > 0 then return h, p.Character end
            end
        end
    end
    local my = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not my then return nil, nil end
    local best, bestD, bestC = nil, 1e9, nil
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hu = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h and hu and hu.Health > 0 then
                local d = (my.Position - h.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bestD then bestD, best, bestC = d, h, p.Character end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bestC
end

local wallAngleCached = nil
local wallAngleNextScan = 0
local wallAngleNoClipOn = false
local wallAngleModeKey = ""
local switchSpamNext = 0
local switchSpamPool = {}
local wallAnglePoolIdx = 0
local wallAngleSlideT = 0

local function wallIsKillZone(pos)
    if not pos then return true end
    if pos.Y ~= pos.Y then return true end
    if pos.Y < -80 then return true end
    if type(killParts) == "table" then
        for _, part in ipairs(killParts) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                local n = string.lower(part.Name)
                local lethal = string.find(n, "kill", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "void", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "death", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "lava", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "outofbounds", 1, true)
                    or string.find(n, "oob", 1, true)
                if lethal or part.Position.Y <= -30 then
                    local pp = part.Position
                    local half = part.Size * 0.5
                    if math.abs(pos.X - pp.X) <= half.X + 2
                        and math.abs(pos.Z - pp.Z) <= half.Z + 2
                        and pos.Y <= pp.Y + half.Y + 3
                        and pos.Y >= pp.Y - half.Y - 2 then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function wallSafeLift(pos)
    if not wallIsKillZone(pos) then return pos end
    for _, up in ipairs({8, 16, 28, 45}) do
        local p = pos + Vector3.new(0, up, 0)
        if not wallIsKillZone(p) then return p end
    end
    return pos + Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
end

function updateWallAngle(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not hrp then return end
    local wa = State.WallAngle and true or false
    local wt = State.WallTarget and true or false
    local spam = State.SwitchSpamWall and true or false
    if not (wa or wt or spam) then return end

    pcall(function()
        local now = tick()
        local enemyHrp = wallAngleGetEnemy()
        local origin = hrp.Position
        local wantNear = wt
        local nearRange = tonumber(State.WallTargetRange) or 14
        local depth = tonumber(State.WallAngleDepth) or 4.5
        if depth < 0.5 then depth = 0.5 end

        local modeKey = tostring(wa) .. tostring(wt) .. tostring(spam)
        if modeKey ~= wallAngleModeKey then
            wallAngleModeKey = modeKey
            wallAngleCached = nil
            wallAngleNextScan = 0
            switchSpamPool = {}
            wallAnglePoolIdx = 0
        end

        local switchT = tonumber(State.WallAngleSwitch) or 0.22
        if switchT < 0.08 then switchT = 0.08 end
        if spam then
            switchT = tonumber(State.SwitchSpamDelay) or 0.35
            if switchT < 0.08 then switchT = 0.08 end
        end
        local scanInterval = math.max(0.2, switchT)

        local needScan = (not wallAngleCached)
            or (now >= wallAngleNextScan)
            or (#switchSpamPool == 0)
        if needScan then
            wallAngleNextScan = now + scanInterval
            wallAngleParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}

            local rayList = {}
            local dirsSelf = {
                Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),
                Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
                Vector3.new(0.7, 0.15, 0.7).Unit, Vector3.new(-0.7, 0.15, -0.7).Unit,
                Vector3.new(0.7, 0.15, -0.7).Unit, Vector3.new(-0.7, 0.15, 0.7).Unit,
                Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 1).Unit, Vector3.new(0, 0.4, -1).Unit,
                Vector3.new(1, 0.3, 0).Unit, Vector3.new(-1, 0.3, 0).Unit,
            }
            for _, d in ipairs(dirsSelf) do
                table.insert(rayList, { origin, d })

                table.insert(rayList, { origin + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), d })
            end
            if enemyHrp then
                local epos = enemyHrp.Position
                local pred = epos + enemyHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity * 0.1
                local eLook = enemyHrp.CFrame.LookVector
                local flat = Vector3.new(eLook.X, 0, eLook.Z)
                if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else flat = flat.Unit end
                local side = flat:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
                if side.Magnitude < 0.05 then side = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) else side = side.Unit end
                local back = -flat
                local origins = wantNear
                    and { pred + back * 2, pred + side * 3, pred - side * 3, origin }
                    or { origin + back * 6, origin + side * 8, origin - side * 8, origin }
                for _, so in ipairs(origins) do
                    for _, d in ipairs({ back, side, -side, flat }) do
                        table.insert(rayList, { so, d })
                    end
                end
            end

            local pool = {}
            local used = 0
            for _, rd in ipairs(rayList) do
                if used >= 18 then break end
                used = used + 1
                local res = Workspace:Raycast(rd[1] + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0), rd[2] * 55, wallAngleParams)
                if res and res.Instance and res.Instance.CanCollide then
                    local n = res.Normal

                    for _, yOff in ipairs({0.5, 2.0, -0.8}) do
                        local inside = res.Position - n * depth + Vector3.new(0, yOff, 0)
                        inside = wallSafeLift(inside)
                        if not wallIsKillZone(inside) then
                            local score = 40 - (inside - origin).Magnitude * 0.06

                            if wallAngleCached and (inside - wallAngleCached.pos).Magnitude < 2.5 then
                                score = score - 35
                            end
                            if enemyHrp then
                                local epos = enemyHrp.Position
                                local toMe = inside - epos
                                local distE = toMe.Magnitude
                                if wantNear then
                                    if distE <= nearRange then score = score + (nearRange - distE) * 8
                                    else score = score - (distE - nearRange) * 5 end
                                else
                                    if distE > 18 then score = score + 20
                                    elseif distE < 10 then score = score - 12 end
                                end
                                if toMe.Magnitude > 0.5 then
                                    local eLook = enemyHrp.CFrame.LookVector
                                    local flatTo = Vector3.new(toMe.X, 0, toMe.Z)
                                    local flatLook = Vector3.new(eLook.X, 0, eLook.Z)
                                    if flatTo.Magnitude > 0.1 and flatLook.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                                        local dot = flatLook.Unit:Dot(flatTo.Unit)
                                        score = score - dot * 85
                                        if dot < -0.5 then score = score + 50 end
                                    end
                                end
                                if used < 20 then
                                    used = used + 1
                                    local los = Workspace:Raycast(epos + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), inside - epos, wallAngleParams)
                                    if los and los.Instance and los.Instance.CanCollide then
                                        score = score + 110
                                    else
                                        score = score - 35
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            table.insert(pool, {
                                pos = inside,
                                n = n,
                                hit = res.Position,
                                score = score,
                                tangent = n:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)),
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            table.sort(pool, function(a, b) return a.score > b.score end)

            local filtered = {}
            for _, cand in ipairs(pool) do
                local far = true
                for _, keep in ipairs(filtered) do
                    if (keep.pos - cand.pos).Magnitude < 3.5 then
                        far = false
                        break
                    end
                end
                if far then
                    table.insert(filtered, cand)
                    if #filtered >= 10 then break end
                end
            end
            if #filtered == 0 then filtered = pool end
            switchSpamPool = filtered

            if #filtered > 0 then

                wallAnglePoolIdx = (wallAnglePoolIdx % #filtered) + 1
                wallAngleCached = filtered[wallAnglePoolIdx]
            else
                local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
                local res = Workspace:Raycast(origin + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), look * 45, wallAngleParams)
                    or Workspace:Raycast(origin + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), -look * 45, wallAngleParams)
                    or Workspace:Raycast(origin + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), hrp.CFrame.RightVector * 45, wallAngleParams)
                if res and res.Instance then
                    local n = res.Normal
                    wallAngleCached = {
                        pos = wallSafeLift(res.Position - n * depth),
                        n = n,
                        hit = res.Position,
                        score = 1,
                        tangent = n:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)),
                    }
                    switchSpamPool = { wallAngleCached }
                end
            end
        else

            if #switchSpamPool >= 2 and now >= (switchSpamNext or 0) then
                local stepT = spam and (tonumber(State.SwitchSpamDelay) or 0.35) or (tonumber(State.WallAngleSwitch) or 0.22)
                if stepT < 0.08 then stepT = 0.08 end
                switchSpamNext = now + stepT * 0.55
                wallAnglePoolIdx = (wallAnglePoolIdx % #switchSpamPool) + 1
                wallAngleCached = switchSpamPool[wallAnglePoolIdx]
            end
        end

        local pick = wallAngleCached
        if not pick then return end
        if wallIsKillZone(pick.pos) then
            pick.pos = wallSafeLift(pick.pos)
        end

        if not wallAngleNoClipOn then
            wallAngleNoClipOn = true
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
            end
        end

        local tilt = math.rad(tonumber(State.WallAngleTilt) or 95)
        local n = pick.n or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        local up = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        local right = n:Cross(up)
        if right.Magnitude < 0.05 then right = n:Cross(Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)) end
        if right.Magnitude > 0.05 then right = right.Unit else right = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end

        wallAngleSlideT = wallAngleSlideT + 0.05
        local tangent = pick.tangent
        if not tangent or tangent.Magnitude < 0.05 then
            tangent = right
        else
            tangent = tangent.Unit
        end
        local slide = tangent * math.sin(wallAngleSlideT * 2.2) * 4.5
            + up * math.sin(wallAngleSlideT * 1.7) * 2.2
            + right * math.cos(wallAngleSlideT * 1.9) * 2.8
        local spin = now * 2.8
        local angledLook = (n * math.cos(tilt) + right * math.sin(tilt + spin) + up * 0.35).Unit
        local j = Vector3.new(math.sin(now * 48) * 0.9, math.cos(now * 41) * 0.7, math.sin(now * 36) * 0.9)
        local pos = pick.pos + slide + j

        pos = pos - n * 0.15
        if wallIsKillZone(pos) then pos = wallSafeLift(pick.pos) end
        local cf = CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + angledLook)

        if State.WallAngleDesync ~= false then
            desyncVisualPos = (pick.hit or pos) + n * 2 + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            desyncActive = true
        end

        hrp.CFrame = cf
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        wallAngleLastCF = cf
    end)
end

function stopWallAngle(char)
    pcall(function()
        if not char then char = LocalPlayer.Character end
        if not char then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                part.CanCollide = true
            end
        end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        wallAngleLastCF = nil
        wallAngleCandidate = nil
        wallAngleCached = nil
        wallAngleNoClipOn = false
        wallAngleNextScan = 0
        desyncActive = false
        desyncVisualPos = nil
    end)
end

local rbLastFire = 0
local rbDeflect = {}
local rbUtil, rbEnum, rbFC, rbSpectate = nil, nil, nil, nil
local rbEquipAt = 0
local RB_SLOT = { Primary = 1, Secondary = 2, Melee = 3 }

local function rbLoadMods()
    pcall(function()
        if not rbUtil then rbUtil = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Utility) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not rbEnum then rbEnum = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.EnumLibrary) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not rbFC then
            rbFC = require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.FighterController)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not rbSpectate then
            rbSpectate = require(LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.SpectateController)
        end
    end)
end

local function rbIsEnemy(player)
    if player == LocalPlayer then return false end
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        if rbSpectate and rbSpectate.CurrentDuelSubject then
            local duel = rbSpectate.CurrentDuelSubject
            local localDueler = duel:GetDueler(LocalPlayer)
            local localTeam = localDueler and localDueler:Get("TeamID") or nil
            if localTeam and duel.Duelers then
                for _, dueler in duel.Duelers do
                    if dueler.Player == player then
                        return dueler:Get("TeamID") ~= localTeam
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local pTeam = player:GetAttribute("TeamID")
        local lTeam = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("TeamID")
        if pTeam and lTeam then return pTeam ~= lTeam end
        return true
    end)
    if ok then return res end
    return true
end

local function rbHasKnifeVM(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer then return false end
    local viewModels = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    if not viewModels then return false end
    local targetName = targetPlayer.Name
    for _, model in ipairs(viewModels:GetChildren()) do
        if model:IsA("Model")
            and string.find(model.Name, targetName, 1, true)
            and string.find(model.Name, "Knife", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function rbClosest()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil, nil, nil end
    local myRoot = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return nil, nil, nil, nil end
    local name = tostring(State.TargetName or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    local range = tonumber(State.RagebotRange) or 500
    local bestP, bestR, bestH, bestD = nil, nil, nil, range
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if rbIsEnemy(player) then
            if name == "" or player.Name == name or player.DisplayName == name then
                local pChar = player.Character
                if pChar then
                    local pRoot = pChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local pHead = pChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
                    if pRoot and pHead and pHum and pHum.Health > 0 then
                        local dist = (myRoot.Position - pRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < bestD then
                            bestD = dist
                            bestP, bestR, bestH = player, pRoot, pHead
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return bestP, bestR, bestH, bestD
end

local function rbUpdateDeflect()
    if not rbFC or not rbFC.Objects then return end
    for _, fighterObj in pairs(rbFC.Objects) do
        local player = fighterObj.Player
        if player then
            local okAlive = false
            pcall(function()
                if fighterObj.Entity and fighterObj.Entity:IsAlive() and not fighterObj:Get("IsSpectating") then
                    okAlive = true
                end
            end)
            if not okAlive then
                rbDeflect[player] = false
            else
                local isDef = false
                pcall(function()
                    local equipped = fighterObj.EquippedItem
                    local isKatana = equipped and equipped.ViewModel and equipped.ViewModel.Name == "Katana"
                    if isKatana and equipped._attack_cooldown and equipped._attack_cooldown > tick() then
                        isDef = true
                    end
                end)
                rbDeflect[player] = isDef
            end
        end
    end
end

rbEquipSlot = nil
local function rbEquipSlot()

    rbLoadMods()
    if not rbFC or not rbFC.LocalFighter then return end
    local slotName = tostring(State.RagebotSlot or "Melee")
    local num = RB_SLOT[slotName] or 3
    pcall(function()
        rbFC.LocalFighter:EquipItem(num)
    end)
end

local function rbWallMove(hrp, char, targetRoot, targetHead)

    wallAngleParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
    local enemyPos = targetRoot.Position
    local tVel = targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local tLook = targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
    local jitterAmp = tonumber(State.RagebotJitter) or 2.25
    local jy = math.sin(tick() * 72) * jitterAmp
    local jx = math.cos(tick() * 55) * (jitterAmp * 0.4)
    local jz = math.sin(tick() * 61) * (jitterAmp * 0.4)

    local stick = enemyPos + tVel * 0.05 + Vector3.new(jx, 0.1 + jy, jz)

    local lookFlat = Vector3.new(tLook.X, 0, tLook.Z)
    if lookFlat.Magnitude < 0.05 then lookFlat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else lookFlat = lookFlat.Unit end
    local side = lookFlat:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    if side.Magnitude < 0.05 then side = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) else side = side.Unit end
    local dirs = {
        -lookFlat, lookFlat, side, -side,
        (-lookFlat + side).Unit, (-lookFlat - side).Unit,
    }
    local bestNear = nil
    local bestDist = 14
    for _, dir in ipairs(dirs) do
        local res = Workspace:Raycast(enemyPos + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), dir * 14, wallAngleParams)
        if res and res.Instance and res.Instance.CanCollide then
            local n = res.Normal
            local depth = math.min(tonumber(State.WallAngleDepth) or 2.5, 2.8)
            local inside = res.Position - n * depth
            local d = (inside - enemyPos).Magnitude
            if d < bestDist then
                bestDist = d
                bestNear = { pos = inside, n = n, hit = res.Position }
            end
        end
    end

    local dest
    if bestNear and bestDist <= 12 then

        dest = bestNear.pos + Vector3.new(jx * 0.5, jy * 0.35, jz * 0.5)
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
        end
        if State.WallAngleDesync ~= false then
            desyncVisualPos = enemyPos + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            desyncActive = true
        end
    else
        dest = stick
    end

    if (dest - enemyPos).Magnitude > 8 then
        local dir = (dest - enemyPos)
        if dir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            dest = enemyPos + dir.Unit * 3 + Vector3.new(0, jy * 0.3, 0)
        else
            dest = stick
        end
    end

    local lookAt = (targetHead and targetHead.Position) or (enemyPos + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
    local cf = CFrame.lookAt(dest, lookAt)
    pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
        end
    end)
    hrp.CFrame = cf
    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel
    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    local bp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanRB_BP")
    if not bp or not bp:IsA("BodyPosition") then
        if bp then pcall(function() bp:Destroy() end) end
        bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
        bp.Name = "ChanRB_BP"
        bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
        bp.P = 1e6
        bp.D = 5000
        bp.Parent = hrp
    end
    bp.Position = dest
    return cf
end

function updateRagebot(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not State.Ragebot or not hrp then return end
    pcall(function()
        rbLoadMods()
        rbUpdateDeflect()
        if tick() - rbEquipAt > 1 then
            rbEquipAt = tick()
            rbEquipSlot()
        end

        local targetPlayer, targetRoot, targetHead = rbClosest()
        if not targetRoot or not targetHead then return end
        if rbDeflect[targetPlayer] then return end

        local desyncCF = rbWallMove(hrp, char, targetRoot, targetHead)

        local shotOriginCF
        if rbHasKnifeVM(targetPlayer) then
            shotOriginCF = CFrame.lookAt((targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 6, 0)).Position, targetHead.Position)
        else
            shotOriginCF = CFrame.lookAt((targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 2)).Position, targetHead.Position)
        end

        local oldCF = hrp.CFrame
        local oldVel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = shotOriginCF
            RunService:BindToRenderStep("__chan_rb_restore", 101, function()
                if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                    hrp.CFrame = desyncCF or oldCF
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = oldVel
                end
                pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("__chan_rb_restore") end)
            end)
        end)
