        local rate = tonumber(State.RagebotFireRate) or 0.0005
        if tick() - rbLastFire < rate then return end
        if not rbFC or not rbFC.LocalFighter then return end
        local item = rbFC.LocalFighter.EquippedItem
        if not item then return end
        if not rbUtil or not rbEnum then return end

        rbLastFire = tick()
        local originPos = (shotOriginCF and shotOriginCF.Position) or targetRoot.Position
        local targetPos = targetHead.Position
        local aimCF = CFrame.lookAt(originPos, targetPos)
        local targetCF = targetHead.CFrame
        local randomOffset = Vector3.new(
            (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1,
            (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1,
            (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1
        )
        local aimedPos = targetPos + randomOffset
        local objSpaceHeadOffset = targetHead.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(aimedPos))
        local cameradata = {}
        cameradata[utf8.char(1)] = {
            [utf8.char(0)] = rbUtil:EncodeCFrame(aimCF),
            [utf8.char(1)] = rbUtil:EncodeCFrame(targetCF),
            [utf8.char(2)] = targetHead,
            [utf8.char(3)] = rbUtil:EncodeCFrame(objSpaceHeadOffset)
        }
        local useRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        useRemote = useRemote and useRemote:FindFirstChild("Replication")
        useRemote = useRemote and useRemote:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
        useRemote = useRemote and useRemote:FindFirstChild("UseItem")
        if useRemote then
            pcall(function()
                useRemote:FireServer(
                    item:Get("ObjectID"),
                    rbEnum:ToEnum("StartShooting"),
                    cameradata,
                    nil
                )
            end)
        end

        local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if tool then
            for _ = 1, 4 do pcall(function() tool:Activate() end) end
        end
    end)
end

local hvhTargetSticky = nil
local hvhBacktrackBuf = {}
local hvhLastSafe = nil
local hvhOrbitAng = 0
local hvhAAYaw = 0

function hvhGetTarget()
    if State.HvHStickyTarget and hvhTargetSticky then
        local p = hvhTargetSticky
        if p.Parent and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                return p.Character, p
            end
        end
        if State.HvHAutoRetarget then
            hvhTargetSticky = nil
        else
            return nil, nil
        end
    end
    local char, plr = rageGetTarget()
    if plr and State.HvHStickyTarget then
        hvhTargetSticky = plr
    end
    return char, plr
end

function hvhRecordBacktrack()
    if not State.HvHBacktrack then return end
    local now = tick()
    local keep = (tonumber(State.HvHBacktrackMs) or 120) / 1000 * 2
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if h then
                local buf = hvhBacktrackBuf[p] or {}
                table.insert(buf, { t = now, pos = h.Position })
                local filtered = {}
                for _, e in ipairs(buf) do
                    if now - e.t <= keep then
                        table.insert(filtered, e)
                    end
                end
                hvhBacktrackBuf[p] = filtered
            end
        end
    end
end

function hvhBacktrackPos(plr)
    local buf = hvhBacktrackBuf[plr]
    if not buf or #buf == 0 then return nil end
    local want = tick() - (tonumber(State.HvHBacktrackMs) or 120) / 1000
    local best = buf[1]
    for _, e in ipairs(buf) do
        if e.t <= want then best = e end
    end
    return best and best.pos
end

function updateHvHPack(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if not hrp then return end
    local now = tick()

    if State.HvHAntiVoid then
        if rageIsSaneHome(hrp.Position) then
            hvhLastSafe = hrp.CFrame
        elseif hvhLastSafe and (math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) > 50000 or hrp.Position.Y ~= hrp.Position.Y) then
            pcall(function()
                hrp.CFrame = hvhLastSafe
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end
    end

    if State.HvHAntiAim then
        local spd = tonumber(State.HvHAntiAimSpeed) or 720
        hvhAAYaw = (hvhAAYaw + spd * 0.016) % 360
        pcall(function()
            local pos = hrp.Position
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(hvhAAYaw), 0)
        end)
    end

    if State.HvHStrafeDesync then
        local amp = tonumber(State.HvHStrafeAmp) or 6
        local side = math.sin(now * 25) * amp
        pcall(function()
            local r = hrp.CFrame.RightVector
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + r * side * 0.15
        end)
    end

    local tChar, tPlr = hvhGetTarget()
    local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if (State.HvHResolver or State.HvHPrediction) and tHrp then
        pcall(function()
            local aimPos = tHrp.Position
            if State.HvHBacktrack and tPlr then
                local bt = hvhBacktrackPos(tPlr)
                if bt then aimPos = bt end
            end
            if State.HvHPrediction then
                local vel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local mul = tonumber(State.HvHPredictionMul) or 0.15
                aimPos = aimPos + vel * mul
            end
            if State.HvHResolver then

                aimPos = (aimPos + tHrp.Position) * 0.5 + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
            end
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam and State.Aimbot then

            end
            char:SetAttribute("HvHAimX", aimPos.X)
            char:SetAttribute("HvHAimY", aimPos.Y)
            char:SetAttribute("HvHAimZ", aimPos.Z)
        end)
    end

    if State.HvHOrbit and tHrp then
        hvhOrbitAng = hvhOrbitAng + (tonumber(State.HvHOrbitSpeed) or 10) * 0.05
        local rad = tonumber(State.HvHOrbitRadius) or 8
        local pos = tHrp.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(hvhOrbitAng) * rad, 2, math.sin(hvhOrbitAng) * rad)
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, tHrp.Position)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    if State.HvHAirStuck then
        pcall(function()
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    hvhRecordBacktrack()
end

local pbScanFolders = nil
local pbLastScan = 0
local function pbIsOwn(inst)
    if not inst then return true end
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        local attrNames = {"Owner", "Creator", "Player", "UserId", "Shooter", "Attacker"}
        for _, a in ipairs(attrNames) do
            local v = inst:GetAttribute(a)
            if v ~= nil then
                if v == LocalPlayer.UserId or v == LocalPlayer.Name or v == LocalPlayer.DisplayName then
                    return true
                end
                if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v == LocalPlayer then return true end
            end
        end

        local p = inst
        for _ = 1, 8 do
            if not p then break end
            if p == LocalPlayer.Character then return true end
            if p.Name == LocalPlayer.Name then return true end
            local own = p:GetAttribute("Owner") or p:GetAttribute("Creator")
            if own == LocalPlayer.UserId or own == LocalPlayer.Name then return true end
            p = p.Parent
        end
        return false
    end)
    return ok and res
end

local function pbLooksProjectile(inst)
    if not inst:IsA("BasePart") and not inst:IsA("Model") then return false end
    local n = string.lower(inst.Name)
    local keys = {"bullet", "projectile", "arrow", "pellet", "slug", "rocket", "missile", "throw", "knife", "bolt", "shot", "ammo", "orb", "ball"}
    for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
        if string.find(n, k, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
        local sp = inst.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
        if sp > 80 and inst.Size.Magnitude < 12 then return true end
    end
    return false
end

function updateProjectileBreaker()
    if not State.ProjectileBreaker then return end
    pcall(function()
        local now = tick()
        if now - (pbLastScan or 0) < 0.2 then return end
        pbLastScan = now
        if not pbScanFolders or now - (pbFolderRefresh or 0) > 3 then
            pbFolderRefresh = now
            pbScanFolders = {}
            for _, name in ipairs({"Projectiles", "Bullets", "Effects", "Debris", "Thrown", "ProjectilesFolder", "Ignore", "Rays"}) do
                local f = Workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
                if f then table.insert(pbScanFolders, f) end
            end
        end
        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        for _, folder in ipairs(pbScanFolders) do
            if folder and folder.Parent then
                local n = 0
                for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    n = n + 1
                    if n > 40 then break end
                    if not (myChar and ch:IsDescendantOf(myChar)) and not pbIsOwn(ch) and pbLooksProjectile(ch) then
                        pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not State then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char.Parent then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

    do
        local anyDesyncFeature = State.VoidSpam or State.AntiHit or State.AntiUnderOffset
            or State.Underground or State.UECounter or State.Rage or State.HvHStrafeDesync
            or State.WallAngle or State.WallTarget or State.SwitchSpamWall or State.Dongko or State.FakePos
        if not anyDesyncFeature then
            desyncActive = false
            desyncVisualPos = nil
        end
        if not State.VoidSpam then
            voidSpamPhase = nil
            voidSpamUntil = 0
        end
        if not State.Rage and rageActive then
            pcall(stopRage)
        end
        if not State.WallAngle and not State.WallTarget and not State.SwitchSpamWall and wallAngleLastCF then
            pcall(stopWallAngle, char)
        end
        if not State.Ragebot and not State.WallAngle and not State.WallTarget and not State.SwitchSpamWall then
            if wallAngleNoClipOn == true then
                pcall(stopWallAngle, char)
            end
            pcall(function()
                local bp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanRB_BP")
                if bp then bp:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        if not State.Underground then
            pcall(function()
                if type(stopLionDesync) == "function" then stopLionDesync() end
            end)
        end
        if not State.Dongko then
            pcall(function()
                local bp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanDongko_BP")
                if bp then bp:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        if not State.Fly and not State.Rage and not State.VoidSpam and not State.WallAngle and not State.WallTarget and not State.SwitchSpamWall and not State.HvHOrbit and not State.HvHAirStuck then
            pcall(function()
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end)
        end

        if not State.Rage and not State.TP and not State.VoidSpam then
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if o:IsA("BodyPosition") or o:IsA("BodyGyro") or o:IsA("BodyVelocity") then
                        local n = o.Name
                        if n:find("Rage") or n:find("Void") or n:find("Chan") or n:find("TP") or n:find("VS") then
                            o:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        if not State.HvHAntiAim and not State.HvHOrbit and not State.WallAngle then
            pcall(function()
                if humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate = true end
            end)
        end
    end

    if not (State.VoidSpam or State.AntiHit or State.AntiUnderOffset or State.Underground or State.UECounter) then
        desyncActive = false
    end

    if State.AntiHit then
        pcall(function()
            local mode = State.AntiHitMode or "Snap"
            local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
            local tHrp = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if mode == "Climb" then
                captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                desyncActive = true
                local step = tonumber(State.AntiHitClimb) or 5
                local pos = hrp.Position
                hrp.Anchored = false
                for _ = 1, 4 do
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, pos.Y + step, pos.Z)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            elseif mode == "Pumping" then
                captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                desyncActive = true
                local amp = tonumber(State.AntiHitPump) or 8
                local yOff = math.sin(tick() * 90) * amp
                local pos = hrp.Position

                if not antiHitLastSafe or math.abs(antiHitLastSafe.Y) < 5000 then
                    if math.abs(pos.Y) < 5000 then
                        antiHitLastSafe = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z)
                    end
                end
                local baseY = (antiHitLastSafe and antiHitLastSafe.Y) or pos.Y
                hrp.Anchored = false
                for _ = 1, 4 do
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, baseY + yOff, pos.Z)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            elseif mode == "ChanhyukAss" then

                local lockFar = true
                if tHrp then
                    local ty = tHrp.Position.Y
                    if isUEUnderOffsetY(ty) then
                        lockFar = false
                    end
                end
                if lockFar then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                    desyncActive = true
                    local dmin = tonumber(State.VoidDistMin) or 1e12
                    local ang = tick() * 2.3
                    local lockPos = Vector3.new(
                        math.cos(ang) * dmin * 0.15,
                        dmin * 0.08,
                        math.sin(ang) * dmin * 0.15
                    )
                    for _ = 1, 6 do
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(lockPos)
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                    hrp.Anchored = false
                else

                    hrp.Anchored = false
                    desyncActive = false
                end
            else

                local myPos = hrp.Position
                if math.abs(myPos.Y) < 8000 and not isUEUnderOffsetY(myPos.Y) then
                    antiHitLastSafe = myPos
                end
                local pulled = isUEUnderOffsetY(myPos.Y) or isUnderOffsetPull(hrp, tHrp)
                if antiHitLastSafe and pulled then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                    desyncActive = true
                    for _ = 1, 12 do
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(antiHitLastSafe)
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                    hrp.Anchored = false
                    if humanoid then
                        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    else

        pcall(function()
            if hrp.Anchored and not State.Underground then
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end
        end)
    end

    if State.Underground then
        pcall(function()
            applyLionUnderground(hrp)
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            end
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.CanCollide = false
        end)
    elseif not State.Ragebot then
        stopLionDesync()
    end

    State.SilentAim = false
    State.Ragebot = false

    if State.MaulSlam then pcall(applyMaulSlam) end
    pcall(applyAimbot)
    if State.PerfectSpam and hrp then pcall(function() applyPerfectSpam(hrp) end) end

    if State.TP then

        desyncActive = false
        desyncVisualPos = nil
        pcall(function()
            hrp.Anchored = false
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                humanoid.Sit = false
                humanoid.AutoRotate = true
            end

            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam then
                if cam.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then
                    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                end
                if humanoid and cam.CameraSubject ~= humanoid then
                    cam.CameraSubject = humanoid
                end
            end
        end)
        local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
        if targetChar and isEnemyCharacter(targetChar) and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local tHrp = targetChar.HumanoidRootPart
            local cf = calculateTPCFrame(tHrp, State.TPMode)
            pcall(function()
                if sethiddenproperty then
                    pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false) end)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
                end)

                for _, n in ipairs({"ChanTP_BP", "ChanTP_BG"}) do
                    local o = hrp:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if o then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
                end
                local tVel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                for _ = 1, 24 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end)
            local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if tool then
                local tname = string.lower(tool.Name or "")
                local knife = tname:find("knife") or tname:find("blade") or tname:find("dagger") or tname:find("katana")
                if not knife then
                    applyNoCD(true)
                    for _ = 1, 8 do
                        pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                    end
                else
                    pcall(function()
                        local h = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                        if h then h.CanTouch = true end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    else
        local tbp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanTP_BP")
        if tbp then pcall(function() tbp:Destroy() end) end
        local tbg = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanTP_BG")
        if tbg then pcall(function() tbg:Destroy() end) end
    end

    if State.HitBehind then
        local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
        if targetChar and isEnemyCharacter(targetChar) and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local tHrp = targetChar.HumanoidRootPart
            local look = tHrp.CFrame.LookVector
            local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then
                flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
            else
                flat = flat.Unit
            end
            local behind = tHrp.Position - flat * 2.2 + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)
            local cf = CFrame.new(behind, tHrp.Position)
            pcall(function()
                hrp.CFrame = cf
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
            local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if tool then
                local tname = string.lower(tool.Name or "")
                local knife = tname:find("knife") or tname:find("blade") or tname:find("dagger") or tname:find("katana")
                if not knife then
                    applyNoCD(true)
                    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                else
                    pcall(function()
                        local h = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                        if h then h.CanTouch = true end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if State.UECounter then
        pcall(function()
            if not _G.__uecLastTp then _G.__uecLastTp = 0 end
            local delay = tonumber(State.UECTPDelay) or 0.05
            if delay < 0.01 then delay = 0.01 end
            local yDist = tonumber(State.UECYDistance) or 500
            if yDist < 1 then yDist = 1 end
            local now = tick()
            local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
            local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local baseX, baseZ = hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Z
            if tHrp then
                baseX = tHrp.Position.X
                baseZ = tHrp.Position.Z
            end
            local prevY = 50
            if _G.__uecHoldCF then
                prevY = _G.__uecHoldCF.Y
            else
                if tHrp then
                    local ty = tHrp.Position.Y
                    if ty > -500 and ty < 5000 then
                        prevY = ty
                    end
                elseif math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 5000 then
                    prevY = hrp.Position.Y
                end
            end
            if (now - _G.__uecLastTp) >= delay then
                _G.__uecLastTp = now

                local fromY = hrp.Position.Y
                if fromY < -500 or fromY > 1e12 then
                    fromY = prevY
                end
                local oy = fromY + yDist
                if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then
                    oy = fromY + yDist + 50
                end
                local cf = CFrame.new(baseX, oy, baseZ)
                _G.__uecHoldCF = cf
                for _ = 1, 16 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end

            hrp.Anchored = false
        end)
    else
        _G.__uecLastTp = nil
        _G.__uecHoldCF = nil
        _G.__uecPulse = nil
    end

    if State.AntiUECounter and not State.UECounter then
        pcall(function()
            local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
            local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not tHrp then return end
            local tPos = tHrp.Position
            local tVel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if not _G.__antiUecLastTY then _G.__antiUecLastTY = tPos.Y end
            local yDelta = math.abs(tPos.Y - _G.__antiUecLastTY)
            _G.__antiUecLastTY = tPos.Y
            local look = tHrp.CFrame.LookVector
            local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else flat = flat.Unit end
            local dest = tPos + Vector3.new(0, 0.35, 0) - flat * 0.15
            local cf = CFrame.new(dest, dest + flat)
            for _ = 1, 16 do
                hrp.CFrame = cf
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            if yDelta > 20 then
                for _ = 1, 10 do
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(tPos.X, tPos.Y + 0.35, tPos.Z)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        _G.__antiUecLastTY = nil
    end

    if State.SolixCounter then
        pcall(function() updateSolixCounter(hrp, humanoid, char) end)
    end

    if State.KiciaCounter then
        pcall(function() updateKiciaCounter(hrp, humanoid, char) end)
    end
    if State.KiciaStickCounter then
        pcall(function() updateKiciaStickCounter(hrp, humanoid, char) end)
    end
    if State.WallAngle or State.WallTarget or State.SwitchSpamWall then
        pcall(function() updateWallAngle(hrp, humanoid, char) end)
    elseif wallAngleLastCF then
        pcall(stopWallAngle, char)
    end
    if State.Ragebot then
        pcall(function() updateRagebot(hrp, humanoid, char) end)
    end
    if State.ProjectileBreaker then
        pcall(updateProjectileBreaker)
    end
    pcall(function() updateHvHPack(hrp, humanoid, char) end)

    if State.GNG then
        pcall(function() updateGNG(hrp, humanoid, char) end)
    end

    if State.UnderOffsetAttack then
        pcall(function()
            if not _G.__uoLastWander then _G.__uoLastWander = 0 end
            local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
            local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local enemyUO = tHrp and isUEUnderOffsetY(tHrp.Position.Y)
            if enemyUO and tHrp then

                local dest = tHrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                local cf = CFrame.new(dest, dest + tHrp.CFrame.LookVector)
                for _ = 1, 14 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                desyncActive = false
            else

                local now = tick()
                if now - _G.__uoLastWander >= 0.04 then
                    _G.__uoLastWander = now
                    if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 8000 then
                        captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                    end
                    desyncActive = true
                    local dist = 80000 + math.random() * 250000
                    local theta = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                    local baseY = 350 + math.random() * 400
                    local ox = math.cos(theta) * dist
                    local oz = math.sin(theta) * dist
                    local oy = baseY + (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 80
                    if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then oy = baseY end
                    local cf = CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz)
                    for _ = 1, 10 do
                        hrp.CFrame = cf
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            part.CanTouch = false
                            part.CanQuery = false
                        end
                    end
                else
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if State.YLock then
        pcall(function()
            local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
            local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local allowMove = tHrp and isUEUnderOffsetY(tHrp.Position.Y)
            if allowMove then

                desyncActive = false
            else
                local yv = tonumber(State.YLockValue) or 5000
                local x = hrp.Position.X
                local z = hrp.Position.Z
                if desyncVisualPos and math.abs(desyncVisualPos.Y) < 50000 then
                    x = desyncVisualPos.X
                    z = desyncVisualPos.Z
                end

                local cf = CFrame.new(x, yv, z)
                for _ = 1, 12 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if State.JustPoop then
        pcall(function()
            local tChar = getClosestPlayer()
            local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local enemyUO = tHrp and isUEUnderOffsetY(tHrp.Position.Y)
            local myY = hrp.Position.Y
            local meGoingUO = isUEUnderOffsetY(myY) or isUnderOffsetPull(hrp, tHrp)

            if meGoingUO then

                if math.abs(myY) < 50000 then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                end
                desyncActive = true
                local safeY = 9e8 + math.random() * 1e7
                local dist = 1e14 + math.random() * 1e16
                local th = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local cf = CFrame.new(math.cos(th) * dist, safeY, math.sin(th) * dist)
                for _ = 1, 16 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanTouch = false
                        part.CanQuery = false
                    end
                end
            elseif enemyUO then

                if math.abs(myY) < 50000 then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                end
                desyncActive = true
                local highY = 5e9 + math.random() * 5e9
                local dist = 1e13 + math.random() * 1e15
                local th = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local cf = CFrame.new(math.cos(th) * dist, highY, math.sin(th) * dist)
                for _ = 1, 14 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            else

                if math.abs(myY) < 50000 then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                end
                desyncActive = true
                local dist = 1e14 + math.random() * 1e17
                local th = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local ph = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * math.pi * 0.5
                local ox = math.cos(th) * math.cos(ph) * dist
                local oy = 1e6 + math.abs(math.sin(ph)) * dist * 0.15
                local oz = math.sin(th) * math.cos(ph) * dist
                if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then oy = 5e6 end
                local cf = CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz)
                for _ = 1, 12 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanTouch = false
                        part.CanQuery = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if State.BookTak then
        pcall(function()
            if not _G.__btPhase then _G.__btPhase = "ready" end
            if not _G.__btUntil then _G.__btUntil = 0 end
            local now = tick()
            local hold = tonumber(State.BookTakTime) or 0.00015
            local cd = tonumber(State.BookTakCd) or 0.15
            local yBack = tonumber(State.BookTakY) or 523
            if hold < 0 then hold = 0 end
            if cd < 0 then cd = 0 end
            if _G.__btPhase == "ready" then
                _G.__btPhase = "uo"
                _G.__btUntil = now + hold
                if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 50000 then captureDesyncVisual(hrp) end
            end
            if _G.__btPhase == "uo" then
                local dest = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, 1004046, hrp.Position.Z)
                for _ = 1, 10 do
                    hrp.CFrame = dest
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                if now >= _G.__btUntil then
                    _G.__btPhase = "back"
                    _G.__btUntil = now + cd
                end
            elseif _G.__btPhase == "back" then
                local x = (desyncVisualPos and desyncVisualPos.X) or hrp.Position.X
                local z = (desyncVisualPos and desyncVisualPos.Z) or hrp.Position.Z
                local cf = CFrame.new(x, yBack, z)
                for _ = 1, 10 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                if now >= _G.__btUntil then
                    _G.__btPhase = "ready"
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        _G.__btPhase = nil
    end

    if State.CallJonjal then
        pcall(function()
            local maxMode = State.CallJonjalMax

            local onT = maxMode and 0.00005 or 0.00012
            local offT = maxMode and 0.0015 or 0.004
            if not _G.__cjUntil then _G.__cjUntil = 0 end
            if not _G.__cjOn then _G.__cjOn = false end
            local now = tick()
            if now >= _G.__cjUntil then
                _G.__cjOn = not _G.__cjOn
                _G.__cjUntil = now + (_G.__cjOn and onT or offT)
            end
            if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 50000 then captureDesyncVisual(hrp) end
            if _G.__cjOn then
                local x = (desyncVisualPos and desyncVisualPos.X) or hrp.Position.X
                local z = (desyncVisualPos and desyncVisualPos.Z) or hrp.Position.Z
                local cf = CFrame.new(x, 1004046, z)
                local reps = maxMode and 20 or 12
                for _ = 1, reps do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end

                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanTouch = false
                        part.CanQuery = false
                    end
                end
            else

                local x = (desyncVisualPos and desyncVisualPos.X) or hrp.Position.X
                local z = (desyncVisualPos and desyncVisualPos.Z) or hrp.Position.Z
                local safeY = maxMode and (5e5 + math.random() * 1e5) or (2e5 + math.random() * 5e4)
                local cf = CFrame.new(x + (math.random()-0.5)*50, safeY, z + (math.random()-0.5)*50)
                for _ = 1, 8 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                desyncActive = true
            end
        end)
    end

    if State.JjDdong then
        pcall(function()
            if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 50000 then captureDesyncVisual(hrp) end
            local x = hrp.Position.X
            local z = hrp.Position.Z
            local cf = CFrame.new(x, 1004046, z)
            for _ = 1, 14 do
                hrp.CFrame = cf
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end)
    end

    if State.Dongko then
        pcall(function()
            local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
            if not targetChar then return end
            local tHrp = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not tHrp then return end
            local tPos = tHrp.Position
            local tVel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            local tLook = tHrp.CFrame.LookVector
            local jitterY = math.sin(tick() * 48) * 1.5
            local dest = tPos + tVel * 0.055 + Vector3.new(0, 0.12 + jitterY, 0)
            local lookFlat = Vector3.new(tLook.X, 0, tLook.Z)
            if lookFlat.Magnitude < 0.05 then lookFlat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else lookFlat = lookFlat.Unit end
            local cf = CFrame.new(dest, dest + lookFlat)

            pcall(function()
                if sethiddenproperty then
                    sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                end
            end)
            hrp.CFrame = cf
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            local bp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanDongko_BP")
            if not bp or not bp:IsA("BodyPosition") then
                if bp then pcall(function() bp:Destroy() end) end
                bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                bp.Name = "ChanDongko_BP"
                bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
                bp.P = 1e6
                bp.D = 5000
                bp.Parent = hrp
            end
            bp.Position = dest

            if not dongkoClientCF then
                local safe = hrp.Position
                if math.abs(safe.Y) < 5000 then
                    dongkoClientCF = CFrame.new(safe)
                end
            end

            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                if tool then
                    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        local bp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanDongko_BP")
        if bp then pcall(function() bp:Destroy() end) end
        dongkoClientCF = nil
    end

    if not State.VoidSpam then
        if voidSpamPhase ~= nil or voidSpamUntil ~= nil then
            voidSpamPhase = nil
            voidSpamUntil = 0
            pcall(function()
                if hrp then
                    hrp.Anchored = false

                    for _, n in ipairs({"ChanVoid_BP", "ChanVoid_BG", "ChanVS_BP", "ChanVS_BG"}) do
                        local o = hrp:FindFirstChild(n)
                        if o then o:Destroy() end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    if State.VoidSpam then
        local now = tick()
        if not voidSpamUntil then voidSpamUntil = 0 end
        if not voidSpamPhase then voidSpamPhase = "void" end
        local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
        local underMe = false
        local tHrp = nil
        if targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            tHrp = targetChar.HumanoidRootPart
            local ty = tHrp.Position.Y

            if isUEUnderOffsetY(ty) then
                underMe = true
            end
        end
        if State.VoidMode == "Chanhyuk" then

            if underMe then

                voidSpamPhase = "stop"
                desyncActive = false
            else
                voidSpamPhase = "void"
            end
            pcall(function()
                if voidSpamPhase == "void" then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                    desyncActive = true
                    local ang = (now * 17 + (hrp.Position.X % 97)) % (math.pi * 2)
                    local elev = math.sin(now * 13) * 0.7
                    local dmin = tonumber(State.VoidDistMin) or 1e12
                    local dmax = tonumber(State.VoidDistMax) or dmin
                    if dmax < dmin then dmax = dmin end
                    local dist = dmin + math.abs(math.sin(now * 19)) * math.max(0, dmax - dmin)
                    if dist < 1e11 then dist = 1e12 end
                    local ox = math.cos(ang) * math.cos(elev) * dist
                    local oy = math.abs(dist * 0.12 + math.sin(now * 7) * dist * 0.08)
                    if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then oy = dist * 0.2 end
                    local oz = math.sin(ang) * math.cos(elev) * dist
                    local cf = CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz)
                    pcall(function()
                        if sethiddenproperty then
                            sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                        end
                        if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
                    end)
                    for _ = 1, 14 do
                        hrp.CFrame = cf
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            part.CanTouch = false
                            part.CanQuery = false
                        end
                    end
                else
                    desyncActive = false
                end
            end)
        elseif State.VoidMode == "UE" then

            pcall(function()
                desyncActive = true
                if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 80000 then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                end

                local now2 = tick()
                local cd = tonumber(State.UETeleportCd) or 0
                if not _G.__ueLastTp then _G.__ueLastTp = 0 end
                local skipTp = (cd > 0 and (now2 - (_G.__ueLastTp or 0)) < cd)
                if not skipTp then
                _G.__ueLastTp = now2
                local Y_LOCK = tonumber(State.UEYLock) or 1000000000
                local Y_DOWN = tonumber(State.UEYDownMax) or 50000000
                local Y_FLOOR = Y_LOCK - Y_DOWN
                local Y_CEIL = Y_LOCK + math.max(Y_DOWN * 4, 200000000)
                local dmin = tonumber(State.VoidDistMin) or 1e12
                local dmax = tonumber(State.VoidDistMax) or 9.999999999999999e18
                if dmax < dmin then dmax = dmin end
                local t = math.random()
                local dist = dmin * ((dmax / dmin) ^ t)
                if dist < dmin then dist = dmin end
                if dist > dmax then dist = dmax end
                local theta = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local ox = math.cos(theta) * dist
                local oz = math.sin(theta) * dist
                local yJitter = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * Y_DOWN
                local oy = Y_LOCK + yJitter
                if oy < Y_FLOOR then oy = Y_FLOOR + math.random() * math.min(Y_DOWN * 0.02, 1e6) end
                if oy > Y_CEIL then oy = Y_CEIL - math.random() * math.min(Y_DOWN * 0.02, 1e6) end
                if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then oy = Y_LOCK end
                local cf = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(ox, oy, oz))
                for _ = 1, 16 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                hrp.Anchored = false
                pcall(function()
                    if sethiddenproperty then
                        sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                    end
                    if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
                end)
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanTouch = false
                        part.CanQuery = false
                    end
                end
                end
            end)

        elseif State.VoidMode == "Basic" then

            pcall(function()
                desyncActive = true
                if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 100000 then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if sethiddenproperty then
                        sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                    end
                    if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
                end)
                local dmin = tonumber(State.VoidDistMin) or 1e12
                local dmax = tonumber(State.VoidDistMax) or 1e18
                if dmax < dmin then dmax = dmin end
                dmin = dmin * 300
                dmax = dmax * 3000
                if dmin < 3e15 then dmin = 3e15 end
                if dmax < dmin then dmax = dmin * 10 end
                local t = math.random()
                local dist = dmin * ((dmax / dmin) ^ t)
                local theta = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local phi = (math.random() - 0.5) * math.pi
                local ox = math.cos(theta) * math.cos(phi) * dist
                local oz = math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi) * dist
                local oy = math.abs(math.sin(phi)) * dist * 0.35 + 1e9 + math.random() * 1e8
                if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then
                    oy = 1e9 + math.random() * 1e8
                end
                local cf = CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz)
                for _ = 1, 18 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end)

        elseif State.VoidMode == "OnlyVoid" then

            pcall(function()
                desyncActive = true
                if math.abs(hrp.Position.Y) < 100000 then
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if sethiddenproperty then
                        sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                    end
                    if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
                end)
                local now = tick()
                if not _G.__ovLastUp then _G.__ovLastUp = 0 end
                if not _G.__ovOriginXZ then
                    _G.__ovOriginXZ = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z)
                end
                if not _G.__ovBaseY then
                    local y = hrp.Position.Y
                    if y < -500 or y > 1e12 then y = 50 end
                    _G.__ovBaseY = y
                end
                if (now - _G.__ovLastUp) >= 0.15 then
                    _G.__ovLastUp = now
                    _G.__ovBaseY = (_G.__ovBaseY or hrp.Position.Y) + 450
                end

                local radius = tonumber(State.OnlyVoidDist) or 50000
                if radius < 1 then radius = 1 end
                local theta = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local r = math.random() * radius
                local ox = _G.__ovOriginXZ.X + math.cos(theta) * r
                local oz = _G.__ovOriginXZ.Z + math.sin(theta) * r
                local oy = _G.__ovBaseY
                if oy > 1000000 and oy < 2000000 then
                    oy = _G.__ovBaseY + 50
                end
                local cf = CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz)
                for _ = 1, 14 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end)

        elseif State.VoidMode == "Side" then

            local dist = tonumber(State.SideSpamDist) or 80
            if dist < 5 then dist = 5 end
            local base = hrp.Position
            if tHrp then base = tHrp.Position end
            local right = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
            pcall(function()
                local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam then
                    right = cam.CFrame.RightVector
                    right = Vector3.new(right.X, 0, right.Z)
                    if right.Magnitude > 0.01 then right = right.Unit else right = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
                end
            end)
            local sign = (math.floor(tick() * 40) % 2 == 0) and 1 or -1
            local dest = Vector3.new(base.X, base.Y + 2, base.Z) + right * (dist * sign)
            local cf = CFrame.new(dest)
            for _ = 1, 20 do
                hrp.CFrame = cf
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            hrp.Anchored = false
        elseif State.VoidMode == "Short" then

            pcall(function() updateShortVoid(hrp) end)
        else

            local voidT = tonumber(State.VoidTime) or 0.0087
            local atkT = tonumber(State.AttackTime) or 0.0087
            if now >= voidSpamUntil then
                if voidSpamPhase == "void" then
                    voidSpamPhase = "tp"
                    voidSpamUntil = now + atkT
                else
                    voidSpamPhase = "void"
                    voidSpamUntil = now + voidT
                end
            end
            pcall(function()
                if voidSpamPhase == "tp" and tHrp then
                    local tPos = tHrp.Position
                    local tVel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local look = tHrp.CFrame.LookVector
                    local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
                    if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else flat = flat.Unit end
                    local side = flat:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
                    if side.Magnitude < 0.05 then side = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) else side = side.Unit end

                    local antiPred = tPos
                    local vflat = Vector3.new(tVel.X, 0, tVel.Z)
                    if vflat.Magnitude > 1.5 then
                        antiPred = tPos - vflat.Unit * 1.2
                    end

                    local settle = (now % 0.2) < 0.07
                    local dest
                    if settle then
                        dest = antiPred + side * (2.6 * (math.sin(now * 11) > 0 and 1 or -1)) - flat * 1.4 + Vector3.new(0, 0.55, 0)
                    else

                        dest = antiPred - flat * 2.15 + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0)
                    end
                    for _ = 1, 5 do
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest, tPos)
                    end
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                else
                    captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                    desyncActive = true

                    local base = tHrp and tHrp.Position or hrp.Position
                    local phase = math.floor(now * 14) % 4
                    local dist, elev
                    if phase == 0 then
                        dist, elev = 1800 + math.random() * 2200, 0.4
                    elseif phase == 1 then
                        dist, elev = 3200 + math.random() * 4000, -0.2
                    elseif phase == 2 then
                        dist, elev = 900 + math.random() * 1200, 0.9
                    else
                        dist, elev = 5000 + math.random() * 5000, 0.15
                    end
                    local ang = now * 7 + math.random() * 2
                    local ox = base.X + math.cos(ang) * dist
                    local oy = base.Y + elev * dist * 0.25 + 40
                    local oz = base.Z + math.sin(ang) * dist
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    if State.AntiNoRage then
        pcall(function()
            local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
            local enemyVoiding = false
            local now = tick()
            if targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local tHrp = targetChar.HumanoidRootPart
                local tPos = tHrp.Position
                local spd = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
                if lastEnemyPos then
                    local jumped = (tPos - lastEnemyPos).Magnitude
                    if jumped > 60 or spd > 150 or math.abs(tPos.Y) > 3000
                        or math.abs(tPos.X) > 20000 or math.abs(tPos.Z) > 20000 then
                        enemyVoiding = true
                    end
                end
                lastEnemyPos = tPos
                lastEnemyCheck = now
            end
            if enemyVoiding then
                antiNoRageLock = nil
                hrp.Anchored = false
            else

                local myPos = hrp.Position
                if not antiNoRageLock or math.abs(antiNoRageLock.Y) > 5000 or math.abs(antiNoRageLock.X) > 50000 then
                    if math.abs(myPos.Y) < 5000 and math.abs(myPos.X) < 50000 then
                        antiNoRageLock = myPos
                    elseif desyncVisualPos then
                        antiNoRageLock = desyncVisualPos
                    else
                        antiNoRageLock = Vector3.new(myPos.X, 50, myPos.Z)
                    end
                end
                for _ = 1, 5 do
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(antiNoRageLock)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        antiNoRageLock = nil
    end

    if State.AntiUnderOffset then
        pcall(function()
            local tHrp = nil
            local tc = getClosestPlayer()
            if tc and tc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                tHrp = tc.HumanoidRootPart
            end
            if isUnderOffsetPull(hrp, tHrp) then
                captureDesyncVisual(hrp)
                desyncActive = true
                local dmin = tonumber(State.VoidDistMin) or 1e12
                local dmax = tonumber(State.VoidDistMax) or dmin
                if dmax < dmin then dmax = dmin end
                local dist = dmin + math.random() * math.max(0, dmax - dmin)
                local ang = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local elev = (math.random() - 0.3) * 1.2
                local dest = Vector3.new(
                    math.cos(ang) * math.cos(elev) * dist,
                    5e5 + math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 9)) * dist * 0.15,
                    math.sin(ang) * math.cos(elev) * dist
                )
                for _ = 1, 12 do
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                voidSpamPhase = "void"
                voidSpamUntil = tick() + (tonumber(State.VoidTime) or 0.0087)
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        local tname = tool and string.lower(tool.Name or "") or ""
        local isKnife = tname ~= "" and (tname:find("knife") or tname:find("blade") or tname:find("dagger") or tname:find("katana") or tname:find("sword"))

        if isKnife then

            pcall(function()
                local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
                    handle.CanTouch = true
                    handle.CanQuery = true
                    handle.CanCollide = false
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        part.CanTouch = true
                    end
                end
            end)
            if State.Knife or State.FastFist then
                applyNoCDKnife()
                for _ = 1, 35 do
                    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                end
                remoteFastAttack(tool)
            end
        elseif State.FastFist or State.Knife then
            applyItemLibraryGuns()
            applyNoCD(true)
            if tool then
                local n = State.FastFist and 12 or 4
                for _ = 1, n do
                    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                end

                remoteFastAttack(tool)
            else
                remoteFastAttack(nil)
            end
        end
    end

    if State.Rage then
        pcall(triggerRage)
    end

    doFly(hrp, humanoid)

    if State.TP and not State.VoidSpam then

        desyncActive = false
        desyncVisualPos = nil
        pcall(stopLionDesync)
        pcall(function()
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam and humanoid then
                cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                cam.CameraSubject = humanoid
                cam.FieldOfView = cam.FieldOfView
            end
            if hrp then
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end
            local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
            if targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local tHrp = targetChar.HumanoidRootPart
                local cf = calculateTPCFrame(tHrp, State.TPMode)
                for _ = 1, 28 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                local tbp = hrp:FindFirstChild("ChanTP_BP")
                if tbp then tbp.Position = cf.Position end
            end
        end)
    end

    if State.UnderAttack then
        pcall(function()
            local now = tick()

            if not underAttackSpawn then
                local p = hrp.Position
                if math.abs(p.Y) < 5000 and math.abs(p.X) < 50000 and math.abs(p.Z) < 50000 then
                    underAttackSpawn = Vector3.new(p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
                elseif desyncVisualPos and math.abs(desyncVisualPos.Y) < 5000 then
                    underAttackSpawn = desyncVisualPos
                else
                    underAttackSpawn = Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
                end
            end
            local holdT = tonumber(State.UnderAttackHold) or 0.35
            local gapT = tonumber(State.UnderAttackGap) or 0.15
            if holdT < 0.01 then holdT = 0.01 end
            if gapT < 0.01 then gapT = 0.01 end
            if underAttackUntil <= 0 then
                underAttackPhase = "hold"
                underAttackUntil = now + holdT
            end
            if now >= underAttackUntil then
                if underAttackPhase == "hold" then
                    underAttackPhase = "gap"
                    underAttackUntil = now + gapT
                else
                    underAttackPhase = "hold"
                    underAttackUntil = now + holdT
                end
            end
            if underAttackPhase == "hold" then
                local off = 4.5 + (math.sin(now * 13) * 0.5 + 0.5) * 0.5
                local dest = underAttackSpawn - Vector3.new(0, off, 0)
                local cf = CFrame.new(dest)
                for _ = 1, 16 do
                    hrp.CFrame = cf
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                hrp.Anchored = false
            end

        end)
    end

    if State.Bloom then
        pcall(applyBloom)
    end
    if State.Snow then
        pcall(function()
            if not snowFolder or not snowFolder.Parent then
                ensureSnow()
            end
        end)
    end
    if State.Flower then
        pcall(function()
            if not flowerFolder or not flowerFolder.Parent then
                ensureFlower()
            end
        end)
    end
    pcall(function()
        if type(applyFogSettings) == "function" then applyFogSettings() end
    end)
    applyFeatherSettings()
    updateFogFeather(0.016)
    updateChanAura(0.016)
    if State.TP or State.FakePos then
        desyncActive = false
    end
    applyDesyncCamera(hrp)
    updateBaiting(hrp, humanoid, char)
    updateUESyncKill(hrp, humanoid, char)
    if State.PerfectBlock then
        pcall(function() applyPerfectBlock(false) end)
    end
    if State.MagicBullet then pcall(applyMagicBullet) end
    if State.MagicMelee then pcall(applyMagicMelee) end

    if State.FakePos and not State.TP then
        pcall(function()
            local pos = hrp.Position

            if math.abs(pos.Y) < 500000 and not (pos.Y > 1003900 and pos.Y < 1004200) then
                if not fakePosReal then
                    fakePosReal = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z)
                else
                    fakePosReal = fakePosReal:Lerp(Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z), 0.5)
                end
            elseif not fakePosReal then
                fakePosReal = Vector3.new(pos.X, 50, pos.Z)
            end
            fakePosActive = true

            local fakeY = 1004046
            local fx = fakePosReal.X
            local fz = fakePosReal.Z
            local cf = CFrame.new(fx, fakeY, fz)
            pcall(function()
                if sethiddenproperty then
                    sethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                end
                if hrp.SetNetworkOwner then hrp:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end
            end)
            for _ = 1, 20 do
                hrp.CFrame = cf
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end)
    else
        fakePosActive = false
        if fakePosReal then
            pcall(function()
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(fakePosReal)
            end)
            fakePosReal = nil
        end
    end

    if State.Baiting and humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
        end)
    end
end)

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if State.TP then

        pcall(function()
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if cam and hum then
                if cam.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then
                    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                end
                if cam.CameraSubject ~= hum then
                    cam.CameraSubject = hum
                end
            end
        end)
        return
    end
    if not State.FakePos or not fakePosActive or not fakePosReal then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    pcall(function()
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local dest = fakePosReal

        if hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            dest = dest + hum.MoveDirection * 0.4
            fakePosReal = dest
        end
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest.X, dest.Y, dest.Z)
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.3)
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastHeavy >= 0.25 then
            lastHeavy = now
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char and char.Parent then
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hrp and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and State.Anti8000 and not State.Rage then
                    local targetChar = getClosestPlayer()
                    if targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local dist = (hrp.Position - targetChar.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist >= 8000 and dist <= 8999 then
                            pcall(function()
                                hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(math.random(-1500, 1500), 0, math.random(-1500, 1500))
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

    end
end)

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if State and State.Bloom then
        pcall(applyBloom)
    end
end)

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not State or not State.TP then return end
    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not cam or not hum then return end
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        cam.CameraSubject = hum
        if root then
            for _, n in ipairs({"ChanTP_BP", "ChanTP_BG"}) do
                local o = root:FindFirstChild(n)
                if o then o:Destroy() end
            end
        end

        desyncActive = false
        desyncVisualPos = nil
    end)
end)

local DEFAULT_SKYBOXES = {
    { name = "Night Space", id = "159454299" },
    { name = "Nebula Purple", id = "150934429" },
    { name = "Cloudy Day", id = "591058823" },
    { name = "Sunset Orange", id = "150939022" },
    { name = "Deep Space", id = "149397684" },
    { name = "Pink Galaxy", id = "151165214" },
    { name = "Blue Horizon", id = "149397558" },
    { name = "Storm Clouds", id = "123333960" },
    { name = "Aurora", id = "12064107" },
    { name = "Void Black", id = "162157285" },
    { name = "Morning Sky", id = "151165384" },
    { name = "Red Dusk", id = "150939098" },
    { name = "Ice World", id = "155061495" },
    { name = "Green Mist", id = "151165284" },
    { name = "White Heaven", id = "151165414" },
}
local skyboxOrig = nil
local function FS_GetSkyId(name)
    for _, sky in ipairs(DEFAULT_SKYBOXES) do
        if sky.name == name then return sky.id end
    end
    local id = nil
    pcall(function()
        if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
        local path = "chanhyuk lua/skyboxes/" .. tostring(name) .. ".txt"
        if isfile and isfile(path) then
            id = tostring(readfile(path)):match("%d+")
        end
    end)
    return id
end
function ApplySkybox()
    pcall(function()
        if not State.SkyboxEnabled then
            local old = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukSky")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            if skyboxOrig then
                for k, v in pairs(skyboxOrig) do
                    pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end)
                end
            end
            return
        end
        local id = tostring(State.SkyboxId or ""):match("%d+")
        if (not id or id == "") and State.SkyboxPreset then
            id = FS_GetSkyId(State.SkyboxPreset)
        end
        if not id or id == "" then return end
        if not skyboxOrig then
            skyboxOrig = {
                Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
                OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
                Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
            }
        end
        local old = Lighting:FindFirstChild("ChanhyukSky")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
        sky.Name = "ChanhyukSky"
        local asset = "rbxassetid://" .. id
        sky.SkyboxBk = asset
        sky.SkyboxDn = asset
        sky.SkyboxFt = asset
        sky.SkyboxLf = asset
        sky.SkyboxRt = asset
        sky.SkyboxUp = asset
        sky.Parent = Lighting
    end)
end

local cachedWeaponList = {"Knife"}
local cachedSkinList = {"Default"}
local cachedWrapList = {"None"}
local cachedFinisherList = {"None"}
local cachedCharmList = {"None"}
local skinFolderIndex = {}
local weaponFolderIndex = {}
local wrapTextureIndex = {}
local wrapMatIndex = {}
local skinCaseFolders = {}

local function uniqueSorted(list)
    local seen, out = {}, {}
    for _, v in ipairs(list) do
        local s = tostring(v)
        if s ~= "" and not seen[s] then
            seen[s] = true
            table.insert(out, s)
        end
    end
    table.sort(out)
    return out
end

local function getAssets()
    local ps = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 3)
    if not ps then return nil end
    return ps:FindFirstChild("Assets") or ps:FindFirstChild("assets")
end

local function getViewModelsRoot()
    local assets = getAssets()
    if not assets then return nil end
    return assets:FindFirstChild("ViewModels") or assets:FindFirstChild("Viewmodels")
end

local function refreshSkinLists()
    local weapons, skins, wraps, finishers, charms = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
    skinFolderIndex, weaponFolderIndex = {}, {}
    wrapTextureIndex, wrapMatIndex = {}, {}
    skinCaseFolders = {}

    local vm = getViewModelsRoot()
    if vm then
        local wFolder = vm:FindFirstChild("Weapons")
        if wFolder then
            for _, ch in ipairs(wFolder:GetChildren()) do
                table.insert(weapons, ch.Name)
                weaponFolderIndex[ch.Name] = ch
            end
        end

        for _, folder in ipairs(vm:GetChildren()) do
            if folder.Name ~= "Weapons" then
                table.insert(skinCaseFolders, folder)
                for _, skin in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    local label = skin.Name
                    table.insert(skins, label)
                    skinFolderIndex[label] = skin
                    local key = folder.Name .. " | " .. skin.Name
                    table.insert(skins, key)
                    skinFolderIndex[key] = skin
                end
            end
        end
        for _, name in ipairs({"Finishers", "Finisher", "Charms", "Charm", "Decorations"}) do
            local f = vm:FindFirstChild(name) or (vm.Parent and vm.Parent:FindFirstChild(name))
            if f then
                for _, ch in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
                    if name:lower():find("finish") then
                        table.insert(finishers, ch.Name)
                    else
                        table.insert(charms, ch.Name)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        local assets = getAssets()
        local wt = assets and (assets:FindFirstChild("WrapTextures") or assets:FindFirstChild("Wraps"))
        if wt then
            for _, ch in ipairs(wt:GetChildren()) do
                table.insert(wraps, ch.Name)
                wrapTextureIndex[ch.Name] = ch
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local ms = game:GetService("MaterialService")
        local w = ms:FindFirstChild("Wraps")
        if w then
            for _, ch in ipairs(w:GetChildren()) do
                table.insert(wraps, ch.Name)
                wrapMatIndex[ch.Name] = ch
            end
        end
    end)

    if #weapons == 0 then
        weapons = {"Knife", "Fists", "Assault Rifle", "Handgun", "SMG", "Shotgun", "Sniper", "Bow", "Slingshot"}
    end
    if #skins == 0 then
        skins = {"Default", "Karambit", "Gold", "Diamond", "Neon"}
    end
    if #wraps == 0 then
        wraps = {"None", "Chrome", "Gold", "Diamond", "Lava", "Camo"}
    end
    if #finishers == 0 then
        finishers = {"None", "Ragdoll", "Flop", "Confetti", "Freeze", "Balloons", "OOF"}
    end
    if #charms == 0 then
        charms = {"None", "Default"}
    end

    table.insert(wraps, 1, "None")
    table.insert(finishers, 1, "None")
    table.insert(charms, 1, "None")

    cachedWeaponList = uniqueSorted(weapons)
    cachedSkinList = uniqueSorted(skins)
    cachedWrapList = uniqueSorted(wraps)
    cachedFinisherList = uniqueSorted(finishers)
    cachedCharmList = uniqueSorted(charms)

    print(string.format("[chanhyuk] skin scan W=%d S=%d Wr=%d F=%d C=%d",
        #cachedWeaponList, #cachedSkinList, #cachedWrapList, #cachedFinisherList, #cachedCharmList))
    return cachedWeaponList
end

local function resolveWeaponFolder(weaponName)
    if weaponFolderIndex[weaponName] then return weaponFolderIndex[weaponName] end
    local vm = getViewModelsRoot()
    local wf = vm and vm:FindFirstChild("Weapons")
    local w = wf and wf:FindFirstChild(weaponName)
    if w then weaponFolderIndex[weaponName] = w end
    return w
end

local function resolveSkinFolder(skinName)
    if skinFolderIndex[skinName] then return skinFolderIndex[skinName] end

    local bare = tostring(skinName):match("([^|]+)$")
    if bare then bare = bare:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") end
    if bare and skinFolderIndex[bare] then return skinFolderIndex[bare] end
    local vm = getViewModelsRoot()
    if not vm then return nil end
    for _, folder in ipairs(vm:GetChildren()) do
        if folder.Name ~= "Weapons" then
            local s = folder:FindFirstChild(skinName) or (bare and folder:FindFirstChild(bare))
            if s then
                skinFolderIndex[skinName] = s
                return s
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function applyViewmodelSkin(weaponName, skinName)
    local wInst = resolveWeaponFolder(weaponName)
    local sInst = resolveSkinFolder(skinName)
    if not wInst then
        return false, "weapon viewmodel missing: " .. tostring(weaponName)
    end
    if not sInst then
        return false, "skin missing: " .. tostring(skinName)
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        wInst:ClearAllChildren()
        for _, ch in ipairs(sInst:GetChildren()) do
            ch:Clone().Parent = wInst
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local root = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
        local fp = root and root:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
        if not fp then return end
        for _, vm in ipairs(fp:GetChildren()) do
            if type(vm.Name) == "string" and vm.Name:find(LocalPlayer.Name, 1, true) then
                if vm.Name:find(weaponName, 1, true) or true then
                    for _, part in ipairs(sInst:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA("MeshPart") or part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local target = vm:FindFirstChild(part.Name, true)
                            if target and target:IsA("BasePart") then
                                pcall(function()
                                    if part:IsA("MeshPart") and target:IsA("MeshPart") then
                                        target.MeshId = part.MeshId
                                        if target.TextureID ~= nil then target.TextureID = part.TextureID end
                                    end
                                    target.Color = part.Color
                                    target.Material = part.Material
                                    target.Transparency = part.Transparency
                                    if part.MaterialVariant then target.MaterialVariant = part.MaterialVariant end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    return ok, ok and "ok" or "clone fail"
end

local function applyWrapToWeapon(weaponName, wrapName)
    if not wrapName or wrapName == "None" or wrapName == "" then return false end
    local wInst = resolveWeaponFolder(weaponName)
    if not wInst then return false end
    local matVar = wrapMatIndex[wrapName]
    local texFolder = wrapTextureIndex[wrapName]
    for _, d in ipairs(wInst:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Transparency ~= 1 then
            if matVar then
                pcall(function()
                    d.Material = Enum.Material.Fabric
                    d.MaterialVariant = matVar.Name
                end)
                for _, t in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do
                    if t:IsA("Texture") or t:IsA("Decal") or t:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then t:Destroy() end
                end
            elseif texFolder then
                for _, t in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do
                    if t:IsA("Texture") or t:IsA("Decal") or t:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then t:Destroy() end
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(texFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Decal") or child:IsA("Texture") or child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
                        child:Clone().Parent = d
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return true
end

local function applyRivalsSkin()
    local wep = tostring(State.SkinWeapon or "")
    local skin = tostring(State.SkinName or "")
    local wrap = tostring(State.SkinWrap or "")
    local fin = tostring(State.SkinFinisher or "")
    local charm = tostring(State.SkinCharm or "")

    refreshSkinLists()

    local ok, msg = false, "nothing"
    if wep == "" then

        pcall(function()
            local tool = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if tool then
                wep = tool.Name
                State.SkinWeapon = wep
            end
        end)
    end
    if skin ~= "" and skin ~= "Default" and skin ~= "(pick weapon)" then
        ok, msg = applyViewmodelSkin(wep, skin)
    end
    if wrap ~= "" and wrap ~= "None" then
        if applyWrapToWeapon(wep, wrap) then ok = true end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local tool = char and (char:FindFirstChild(wep) or char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool"))
        if tool then
            if fin ~= "" and fin ~= "None" then tool:SetAttribute("Finisher", fin) end
            if charm ~= "" and charm ~= "None" then tool:SetAttribute("Charm", charm) end
            if skin ~= "" then tool:SetAttribute("Skin", skin) end
            if wrap ~= "" then tool:SetAttribute("Wrap", wrap) end
        end
    end)
    print("[chanhyuk] skin apply", wep, skin, wrap, fin, charm, ok, msg)
    return ok, msg
end

local UISystem = {}
UISystem.Elements = {}
UISystem.Library = nil
UISystem.Window = nil
UISystem.Built = false

local function bindNum(flag)
    return function(v)
        local n = tonumber(v)
        if n ~= nil then State[flag] = n end
    end
end

local function bindToggle(flag, onEnable, onDisable)
    return function(v)
        State[flag] = v and true or false
        if State[flag] then
            if onEnable then pcall(onEnable) end
        else
            if onDisable then pcall(onDisable) end
        end
    end
end

function UISystem.Destroy()
    pcall(function()
        if UISystem.Library and UISystem.Library.Unload then
            UISystem.Library:Unload()
        end
    end)
    UISystem.Built = false
    UISystem.Elements = {}
end

function UISystem.Exists()
    return UISystem.Built == true
end

function UISystem.UpdateVisuals()
end

function UISystem.ClearDragConnections()
end

local function updateCoordLabel()
end

function UISystem.Create()
    if UISystem.Built then return true end
    if _G.__chanhyuk_obsidian_built then
        UISystem.Built = true
        return true
    end
    _G.__chanhyuk_obsidian_building = true

    local okLib, Library = pcall(function()
        local repo = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/main/"
        return loadstring(game:HttpGet(repo .. "Library.lua"))()
    end)
    if not okLib or not Library then
        warn("[chanhyuk] Obsidian Library load failed:", Library)
        return false
    end

    local ThemeManager, SaveManager
    pcall(function()
        local repo = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/main/"
