        ThemeManager = loadstring(game:HttpGet(repo .. "addons/ThemeManager.lua"))()
        SaveManager = loadstring(game:HttpGet(repo .. "addons/SaveManager.lua"))()
    end)

    UISystem.Library = Library
    local Window = Library:CreateWindow({
        Title = "chanhyuk rivals",
        Footer = "RightControl = toggle | Skins tab",
        Center = true,
        AutoShow = true,
        ToggleKeybind = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
        NotifySide = "Right",
        ShowCustomCursor = false,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(560, 460),
    })
    UISystem.Window = Window
    pcall(function() Library.ShowCustomCursor = false end)

    local Tabs = {
        Combat = Window:AddTab("전투", "sword"),
        Movement = Window:AddTab("이동", "user"),
        Visuals = Window:AddTab("비주얼", "eye"),
        Skins = Window:AddTab("스킨", "sparkles"),
        Misc = Window:AddTab("기타", "box"),
        Settings = Window:AddTab("설정", "cog"),
    }

    local C1 = Tabs.Combat:AddLeftGroupbox("코어")
    C1:AddToggle("Rage", { Text = "레이지", Default = State.Rage, Callback = bindToggle("Rage", function() startRage() end, function() stopRage() end) })
    C1:AddToggle("Ragebot", { Text = "레이지봇", Default = State.Ragebot, Callback = function(v)
        State.Ragebot = v and true or false
        if State.Ragebot then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(rbEquipSlot)
            end)
        end
    end })
    C1:AddDropdown("RagebotSlot", { Text = "무기 슬롯", Values = {"Primary", "Secondary", "Melee"}, Default = 3, Callback = function(v)
        State.RagebotSlot = v
        pcall(rbEquipSlot)
    end })
    C1:AddInput("RagebotFireRate", { Text = "연사 속도", Default = tostring(State.RagebotFireRate or 0.0005), Callback = function(v) State.RagebotFireRate = tonumber(v) or 0.0005 end })
    C1:AddInput("RagebotJitter", { Text = "지터 (똥꼬×1.5)", Default = tostring(State.RagebotJitter or 2.25), Callback = function(v) State.RagebotJitter = tonumber(v) or 2.25 end })
    C1:AddInput("RagebotRange", { Text = "레이지봇 범위", Default = tostring(State.RagebotRange or 500), Callback = function(v) State.RagebotRange = tonumber(v) or 500 end })

    C1:AddToggle("TP", { Text = "타겟 TP", Default = State.TP, Callback = bindToggle("TP") })
    C1:AddDropdown("TPMode", { Text = "TP 모드", Values = {"Overlap","Above","Below","Front","Back"}, Default = 1, Callback = function(v) State.TPMode = v end })
    C1:AddToggle("FastFist", { Text = "패스트 피스트/나이프", Default = State.FastFist, Callback = function(v) State.FastFist = v; State.Knife = v; if not v then pcall(restoreWeaponCooldowns) end end })
    C1:AddToggle("Aimbot", { Text = "에임봇", Default = State.Aimbot, Callback = bindToggle("Aimbot") })

    local S1 = Tabs.Skins:AddLeftGroupbox("아바타")
    S1:AddInput("AvatarID", { Text = "User ID", Default = tostring(State.AvatarID or ""), Callback = function(v) State.AvatarID = tostring(v or "") end })
    S1:AddButton({ Text = "Apply Avatar From User ID", Func = function()
        local id = tonumber(tostring(State.AvatarID or ""):gsub("%s+", ""))
        if not id then pcall(function() Library:Notify("Invalid User ID", 3) end) return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                local desc = Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(id)
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                if hum.ApplyDescriptionReset then hum:ApplyDescriptionReset(desc) else hum:ApplyDescription(desc) end
            end)
            pcall(function() Library:Notify(ok and "Avatar applied" or ("Fail: "..tostring(err)), 3) end)
        end)
    end })
    S1:AddButton({ Text = "Full Rescan Cosmetics", Func = function()
        local w = refreshSkinLists()
        pcall(function()
            Library:Notify(string.format("W:%d S:%d Wr:%d F:%d C:%d — reopen Skins tab if needed", #cachedWeaponList, #cachedSkinList, #cachedWrapList, #cachedFinisherList, #cachedCharmList), 6)
        end)
        for i, n in ipairs(cachedWeaponList) do if i <= 30 then print("[weapon]", n) end end
        for i, n in ipairs(cachedSkinList) do if i <= 30 then print("[skin]", n) end end
        for i, n in ipairs(cachedWrapList) do if i <= 30 then print("[wrap]", n) end end
    end })

    ST2:AddInput("KeyUnderground", { Text = "바인드 언더그라운드", Default = tostring(State.KeyUnderground or "U"), Callback = function(v) State.KeyUnderground = tostring(v or "U") end })
    ST2:AddInput("KeyVoid", { Text = "바인드 보이드스팸", Default = tostring(State.KeyVoid or "V"), Callback = function(v) State.KeyVoid = tostring(v or "V") end })
    ST2:AddButton({ Text = "무기 스캔", Func = function() scanned = false; pcall(scanWeapons) end })

    if ThemeManager and SaveManager then
        pcall(function()
            ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Library)
            SaveManager:SetLibrary(Library)
            SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
            ThemeManager:SetFolder("chanhyuk rivals")
            SaveManager:SetFolder("chanhyuk rivals/configs")
            SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(Tabs.Settings)
            ThemeManager:ApplyToTab(Tabs.Settings)
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        Library:OnUnload(function() UISystem.Built = false end)
    end)

    UISystem.Built = true
    _G.__chanhyuk_obsidian_built = true
    _G.__chanhyuk_obsidian_building = false
    UISystem.Elements = { Library = Library, Window = Window }
    _G.__chanhyuk_rivals_unload = function()
        pcall(function()
            if UISystem.Library and UISystem.Library.Unload then
                UISystem.Library:Unload()
            end
        end)
        UISystem.Built = false
        _G.__chanhyuk_obsidian_built = false
        _G.__chanhyuk_rivals_active = false
    end
    print("[chanhyuk] Obsidian UI ready | single instance")
    return true
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    updateCoordLabel()
end)

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    antiAimOriginal = {}
    originalPartSizes = {}
    lastAntiKillHealth = nil
    baitCenter = nil
    baitCF = nil
    task.delay(0.5, function()
        if State.Aura then buildChanAura() end
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    if _G.__chanhyuk_obsidian_built then return end
    if type(UISystem) ~= "table" or type(UISystem.Create) ~= "function" then
        warn("[chanhyuk] UISystem missing")
        return
    end
    local ok, err = pcall(UISystem.Create)
    if not ok then
        warn("[chanhyuk] UI error:", tostring(err))
        task.wait(1)
        if not _G.__chanhyuk_obsidian_built then
            pcall(UISystem.Create)
        end
    end
end)

local function keyMatches(bind, keyName)
    if not bind or bind == "" then return false end
    bind = tostring(bind):gsub("%s+", "")
    keyName = tostring(keyName)
    if bind:lower() == keyName:lower() then return true end

    if #bind == 1 and keyName:lower() == bind:lower() then return true end
    if bind:lower() == ("Key" .. keyName):lower() then return true end
    return false
end
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if not State.KeybindsEnabled then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
    local k = input.KeyCode.Name
    local function toggle(flag)
        State[flag] = not State[flag]
        if flag == "Underground" then
            if State.Underground then
                State.UndergroundDesync = true
                local h = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then pcall(applyLionUnderground, h) end
            else
                pcall(stopLionDesync)
            end
        elseif flag == "Rage" then
            if State.Rage then pcall(startRage) else pcall(stopRage) end
        end
        pcall(function() if UISystem and UISystem.UpdateVisuals then UISystem.UpdateVisuals() end end)
        print("[chanhyuk]", flag, State[flag] and "ON" or "OFF")
    end
    if keyMatches(State.KeyAimbot, k) then
        toggle("Aimbot")
    elseif keyMatches(State.KeyTP, k) then
        toggle("TP")
    elseif keyMatches(State.KeyFly, k) then
        toggle("Fly")
    elseif keyMatches(State.KeyDongko, k) then
        toggle("Dongko")
    elseif keyMatches(State.KeyRage, k) then
        State.Rage = not State.Rage
        if State.Rage then pcall(startRage) else pcall(stopRage) end
        pcall(function() if UISystem and UISystem.UpdateVisuals then UISystem.UpdateVisuals() end end)
        print("[chanhyuk] Rage", State.Rage and "ON" or "OFF")
    elseif keyMatches(State.KeyUnderground, k) then
        toggle("Underground")
    elseif keyMatches(State.KeyVoid, k) then
        toggle("VoidSpam")
    end
end)

print("chanhyuk lua loaded")
